│     QString posKeyR(double x, double y) {
│         return QString("%1,%2").arg(int(std::round(x))).arg(int(std::round(y)));
│     }
│     
│     void collectPortWorldPositions(const QVariantMap& c, QSet<QString>& out) {
│         double cx = c.value("x").toDouble();
│         double cy = c.value("y").toDouble();
│         QVariantList outs = ComponentLogic::outputPorts(c);
│         for (const auto& pv : outs) {
│             QVariantMap p = pv.toMap();
│             out.insert(posKeyR(cx + p.value("x").toDouble(),
│                                cy + p.value("y").toDouble()));
│         }
│         QVariantList ins = ComponentLogic::inputPorts(c);
│         for (const auto& pv : ins) {
│             QVariantMap p = pv.toMap();
│             out.insert(posKeyR(cx + p.value("x").toDouble(),
│                                cy + p.value("y").toDouble()));
│         }
│     }
│     
│     bool wireTouchesPositions(const QVariantMap& w, const QSet<QString>& positions) {
│         QString wt = w.value("wireType").toString();
│         if (wt != "ortho") return false;
│         QVariantList path = w.value("path").toList();
│         if (path.isEmpty()) return false;
│     
│         QList<QPointF> portPts;
│         portPts.reserve(positions.size());
│         for (const auto& key : positions) {
│             QStringList parts = key.split(',');
│             if (parts.size() != 2) continue;
│             portPts.append(QPointF(parts[0].toDouble(), parts[1].toDouble()));
│         }
│         if (portPts.isEmpty()) return false;
│     
│         for (const auto& pv : path) {
│             QVariantMap p = pv.toMap();
│             double px = p.value("x").toDouble();
│             double py = p.value("y").toDouble();
│             for (const auto& pp : portPts) {
│                 if (qAbs(px - pp.x()) < 15.0 && qAbs(py - pp.y()) < 15.0) {
│                     return true;
│                 }
│             }
│         }
│         return false;
│     }
│     
│     } // namespace
│     
│     QString Circuit::addComponent(const QString& type, double x, double y,
│                                   int bitWidth, int inputCount, const QString& subId) {
│         if (m_viewOnly) return QString();
│         if (bitWidth < 1) bitWidth = 1;
│         if (bitWidth > 64) bitWidth = 64;
│         if (inputCount < 2) inputCount = 2;
│         if (inputCount > 64) inputCount = 64;
│         if (type == "sub" && !canAddSubInstance(subId)) return QString();
│     
│         pushUndo();
│         QVariantMap c;
│         Context& ctx = currentCtx();
│         c["id"] = IdAllocator::nextComponentId(ctx);
│         c["type"] = type;
│         c["x"] = x; c["y"] = y;
│         c["bitWidth"] = bitWidth;
│         c["inputCount"] = inputCount;
│         c["displayBase"] = "bin";
│         c["name"] = "";
│         QVariantList bits;
│         for (int i = 0; i < bitWidth; ++i) bits.append(false);
│         c["inputBits"] = bits;
│     
│         QVariantList outPorts, inPorts;
│         if (type == "text") c["content"] = "文本";
│         else if (type == "clock") c["name"] = "CLK";
│         else if (type == "const") c["name"] = "CONST";
│         else if (type == "sub") {
│             c["subId"] = subId;
│             c["subInputNames"] = QVariantList();
│             c["subOutputNames"] = QVariantList();
│             c["subInputWidths"] = QVariantList();
│             c["subOutputWidths"] = QVariantList();
│         } else if (type == "splitter") {
│             QVariantList splits;
│             for (int i = 0; i < bitWidth; ++i) splits.append(1);
│             c["outputSplits"] = splits;
│             inPorts.append(QString(bitWidth, '0'));
│             for (int i = 0; i < splits.size(); ++i) {
│                 int w = splits[i].toInt(); if (w < 1) w = 1;
│                 outPorts.append(QString(w, '0'));
│             }
│         } else if (type == "hub") {
│             QVariantList splits;
│             for (int i = 0; i < bitWidth; ++i) splits.append(1);
│             c["outputSplits"] = splits;
│             for (int i = 0; i < splits.size(); ++i) {
│                 int w = splits[i].toInt(); if (w < 1) w = 1;
│                 inPorts.append(QString(w, '0'));
│             }
│             outPorts.append(QString(bitWidth, '0'));
│         } else if (type == "tgate") {
│             c["inputCount"] = 3;
│             outPorts.append(QString(bitWidth, '0'));
│             inPorts.append(QString(bitWidth, '0'));
│             inPorts.append(QString(bitWidth, '0'));
│             inPorts.append(QString(bitWidth, '0'));
│         } else if (type == "ntran" || type == "ptran") {
│             c["inputCount"] = 2;
│             outPorts.append(QString(bitWidth, '0'));
│             inPorts.append(QString(bitWidth, '0'));
│             inPorts.append(QString(bitWidth, '0'));
│         } else {
│             int n = 0;
│             if (type == "input" || type == "const" || type == "clock" || type == "not" ||
│                 type == "and" || type == "or" || type == "nand" ||
│                 type == "nor" || type == "xor" || type == "xnor") n = 1;
│             for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) outPorts.append(QString(bitWidth, '0'));
│             int ic = 0;
│             if (type == "not" || type == "led" || type == "output") ic = 1;
│             else if (type == "and" || type == "or" || type == "nand" ||
│                      type == "nor" || type == "xor" || type == "xnor") ic = inputCount;
│             for (int i = 0; i < ic; ++i) inPorts.append(QString(bitWidth, '0'));
│         }
│         c["outputPorts"] = outPorts;
│         c["inputPortValues"] = inPorts;
│     
│         ctx.components.append(c);
│         m_ctxSubInstCache.clear();
│         if (type == "clock") m_clockStates[c["id"].toString()] = false;
│     
│         evaluateAll();
│         emit changed();
│         emit structureChanged();
│         return c["id"].toString();
│     }
│     
│     void Circuit::removeComponent(const QString& id) {
│         if (m_viewOnly) return;
│         int idx = indexOfComponent(id);
│         if (idx < 0) return;
│         pushUndo();
│         Context& ctx = currentCtx();
│         QVariantMap c = ctx.components[idx].toMap();
│     
│         QSet<QString> portPositions;
│         collectPortWorldPositions(c, portPositions);
│     
│         if (c.value("type").toString() == "clock") m_clockStates.remove(id);
│         ctx.components.removeAt(idx);
│         m_ctxSubInstCache.clear();
│     
│         for (int i = ctx.wires.size() - 1; i >= 0; --i) {
│             auto w = ctx.wires[i].toMap();
│             bool shouldRemove = false;
│             if (w.value("fromComp").toString() == id ||
│                 w.value("toComp").toString() == id) {
│                 shouldRemove = true;
│             } else if (wireTouchesPositions(w, portPositions)) {
│                 shouldRemove = true;
│             }
│             if (shouldRemove) ctx.wires.removeAt(i);
│         }
│     
│         evaluateAll();
│         emit changed();
│         emit structureChanged();
│     }
│     
│     void Circuit::removeComponents(const QStringList& ids) {
│         if (m_viewOnly) return;
│         if (ids.isEmpty()) return;
│         QSet<QString> idSet;
│         for (const auto& id : ids) idSet.insert(id);
│         bool any = false;
│         for (const auto& cv : currentCtx().components) {
│             if (idSet.contains(cv.toMap().value("id").toString())) { any = true; break; }
│         }
│         if (!any) return;
│         pushUndo();
│         Context& ctx = currentCtx();
│     
│         QSet<QString> portPositions;
│         for (int i = ctx.components.size() - 1; i >= 0; --i) {
│             QVariantMap c = ctx.components[i].toMap();
│             if (!idSet.contains(c.value("id").toString())) continue;
│             collectPortWorldPositions(c, portPositions);
│             if (c.value("type").toString() == "clock")
│                 m_clockStates.remove(c.value("id").toString());
│             ctx.components.removeAt(i);
│         }
│     
│         m_ctxSubInstCache.clear();
│     
│         for (int i = ctx.wires.size() - 1; i >= 0; --i) {
│             auto w = ctx.wires[i].toMap();
│             bool shouldRemove = false;
│             if (idSet.contains(w.value("fromComp").toString()) ||
│                 idSet.contains(w.value("toComp").toString())) {
│                 shouldRemove = true;
│             } else if (wireTouchesPositions(w, portPositions)) {
│                 shouldRemove = true;
│             }
│             if (shouldRemove) ctx.wires.removeAt(i);
│         }
│     
│         evaluateAll();
│         emit changed();
│         emit structureChanged();
│     }
│     
│     void Circuit::updateWiresForMovedComponent(Context& ctx, const QString& movedId) {
│         for (int i = 0; i < ctx.wires.size(); ++i) {
│             QVariantMap w = ctx.wires[i].toMap();
│             if (w.value("wireType").toString() != "ortho") continue;
│             QString fromComp = w.value("fromComp").toString();
│             QString toComp = w.value("toComp").toString();
│             bool fromIsMoved = (fromComp == movedId);
│             bool toIsMoved = (toComp == movedId);
│             if (!fromIsMoved && !toIsMoved) continue;
│     
│             QVariantList path = w.value("path").toList();
│             if (path.size() < 2) continue;
│     
│             auto getNewPortPos = [&](const QString& compId, int port, bool isOut) -> QPointF {
│                 int ci = ctx.indexOfComponent(compId);
│                 if (ci < 0) return QPointF();
│                 QVariantMap c = ctx.components[ci].toMap();
│                 QVariantList ports = isOut ? ComponentLogic::outputPorts(c) : ComponentLogic::inputPorts(c);
│                 if (port < 0 || port >= ports.size()) return QPointF();
│                 QVariantMap p = ports[port].toMap();
│                 return QPointF(c.value("x").toDouble() + p.value("x").toDouble(),
│                                c.value("y").toDouble() + p.value("y").toDouble());
│             };
│     
│             if (fromIsMoved) {
│                 int fp = w.value("fromPort").toInt();
│                 QPointF np = getNewPortPos(movedId, fp, true);
│                 QVariantMap oldP0 = path[0].toMap();
│                 double oldX = oldP0.value("x").toDouble();
│                 QVariantMap newP0;
│                 newP0["x"] = np.x(); newP0["y"] = np.y();
│                 path[0] = newP0;
│     
│                 if (path.size() >= 2) {
│                     QVariantMap p1 = path[1].toMap();
│                     double p1x = p1.value("x").toDouble();
│                     bool wasVertical = (qAbs(p1x - oldX) < 0.5);
│                     if (wasVertical) p1["x"] = np.x();
│                     else p1["y"] = np.y();
│                     path[1] = p1;
│                 }
│             }
│     
│             if (toIsMoved) {
│                 int tp = w.value("toPort").toInt();
│                 QPointF np = getNewPortPos(movedId, tp, false);
│                 int n = path.size();
│                 QVariantMap oldPn = path[n-1].toMap();
│                 double oldX = oldPn.value("x").toDouble();
│                 QVariantMap newPn;
│                 newPn["x"] = np.x(); newPn["y"] = np.y();
│                 path[n-1] = newPn;
│     
│                 if (n >= 2) {
│                     QVariantMap pn1 = path[n-2].toMap();
│                     double pn1x = pn1.value("x").toDouble();
│                     bool wasVertical = (qAbs(pn1x - oldX) < 0.5);
│                     if (wasVertical) pn1["x"] = np.x();
│                     else pn1["y"] = np.y();
│                     path[n-2] = pn1;
│                 }
│             }
│     
│             w["path"] = path;
│             ctx.wires[i] = w;
│         }
│     }
│     
│     void Circuit::moveComponent(const QString& id, double x, double y) {
│         if (m_viewOnly) return;
│         int idx = indexOfComponent(id);
│         if (idx < 0) return;
│         Context& ctx = currentCtx();
│         QVariantMap c = ctx.components[idx].toMap();
│         double ox = c.value("x").toDouble(), oy = c.value("y").toDouble();
│         if (qAbs(ox - x) < 1e-9 && qAbs(oy - y) < 1e-9) return;
│         pushUndo("move:" + id);
│         c["x"] = x; c["y"] = y;
│         ctx.components[idx] = c;
│         updateWiresForMovedComponent(ctx, id);
│         evaluateAll();
│         emit geometryChanged();
│         emit changed();
│         emit structureChanged();
│     }
│     
│     void Circuit::moveSelection(const QStringList& ids, double dx, double dy) {
│         if (m_viewOnly) return;
│         if (ids.isEmpty()) return;
│         if (qAbs(dx) < 1e-9 && qAbs(dy) < 1e-9) return;
│         Context& ctx = currentCtx();
│         QSet<QString> idSet;
│         for (const auto& id : ids) idSet.insert(id);
│         bool any = false;
│         for (int i = 0; i < ctx.components.size(); ++i) {
│             if (idSet.contains(ctx.components[i].toMap().value("id").toString())) { any = true; break; }
│         }
│         if (!any) return;
│         pushUndo("moveSel");
│         for (int i = 0; i < ctx.components.size(); ++i) {
│             QVariantMap c = ctx.components[i].toMap();
│             if (idSet.contains(c.value("id").toString())) {
│                 c["x"] = c.value("x").toDouble() + dx;
│                 c["y"] = c.value("y").toDouble() + dy;
│                 ctx.components[i] = c;
│             }
│         }
│         for (const auto& id : ids) {
│             if (ctx.indexOfComponent(id) >= 0) {
│                 updateWiresForMovedComponent(ctx, id);
│             }
│         }
│         evaluateAll();
│         emit geometryChanged();
│         emit changed();
│         emit structureChanged();
│     }
│     
│     void Circuit::moveComponentWithWires(const QString& id, double x, double y) {
│         moveComponent(id, x, y);
│     }
│     
│     void Circuit::moveSelectionWithWires(const QStringList& ids, double dx, double dy) {
│         moveSelection(ids, dx, dy);
│     }

│     // ★ 修复：rebuild 之后必须先跑完初始 Recalc 事件，再 apply
│     void Circuit::toggleInput(const QString& id) {
│         if (m_viewOnly) return;
│         int idx = indexOfComponent(id);
│         if (idx < 0) return;
│         Context& ctx = currentCtx();
│         QVariantMap c = ctx.components[idx].toMap();
│         if (c.value("type").toString() != "input") return;
│         QVariantList bits = c.value("inputBits").toList();
│         int bw = c.value("bitWidth").toInt();
│         if (bw < 1) bw = 1;
│         while (bits.size() < bw) bits.append(false);
│         while (bits.size() > bw) bits.removeLast();
│         bool v = !bits[0].toBool();
│         for (int i = 0; i < bits.size(); ++i) bits[i] = v;
│         c["inputBits"] = bits;
│         ctx.components[idx] = c;
│     
│         if (m_engineType == "event" && m_eventEngine && m_simulationRunning) {
│             if (m_engineDirty) {
│                 rebuildEventEngine();
│                 quint64 wd0 = m_eventEngine->wireDelay();
│                 quint64 gd0 = m_eventEngine->globalGateDelay();
│                 runEventEngineFully((wd0 + gd0) * 64 + 200, 500000);
│                 applyEngineOutputsToContexts();
│             }
│             QString binStr;
│             binStr.reserve(bw);
│             for (int i = 0; i < bw; ++i) binStr += v ? '1' : '0';
│             QStringList qids = engineQidsFor(m_currentCtxId, id);
│             for (const auto& qid : qids)
│                 m_eventEngine->triggerInputString(qid, binStr);
│             quint64 wd = m_eventEngine->wireDelay();
│             quint64 gd = m_eventEngine->globalGateDelay();
│             runEventEngineFully((wd + gd) * 64 + 200, 500000);
│             applyEngineOutputsToContexts();
│             emit simulationTick();
│         } else if (m_engineType == "event") {
│             m_engineDirty = true;
│         } else {
│             evaluateAll();
│         }
│         emit changed();
│         emit structureChanged();
│     }
│     
│     void Circuit::setBitValue(const QString& id, int bit, bool value) {
│         if (m_viewOnly) return;
│         int idx = indexOfComponent(id);
│         if (idx < 0) return;
│         Context& ctx = currentCtx();
│         QVariantMap c = ctx.components[idx].toMap();
│         QString t = c.value("type").toString();
│         if (t != "input" && t != "const") return;
│         QVariantList bits = c.value("inputBits").toList();
│         int bw = c.value("bitWidth").toInt();
│         if (bw < 1) bw = 1;
│         while (bits.size() < bw) bits.append(false);
│         while (bits.size() > bw) bits.removeLast();
│         if (bit < 0 || bit >= bits.size()) return;
│         if (bits[bit].toBool() == value) return;
│         bits[bit] = value;
│         c["inputBits"] = bits;
│         ctx.components[idx] = c;
│     
│         if (m_engineType == "event" && m_eventEngine && m_simulationRunning) {
│             if (m_engineDirty) {
│                 rebuildEventEngine();
│                 quint64 wd0 = m_eventEngine->wireDelay();
│                 quint64 gd0 = m_eventEngine->globalGateDelay();
│                 runEventEngineFully((wd0 + gd0) * 64 + 200, 500000);
│                 applyEngineOutputsToContexts();
│             }
│             QStringList qids = engineQidsFor(m_currentCtxId, id);
│             for (const auto& qid : qids)
│                 m_eventEngine->triggerInputBit(qid, bit, value);
│             quint64 wd = m_eventEngine->wireDelay();
│             quint64 gd = m_eventEngine->globalGateDelay();
│             runEventEngineFully((wd + gd) * 64 + 200, 500000);
│             applyEngineOutputsToContexts();
│             emit simulationTick();
│         } else if (m_engineType == "event") {
│             m_engineDirty = true;
│         } else {
│             evaluateAll();
│         }
│         emit changed();
│         emit structureChanged();
│     }
│     
│     void Circuit::setComponentProp(const QString& id, const QString& key, const QVariant& value) {
│         if (m_viewOnly) return;
│         int idx = indexOfComponent(id);
│         if (idx < 0) return;
│         Context& ctx = currentCtx();
│         QVariantMap c = ctx.components[idx].toMap();
│         if (key == "bitWidth" || key == "inputCount" || key == "rotation") {
│             if (c.value(key).toInt() == value.toInt()) return;
│         } else {
│             if (c.value(key) == value) return;
│         }
│         pushUndo("prop:" + id + ":" + key);
│         bool structural = false;
│         if (key == "bitWidth") {
│             int bw = value.toInt();
│             if (bw < 1) bw = 1;
│             if (bw > 64) bw = 64;
│             c["bitWidth"] = bw;
│             QVariantList bits = c.value("inputBits").toList();
│             while (bits.size() < bw) bits.prepend(false);
│             while (bits.size() > bw) bits.removeFirst();
│             c["inputBits"] = bits;
│             QString t = c.value("type").toString();
│             if (t == "splitter" || t == "hub") {
│                 QVariantList splits = c.value("outputSplits").toList();
│                 int sum = 0;
│                 for (auto& sv : splits) sum += sv.toInt();
│                 if (sum != bw) {
│                     QVariantList ns;
│                     ns.reserve(bw);
│                     for (int i = 0; i < bw; ++i) ns.append(1);
│                     c["outputSplits"] = ns;
│                 }
│             }
│             structural = true;
│         } else if (key == "inputCount") {
│             int v = value.toInt();
│             if (v < 2) v = 2;
│             if (v > 64) v = 64;
│             c[key] = v;
│             structural = true;
│         } else if (key == "rotation") {
│             int v = value.toInt();
│             v = ((v % 4) + 4) % 4;
│             c["rotation"] = v;
│             structural = true;
│         } else {
│             c[key] = value;
│         }
│         ctx.components[idx] = c;
│         evaluateAll();
│         emit changed();
│         if (structural) emit structureChanged();
│     }
│     
│     void Circuit::rotateComponent(const QString& id, int delta) {
│         if (m_viewOnly) return;
│         int idx = indexOfComponent(id);
│         if (idx < 0) return;
│         Context& ctx = currentCtx();
│         QVariantMap c = ctx.components[idx].toMap();
│         int cur = c.value("rotation").toInt();
│         int nw = ((cur + delta) % 4 + 4) % 4;
│         if (nw == cur) return;
│         pushUndo("rot:" + id);
│         c["rotation"] = nw;
│         ctx.components[idx] = c;
│         emit changed();
│         emit structureChanged();
│     }
│     
│     void Circuit::renameComponent(const QString& id, const QString& name) {
│         if (m_viewOnly) return;
│         int idx = indexOfComponent(id);
│         if (idx < 0) return;
│         Context& ctx = currentCtx();
│         QVariantMap c = ctx.components[idx].toMap();
│         if (c.value("name").toString() == name) return;
│         pushUndo("rename:" + id);
│         c["name"] = name;
│         ctx.components[idx] = c;
│         emit changed();
│     }
│     
│     void Circuit::setSplitterSplits(const QString& id, const QString& splitsStr) {
│         if (m_viewOnly) return;
│         int idx = indexOfComponent(id);
│         if (idx < 0) return;
│         Context& ctx = currentCtx();
│         QVariantMap c = ctx.components[idx].toMap();
│         QString t = c.value("type").toString();
│         if (t != "splitter" && t != "hub") return;
│         int bw = c.value("bitWidth").toInt();
│         if (bw < 1) bw = 1;
│         QString s = splitsStr;
│         s.replace(',', ' ');
│         QStringList parts = s.split(' ', Qt::SkipEmptyParts);
│         QVariantList splits;
│         int sum = 0;
│         for (auto& p : parts) {
│             bool ok = false;
│             int v = p.toInt(&ok);
│             if (!ok || v < 1) continue;
│             splits.append(v); sum += v;
│         }
│         if (sum != bw || splits.isEmpty()) return;
│         if (c.value("outputSplits").toList() == splits) return;
│         pushUndo("splits:" + id);
│         c["outputSplits"] = splits;
│         ctx.components[idx] = c;
│         evaluateAll();
│         emit changed();
│         emit structureChanged();
│     }
│     
│     QString Circuit::getSplitterSplitsStr(const QString& id) const {
│         int idx = indexOfComponent(id);
│         if (idx < 0) return {};
│         QVariantMap c = currentCtx().components[idx].toMap();
│         QString t = c.value("type").toString();
│         if (t != "splitter" && t != "hub") return {};
│         QVariantList splits = c.value("outputSplits").toList();
│         QStringList parts;
│         for (auto& v : splits) parts.append(QString::number(v.toInt()));
│         return parts.join(",");
│     }
│     
│     QString Circuit::addText(double x, double y, const QString& text) {
│         QString id = addComponent("text", x, y, 1, 2, "");
│         if (!id.isEmpty()) setTextContent(id, text);
│         return id;
│     }
│     
│     void Circuit::setTextContent(const QString& id, const QString& content) {
│         if (m_viewOnly) return;
│         int idx = indexOfComponent(id);
│         if (idx < 0) return;
│         Context& ctx = currentCtx();
│         QVariantMap c = ctx.components[idx].toMap();
│         if (c.value("type").toString() != "text") return;
│         if (c.value("content").toString() == content) return;
│         pushUndo("text:" + id);
│         c["content"] = content;
│         ctx.components[idx] = c;
│         emit changed();
│     }

│     QVariantList Circuit::subcircuits() const {
│         QVariantList out;
│         for (auto it = m_subNames.begin(); it != m_subNames.end(); ++it) {
│             QVariantMap m;
│             m["id"] = it.key();
│             m["name"] = it.value();
│             const Context* ctx = ctxById(it.key());
│             int inCount = 0, outCount = 0;
│             if (ctx) {
│                 for (auto& cv : ctx->components) {
│                     QString t = cv.toMap().value("type").toString();
│                     if (t == "input") inCount++;
│                     else if (t == "output") outCount++;
│                 }
│             }
│             m["inCount"] = inCount;
│             m["outCount"] = outCount;
│             out.append(m);
│         }
│         return out;
│     }

│     QVariantList Circuit::editContexts() const {
│         QVariantList out;
│         QVariantMap rootMap;
│         rootMap["id"] = "";
│         rootMap["name"] = "主电路";
│         out.append(rootMap);
│         QStringList ids = m_subNames.keys();
│         std::sort(ids.begin(), ids.end());
│         for (auto& id : ids) {
│             QVariantMap m;
│             m["id"] = id;
│             m["name"] = m_subNames.value(id);
│             out.append(m);
│         }
│         return out;
│     }
│     
│     void Circuit::setEngineType(const QString& t) {
│         if (m_engineType == t) return;
│         if (t != "iter" && t != "event") return;
│         if (m_simulationRunning) pauseSimulation();
│         if (m_clockRunning) stopClock();
│         m_engineType = t;
│         saveSettings();
│         if (m_engineType == "event") {
│             rebuildEventEngine();
│             applyEngineOutputsToContexts();
│         } else {
│             applyEngineOutputsToContexts();
│             evaluateAll();
│         }
│         emit engineChanged();
│         emit engineSettingsChanged();
│         emit changed();
│         emit structureChanged();
│     }
│     
│     void Circuit::evaluateAll() {
│         IterEngine::syncSubPins(m_root, m_subContexts);
│         for (auto it = m_subContexts.begin(); it != m_subContexts.end(); ++it)
│             IterEngine::syncSubPins(it.value(), m_subContexts);
│     
│         if (m_viewOnly && !m_currentCtxId.isEmpty())
│             syncSubStateFromParent(m_currentCtxId);
│     
│         if (m_engineType == "event") {
│             if (m_simulationRunning && m_eventEngine) {
│                 rebuildEventEngine();
│                 // ★ 修复：rebuild 后必须先处理初始 Recalc 事件，否则 apply 全量读到的是初始 0
│                 //   大子电路初始事件多，需要预算足够
│                 {
│                     quint64 wd = m_eventEngine->wireDelay();
│                     quint64 gd = m_eventEngine->globalGateDelay();
│                     runEventEngineFully((wd + gd) * 64 + 200, 500000);
│                 }
│                 applyEngineOutputsToContexts();
│             } else {
│                 m_engineDirty = true;
│                 IterEngine::evaluateAll(m_root, m_subContexts);
│             }
│         } else {
│             IterEngine::evaluateAll(m_root, m_subContexts);
│         }
│     
│         bool eventSimOnCurrent = !m_viewOnly && m_engineType == "event" && m_simulationRunning;
│         if (!m_currentCtxId.isEmpty() && !eventSimOnCurrent) {
│             auto it = m_subContexts.find(m_currentCtxId);
│             if (it != m_subContexts.end())
│                 IterEngine::evaluateStandalone(it.value(), m_subContexts);
│         }
│         emit subcircuitsChanged();
│     }
│     
│     void Circuit::evaluate() {
│         evaluateAll();
│     }
│     
│     void Circuit::rebuildEventEngine() {
│         if (!m_eventEngine) return;
│     
│         IterEngine::syncSubPins(m_root, m_subContexts);
│         for (auto it = m_subContexts.begin(); it != m_subContexts.end(); ++it)
│             IterEngine::syncSubPins(it.value(), m_subContexts);
│     
│         QHash<QString, QVariantList> subComps, subWires;
│         for (auto it = m_subContexts.begin(); it != m_subContexts.end(); ++it) {
│             subComps[it.key()] = it.value().components;
│             subWires[it.key()] = it.value().wires;
│         }
│     
│         bool editingSubAsRoot = !m_currentCtxId.isEmpty() && !m_viewOnly;
│         if (editingSubAsRoot) {
│             auto it = m_subContexts.find(m_currentCtxId);
│             if (it != m_subContexts.end()) {
│                 QVariantList allWires = it.value().wires;
│                 QVariantList vw = WireNetworkSolver::generateVirtualWires(
│                     it.value().components, it.value().wires);
│                 for (const auto& w : vw) allWires.append(w);
│                 m_eventEngine->build(it.value().components, allWires, subComps, subWires);
│                 m_engineDirty = false;
│                 m_needFullApply = true;
│                 m_ctxSubInstCache.clear();
│                 return;
│             }
│         }
│     
│         QVariantList allWires = m_root.wires;
│         QVariantList vw = WireNetworkSolver::generateVirtualWires(
│             m_root.components, m_root.wires);
│         for (const auto& w : vw) allWires.append(w);
│         m_eventEngine->build(m_root.components, allWires, subComps, subWires);
│         m_engineDirty = false;
│         m_needFullApply = true;
│         m_ctxSubInstCache.clear();
│     }
│     
│     // ★ input/const 的 inputBits 同步回 ctx（与 engine 输出一致）
│     static void updateOneComponent(QVariantMap& c,
│                                    EventEngine* eng,
│                                    const QHash<QString, CircuitContext>& subContexts)
│     {
│         QString localId = c.value("id").toString();
│         QString type = c.value("type").toString();
│     
│         if (type == "sub") {
│             QString subId = c.value("subId").toString();
│             auto sit = subContexts.find(subId);
│             if (sit == subContexts.end()) return;
│     
│             QVariantList outVals;
│             for (const auto& scv : sit.value().components) {
│                 QVariantMap sc = scv.toMap();
│                 if (sc.value("type").toString() != "output") continue;
│                 QString innerQid = localId + "/" + sc.value("id").toString();
│                 QStringList outs = eng->getOutputs(innerQid);
│                 QString v = outs.isEmpty() ? QString() : outs[0];
│                 if (v.isEmpty()) {
│                     int w = sc.value("bitWidth").toInt();
│                     if (w < 1) w = 1;
│                     v = QString(w, '0');
│                 }
│                 outVals.append(v);
│             }
│             if (!outVals.isEmpty()) c["outputPorts"] = outVals;
│     
│             QVariantList inVals;
│             for (const auto& scv : sit.value().components) {
│                 QVariantMap sc = scv.toMap();
│                 if (sc.value("type").toString() != "input") continue;
│                 QString innerQid = localId + "/" + sc.value("id").toString();
│                 QStringList outs = eng->getOutputs(innerQid);
│                 QString v = outs.isEmpty() ? QString() : outs[0];
│                 if (v.isEmpty()) {
│                     int w = sc.value("bitWidth").toInt();
│                     if (w < 1) w = 1;
│                     v = QString(w, '0');
│                 }
│                 inVals.append(v);
│             }
│             if (!inVals.isEmpty()) c["inputPortValues"] = inVals;
│             return;
│         }
│     
│         QStringList outs = eng->getOutputs(localId);
│         if (!outs.isEmpty()) {
│             QVariantList ops;
│             ops.reserve(outs.size());
│             for (const auto& s : outs) ops.append(s);
│             c["outputPorts"] = ops;
│     
│             // ★ input/const 同步 inputBits（前提：调用方已保证 engine 已 run 收敛）
│             if (type == "input" || type == "const") {
│                 int bw = c.value("bitWidth").toInt();
│                 if (bw < 1) bw = 1;
│                 QString s = outs[0];
│                 if (s.length() != bw) {
│                     if (s.length() < bw) s = QString(bw - s.length(), '0') + s;
│                     else                 s = s.right(bw);
│                 }
│                 QVariantList bits;
│                 bits.reserve(bw);
│                 for (int i = 0; i < bw; ++i) bits.append(s[i] == '1');
│                 c["inputBits"] = bits;
│             }
│         }
│         if (type == "output" || type == "led") {
│             if (!outs.isEmpty()) {
│                 QVariantList ips;
│                 ips.reserve(outs.size());
│                 for (const auto& s : outs) ips.append(s);
│                 c["inputPortValues"] = ips;
│             }
│         }
│     }
│     
│     void Circuit::applyEngineOutputsToContexts() {
│         if (!m_eventEngine) return;
│         if (m_engineDirty) {
│             rebuildEventEngine();
│         }
│     
│         bool editingSubAsRoot = !m_currentCtxId.isEmpty() && !m_viewOnly;
│         Context* targetPtr = editingSubAsRoot ? ctxById(m_currentCtxId) : &m_root;
│         if (!targetPtr) targetPtr = &m_root;
│         Context& targetCtx = *targetPtr;
│     
│         if (m_needFullApply) {
│             m_needFullApply = false;
│             for (int i = 0; i < targetCtx.components.size(); ++i) {
│                 QVariantMap c = targetCtx.components[i].toMap();
│                 updateOneComponent(c, m_eventEngine, m_subContexts);
│                 targetCtx.components[i] = c;
│             }
│             m_eventEngine->clearDirtyQids();
│             return;
│         }
│     
│         QVector<QString> dirty = m_eventEngine->takeDirtyQids();
│         if (dirty.isEmpty()) return;
│     
│         QHash<QString, int> idToIdx;
│         idToIdx.reserve(targetCtx.components.size() * 2);
│         for (int i = 0; i < targetCtx.components.size(); ++i)
│             idToIdx.insert(targetCtx.components[i].toMap().value("id").toString(), i);
│     
│         QSet<QString> instanceIdsToUpdate;
│         for (const QString& qid : dirty) {
│             int slash = qid.indexOf('/');
│             QString topId = (slash < 0) ? qid : qid.left(slash);
│             if (idToIdx.contains(topId))
│                 instanceIdsToUpdate.insert(topId);
│         }
│     
│         for (const QString& id : instanceIdsToUpdate) {
│             int i = idToIdx.value(id, -1);
│             if (i < 0) continue;
│             QVariantMap c = targetCtx.components[i].toMap();
│             updateOneComponent(c, m_eventEngine, m_subContexts);
│             targetCtx.components[i] = c;
│         }
│     
│         if (m_viewOnly) refreshCurrentSubContext();
│     }
│     
│     QStringList Circuit::engineQidsFor(const QString& ctxId, const QString& localCompId) const {
│         if (!m_currentCtxId.isEmpty() && !m_viewOnly)
│             return QStringList{ localCompId };
│         if (ctxId.isEmpty()) return QStringList{ localCompId };
│     
│         auto it = m_ctxSubInstCache.constFind(ctxId);
│         QStringList instIds;
│         if (it != m_ctxSubInstCache.constEnd()) {
│             instIds = it.value();
│         } else {
│             for (const auto& cv : m_root.components) {
│                 QVariantMap c = cv.toMap();
│                 if (c.value("type").toString() != "sub") continue;
│                 if (c.value("subId").toString() == ctxId)
│                     instIds.append(c.value("id").toString());
│             }
│             m_ctxSubInstCache.insert(ctxId, instIds);
│         }
│     
│         QStringList result;
│         result.reserve(instIds.size());
│         for (const QString& instId : instIds)
│             result.append(instId + "/" + localCompId);
│         return result;
│     }
│     
│     void Circuit::runEventEngineFully(quint64 advance, quint64 budget) {
│         if (!m_eventEngine) return;
│         if (advance < 1) advance = 1;
│         if (budget < 1) budget = 200000;
│         quint64 a = advance;
│         // ★ 轮次上限从 100 提升到 200，大子电路初始事件多，需要更多轮次收敛
│         for (int round = 0; round < 200; ++round) {
│             if (m_eventEngine->isIdle()) break;
│             bool ok = m_eventEngine->run(a, budget);
│             if (m_eventEngine->isIdle()) break;
│             if (ok) {
│                 if (a < 100000000ULL) a *= 2;
│                 else a = 100000000ULL;
│             }
│         }
│     }
│     
│     void Circuit::clear() {
│         if (m_viewOnly) return;
│         pushUndo();
│         m_root = Context();
│         m_subContexts.clear();
│         m_subNames.clear();
│         m_currentCtxId = "";
│         m_viewOnly = false;
│         m_subCounter = 0;
│         m_importCounter = 0;
│         m_errors.clear();
│         m_clockStates.clear();
│         m_clockTickCount = 0;
│         m_clipboardCache.clear();
│         m_needFullApply = true;
│         m_importedFiles.clear();
│         if (m_clockRunning) stopClock();
│         if (m_simulationRunning) pauseSimulation();
│         if (m_eventEngine) m_eventEngine->clear();
│         m_engineDirty = true;
│         m_ctxSubInstCache.clear();
│         emit contextChanged();
│         emit subcircuitsChanged();
│         emit editContextsChanged();
│         emit errorsChanged();
│         emit clockChanged();
│         emit changed();
│     }
│     
│     void Circuit::refreshErrors() {
│         m_errors.clear();
│         auto checkCtx = [&](const Context& ctx, const QString& label) {
│             QHash<QString, int> idToIdx;
│             idToIdx.reserve(ctx.components.size() * 2);
│             for (int i = 0; i < ctx.components.size(); ++i)
│                 idToIdx.insert(ctx.components[i].toMap().value("id").toString(), i);
│     
│             for (auto& wv : ctx.wires) {
│                 QVariantMap w = wv.toMap();
│                 QString wt = w.value("wireType").toString();
│                 if (wt == "ortho" || wt == "virtual") continue;
│                 QString fromId = w.value("fromComp").toString();
│                 QString toId = w.value("toComp").toString();
│                 int fromPort = w.value("fromPort").toInt();
│                 int toPort = w.value("toPort").toInt();
│                 int fi = idToIdx.value(fromId, -1);
│                 int ti = idToIdx.value(toId, -1);
│                 if (fi < 0 || ti < 0) continue;
│                 int fbw = ComponentLogic::outputBitWidth(ctx.components[fi].toMap(), fromPort);
│                 int tbw = ComponentLogic::inputBitWidth(ctx.components[ti].toMap(), toPort);
│                 if (fbw != tbw) {
│                     QVariantMap err;
│                     err["type"] = "width_mismatch";
│                     err["msg"] = QString("%1：%2 位宽不匹配（源 %3 位，目标 %4 位）")
│                                      .arg(label, w.value("id").toString()).arg(fbw).arg(tbw);
│                     m_errors.append(err);
│                 }
│             }
│         };
│         checkCtx(m_root, "主电路");
│         for (auto it = m_subContexts.begin(); it != m_subContexts.end(); ++it)
│             checkCtx(it.value(), m_subNames.value(it.key(), it.key()));
│         emit errorsChanged();
│     }
│     
│     QVariantMap Circuit::toJson() const {
│         QHash<QString, quint64> gateDelays;
│         if (m_eventEngine) gateDelays = m_eventEngine->gateTypeDelays();
│         return JsonCodec::encode(m_root, m_subContexts, m_subNames,
│                                  m_subCounter, m_clockFrequency, m_clockTickCount,
│                                  m_engineType, globalGateDelay(), wireDelay(),
│                                  m_simulationSpeed, m_simulationSpeedExp, m_autoSpeed,
│                                  gateDelays);
│     }
│     
│     bool Circuit::fromJson(const QVariantMap& data) {
│         CircuitUndoData* d = und();
│         bool wasRestoring = d ? d->restoring : false;
│         if (d) d->restoring = true;
│     
│         JsonCodec::DecodedData dec = JsonCodec::decode(data);
│     
│         m_root = dec.root;
│         m_subContexts = dec.subContexts;
│         m_subNames = dec.subNames;
│         m_currentCtxId = "";
│         m_viewOnly = false;
│         m_clockStates = dec.clockStates;
│         m_subCounter = dec.subCounter;
│         m_clockTickCount = dec.clockTickCount;
│         m_needFullApply = true;
│         m_importedFiles.clear();
│     
│         if (m_eventEngine) {
│             m_eventEngine->clearGateTypeDelays();
│             for (auto it = dec.gateTypeDelays.begin(); it != dec.gateTypeDelays.end(); ++it)
│                 m_eventEngine->setGateTypeDelay(it.key(), it.value());
│         }
│     
│         if (m_engineType == "event") rebuildEventEngine();
│         else evaluateAll();
│     
│         if (!wasRestoring) {
│             if (d) {
│                 d->undoStack.clear();
│                 d->redoStack.clear();
│                 d->lastMergeKey.clear();
│                 d->lastUndoTime = QDateTime();
│             }
│         }
│         if (d) d->restoring = wasRestoring;
│     
│         if (wasRestoring) return true;
│     
│         emit contextChanged();
│         emit subcircuitsChanged();
│         emit editContextsChanged();
│         emit errorsChanged();
│         emit clockChanged();
│         emit engineChanged();
│         emit engineSettingsChanged();
│         emit structureChanged();
│         emit changed();
│         return true;
│     }
│     
│     static QByteArray extractFirstJsonObject(const QByteArray& data) {
│         int depth = 0;
│         int start = -1;
│         bool inStr = false;
│         bool esc = false;
│         for (int i = 0; i < data.size(); ++i) {
│             char c = data[i];
│             if (esc) { esc = false; continue; }
│             if (inStr) {
│                 if (c == '\\') { esc = true; continue; }
│                 if (c == '"') { inStr = false; }
│                 continue;
│             }
│             if (c == '"') { inStr = true; continue; }
│             if (c == '{') {
│                 if (depth == 0) start = i;
│                 ++depth;
│             } else if (c == '}') {
│                 --depth;
│                 if (depth == 0 && start >= 0)
│                     return data.mid(start, i - start + 1);
│             }
│         }
│         return QByteArray();
│     }
│     
│     static bool isOnlyWhitespaceFrom(const QByteArray& data, int pos) {
│         for (int i = pos; i < data.size(); ++i) {
│             char c = data[i];
│             if (c != 0x20 && c != 0x09 && c != 0x0A && c != 0x0D) return false;
│         }
│         return true;
│     }
│     
│     bool Circuit::saveToFile(const QString& path) {
│         if (path.isEmpty()) return false;
│         QJsonObject obj = QJsonObject::fromVariantMap(toJson());
│         QByteArray bytes = QJsonDocument(obj).toJson(QJsonDocument::Indented);
│         if (bytes.isEmpty()) return false;
│         QFile f(path);
│         if (path.startsWith("content://")) {
│             if (!f.open(QIODevice::WriteOnly | QIODevice::Text)) return false;
│         } else {
│             QString local = path;
│             if (local.startsWith("file://")) local = QUrl(local).toLocalFile();
│             f.setFileName(local);
│             if (!f.open(QIODevice::WriteOnly | QIODevice::Truncate | QIODevice::Text)) return false;
│         }
│         qint64 n = f.write(bytes);
│         if (n != bytes.size()) { f.close(); return false; }
│         f.close();
│         addRecentFile(path);
│         return true;
│     }
│     
│     bool Circuit::loadFromFile(const QString& path) {
│         if (path.isEmpty()) return false;
│         QFile f(path);
│         if (!f.open(QIODevice::ReadOnly | QIODevice::Text)) return false;
│         QByteArray data = f.readAll();
│         f.close();
│         QJsonParseError err;
│         QJsonDocument doc = QJsonDocument::fromJson(data, &err);
│         QByteArray cleanData;
│         bool needsHeal = false;
│         if (err.error != QJsonParseError::NoError) {
│             cleanData = extractFirstJsonObject(data);
│             if (cleanData.isEmpty()) return false;
│             if (cleanData.size() == data.size()) return false;
│             int endPos = data.indexOf(cleanData) + cleanData.size();
│             needsHeal = !isOnlyWhitespaceFrom(data, endPos);
│             QJsonParseError err2;
│             QJsonDocument doc2 = QJsonDocument::fromJson(cleanData, &err2);
│             if (err2.error != QJsonParseError::NoError) return false;
│             if (!doc2.isObject()) return false;
│             doc = doc2;
│         }
│         if (!doc.isObject()) return false;
│         bool ok = fromJson(doc.object().toVariantMap());
│         if (!ok) return false;
│         if (needsHeal) {
│             QFile wf(path);
│             bool openOk = path.startsWith("content://")
│                               ? wf.open(QIODevice::WriteOnly | QIODevice::Text)
│                               : wf.open(QIODevice::WriteOnly | QIODevice::Truncate | QIODevice::Text);
│             if (openOk) { wf.write(cleanData); wf.close(); }
│         }
│         addRecentFile(path);
│         return ok;
│     }
│     
│     void Circuit::removeSubContextsByPrefix(const QString& prefix) {
│         if (prefix.isEmpty()) return;
│         QSet<QString> subIdSet;
│         for (auto it = m_subContexts.constBegin(); it != m_subContexts.constEnd(); ++it) {
│             if (it.key().startsWith(prefix)) subIdSet.insert(it.key());
│         }
│         if (subIdSet.isEmpty()) return;
│         for (const auto& sid : subIdSet) {
│             m_subContexts.remove(sid);
│             m_subNames.remove(sid);
│         }
│         auto removeInstances = [&](Context& ctx) {
│             for (int i = ctx.components.size() - 1; i >= 0; --i) {
│                 auto c = ctx.components[i].toMap();
│                 if (c.value("type").toString() != "sub") continue;
│                 if (!subIdSet.contains(c.value("subId").toString())) continue;
│                 QString id = c.value("id").toString();
│                 for (int j = ctx.wires.size() - 1; j >= 0; --j) {
│                     auto w = ctx.wires[j].toMap();
│                     if (w.value("fromComp").toString() == id ||
│                         w.value("toComp").toString() == id)
│                         ctx.wires.removeAt(j);
│                 }
│                 ctx.components.removeAt(i);
│             }
│         };
│         removeInstances(m_root);
│         for (auto it = m_subContexts.begin(); it != m_subContexts.end(); ++it)
│             removeInstances(it.value());
│         if (subIdSet.contains(m_currentCtxId)) {
│             m_currentCtxId = "";
│             m_viewOnly = false;
│             m_currentInstancePath.clear();
│             m_browseStack.clear();
│             emit contextChanged();
│         }
│         m_ctxSubInstCache.clear();
│     }
│     
│     bool Circuit::importFromFile(const QString& path) {
│         if (path.isEmpty()) return false;
│         if (m_viewOnly) return false;
│         QFile f(path);
│         if (!f.open(QIODevice::ReadOnly | QIODevice::Text)) return false;
│         QByteArray data = f.readAll();
│         f.close();
│         if (data.isEmpty()) return false;
│         QJsonParseError err;
│         QJsonDocument doc = QJsonDocument::fromJson(data, &err);
│         if (err.error != QJsonParseError::NoError) {
│             QByteArray clean = extractFirstJsonObject(data);
│             if (clean.isEmpty()) return false;
│             QJsonParseError err2;
│             QJsonDocument doc2 = QJsonDocument::fromJson(clean, &err2);
│             if (err2.error != QJsonParseError::NoError || !doc2.isObject()) return false;
│             doc = doc2;
│         }
│         if (!doc.isObject()) return false;
│     
│         JsonCodec::DecodedData dec = JsonCodec::decode(doc.object().toVariantMap());
│         QString baseName = QFileInfo(path).completeBaseName();
│         if (baseName.isEmpty()) baseName = "外部电路";
│     
│         if (m_importedFiles.contains(path)) {
│             QString oldPrefix = m_importedFiles.value(path).value("prefix").toString();
│             removeSubContextsByPrefix(oldPrefix);
│             m_importedFiles.remove(path);
│         } else {
│             QString suffix = QString("（%1）").arg(baseName);
│             QStringList stale;
│             for (auto it = m_subNames.constBegin(); it != m_subNames.constEnd(); ++it) {
│                 if (it.value().endsWith(suffix)) stale.append(it.key());
│             }
│             if (!stale.isEmpty()) {
│                 pushUndoAll();
│                 QSet<QString> staleSet;
│                 for (const auto& sid : stale) staleSet.insert(sid);
│                 for (const auto& sid : stale) {
│                     m_subContexts.remove(sid);
│                     m_subNames.remove(sid);
│                 }
│                 auto removeInstances = [&](Context& ctx) {
│                     for (int i = ctx.components.size() - 1; i >= 0; --i) {
│                         auto c = ctx.components[i].toMap();
│                         if (c.value("type").toString() != "sub") continue;
│                         if (!staleSet.contains(c.value("subId").toString())) continue;
│                         QString id = c.value("id").toString();
│                         for (int j = ctx.wires.size() - 1; j >= 0; --j) {
│                             auto w = ctx.wires[j].toMap();
│                             if (w.value("fromComp").toString() == id ||
│                                 w.value("toComp").toString() == id)
│                                 ctx.wires.removeAt(j);
│                         }
│                         ctx.components.removeAt(i);
│                     }
│                 };
│                 removeInstances(m_root);
│                 for (auto it = m_subContexts.begin(); it != m_subContexts.end(); ++it)
│                     removeInstances(it.value());
│                 m_ctxSubInstCache.clear();
│             } else {
│                 pushUndoAll();
│             }
│         }
│     
│         QString prefix;
│         do {
│             prefix = QString("IMP%1_").arg(++m_importCounter);
│         } while (m_subContexts.contains(prefix + "ROOT"));
│     
│         QHash<QString, QString> subIdMap;
│         subIdMap.insert(QString(), prefix + "ROOT");
│         for (auto it = dec.subContexts.constBegin(); it != dec.subContexts.constEnd(); ++it)
│             subIdMap.insert(it.key(), prefix + it.key());
│     
│         auto processCtx = [&](CircuitContext ctx) -> CircuitContext {
│             for (int i = 0; i < ctx.components.size(); ++i) {
│                 QVariantMap c = ctx.components[i].toMap();
│                 QString oldId = c.value("id").toString();
│                 c["id"] = prefix + oldId;
│                 if (c.value("type").toString() == "sub") {
│                     QString oldSubId = c.value("subId").toString();
│                     if (subIdMap.contains(oldSubId))
│                         c["subId"] = subIdMap.value(oldSubId);
│                 }
│                 ctx.components[i] = c;
│             }
│             for (int i = 0; i < ctx.wires.size(); ++i) {
│                 QVariantMap w = ctx.wires[i].toMap();
│                 w["id"] = prefix + w.value("id").toString();
│                 if (w.contains("fromComp"))
│                     w["fromComp"] = prefix + w.value("fromComp").toString();
│                 if (w.contains("toComp"))
│                     w["toComp"] = prefix + w.value("toComp").toString();
│                 ctx.wires[i] = w;
│             }
│             ctx.compCounter = ctx.components.size();
│             ctx.wireCounter = ctx.wires.size();
│             return ctx;
│         };
│     
│         QStringList newSubIds;
│         {
│             CircuitContext ctx = processCtx(dec.root);
│             QString newId = subIdMap.value(QString());
│             m_subContexts[newId] = ctx;
│             m_subNames[newId] = QString("主电路（%1）").arg(baseName);
│             newSubIds.append(newId);
│         }
│         for (auto it = dec.subContexts.constBegin(); it != dec.subContexts.constEnd(); ++it) {
│             CircuitContext ctx = processCtx(it.value());
│             QString newId = subIdMap.value(it.key());
│             m_subContexts[newId] = ctx;
│             QString oldName = dec.subNames.value(it.key(), it.key());
│             m_subNames[newId] = QString("%1（%2）").arg(oldName, baseName);
│             newSubIds.append(newId);
│         }
│     
│         QVariantMap info;
│         info["path"] = path;
│         info["prefix"] = prefix;
│         info["baseName"] = baseName;
│         info["subIds"] = newSubIds;
│         m_importedFiles[path] = info;
│     
│         for (auto it = m_subContexts.constBegin(); it != m_subContexts.constEnd(); ++it) {
│             for (const auto& cv : it.value().components) {
│                 QVariantMap c = cv.toMap();
│                 if (c.value("type").toString() == "clock") {
│                     if (!m_clockStates.contains(c.value("id").toString()))
│                         m_clockStates[c.value("id").toString()] = false;
│                 }
│             }
│         }
│     
│         m_ctxSubInstCache.clear();
│         evaluateAll();
│         emit subcircuitsChanged();
│         emit editContextsChanged();
│         emit changed();
│         emit structureChanged();
│         return true;
│     }
│     
│     bool Circuit::removeImportedFile(const QString& path) {
│         if (m_viewOnly) return false;
│         if (!m_importedFiles.contains(path)) return false;
│         pushUndoAll();
│         QString prefix = m_importedFiles.value(path).value("prefix").toString();
│         removeSubContextsByPrefix(prefix);
│         m_importedFiles.remove(path);
│         evaluateAll();
│         emit subcircuitsChanged();
│         emit editContextsChanged();
│         emit changed();
│         emit structureChanged();
│         return true;
│     }
│     
│     QVariantList Circuit::importedFiles() const {
│         QVariantList out;
│         for (auto it = m_importedFiles.constBegin(); it != m_importedFiles.constEnd(); ++it) {
│             QVariantMap m;
│             m["path"] = it.value().value("path");
│             m["prefix"] = it.value().value("prefix");
│             m["baseName"] = it.value().value("baseName");
│             m["subCount"] = it.value().value("subIds").toList().size();
│             out.append(m);
│         }
│         return out;
│     }
│     
│     UndoEntry Circuit::captureUndoEntry(UndoScope scope, const QString& ctxId,
│                                         const QString& mergeKey) const
│     {
│         UndoEntry e;
│         e.scope = scope;
│         e.mergeKey = mergeKey;
│         e.time = QDateTime::currentDateTime();
│     
│         if (scope == UndoScope::CurrentCtx) {
│             e.ctxId = ctxId;
│             if (ctxId.isEmpty()) e.singleCtx = m_root;
│             else {
│                 auto it = m_subContexts.constFind(ctxId);
│                 if (it != m_subContexts.constEnd()) e.singleCtx = it.value();
│             }
│         } else if (scope == UndoScope::Full) {
│             e.allContexts.insert(QString(), m_root);
│             for (auto it = m_subContexts.constBegin(); it != m_subContexts.constEnd(); ++it)
│                 e.allContexts.insert(it.key(), it.value());
│             e.subNames = m_subNames;
│             e.subCounter = m_subCounter;
│             e.clockStates = m_clockStates;
│             e.importedFiles = m_importedFiles;
│         } else {
│             e.subNames = m_subNames;
│             e.subCounter = m_subCounter;
│         }
│         return e;
│     }
│     
│     UndoEntry Circuit::captureSameScopeAs(const UndoEntry& ref) const {
│         return captureUndoEntry(ref.scope, ref.ctxId, QString());
│     }
│     
│     void Circuit::applyUndoEntry(const UndoEntry& e) {
│         QString savedCtx = m_currentCtxId;
│         bool savedView = m_viewOnly;
│     
│         if (e.scope == UndoScope::CurrentCtx) {
│             if (e.ctxId.isEmpty()) m_root = e.singleCtx;
│             else m_subContexts[e.ctxId] = e.singleCtx;
│         } else if (e.scope == UndoScope::Full) {
│             auto it = e.allContexts.constFind(QString());
│             if (it != e.allContexts.constEnd()) m_root = it.value();
│             m_subContexts.clear();
│             for (auto it2 = e.allContexts.constBegin(); it2 != e.allContexts.constEnd(); ++it2) {
│                 if (!it2.key().isEmpty())
│                     m_subContexts.insert(it2.key(), it2.value());
│             }
│             m_subNames = e.subNames;
│             m_subCounter = e.subCounter;
│             m_clockStates = e.clockStates;
│             m_importedFiles = e.importedFiles;
│         } else {
│             m_subNames = e.subNames;
│             m_subCounter = e.subCounter;
│         }
│     
│         if (!savedCtx.isEmpty() && !m_subContexts.contains(savedCtx)) {
│             savedCtx = "";
│             savedView = false;
│         }
│         bool ctxChanged = (savedCtx != m_currentCtxId) || (savedView != m_viewOnly);
│         m_currentCtxId = savedCtx;
│         m_viewOnly = savedView;
│     
│         m_engineDirty = true;
│         if (m_engineType == "event") {
│             rebuildEventEngine();
│             applyEngineOutputsToContexts();
│         } else {
│             evaluateAll();
│         }
│     
│         if (ctxChanged) emit contextChanged();
│         emit subcircuitsChanged();
│         emit editContextsChanged();
│         emit errorsChanged();
│         emit clockChanged();
│         emit structureChanged();
│         emit changed();
│     }
│     
│     void Circuit::pushUndoInternal(UndoScope scope, const QString& ctxId,
│                                    const QString& mergeKey)
│     {
│         CircuitUndoData* d = und();
│         if (!d) return;
│         if (m_viewOnly) return;
│         if (d->restoring) return;
│         if (d->redoRunning) return;
│     
│         if (!mergeKey.isEmpty() && mergeKey == d->lastMergeKey) {
│             if (d->lastUndoTime.isValid() &&
│                 d->lastUndoTime.msecsTo(QDateTime::currentDateTime()) < 500) {
│                 d->lastUndoTime = QDateTime::currentDateTime();
│                 return;
│             }
│         }
│     
│         UndoEntry e = captureUndoEntry(scope, ctxId, mergeKey);
│         d->undoStack.push_back(e);
│         while (d->undoStack.size() > static_cast<size_t>(d->maxUndo))
│             d->undoStack.erase(d->undoStack.begin());
│         d->redoStack.clear();
│         d->lastMergeKey = mergeKey;
│         d->lastUndoTime = QDateTime::currentDateTime();
│         emit undoRedoChanged();
│     }
│     
│     void Circuit::pushUndo(const QString& mergeKey) {
│         pushUndoInternal(UndoScope::CurrentCtx, m_currentCtxId, mergeKey);
│     }
│     
│     void Circuit::pushUndoAll(const QString& mergeKey) {
│         pushUndoInternal(UndoScope::Full, QString(), mergeKey);
│     }
│     
│     void Circuit::pushUndoMeta(const QString& mergeKey) {
│         pushUndoInternal(UndoScope::MetaOnly, QString(), mergeKey);
│     }
│     
│     void Circuit::breakUndoMerge() {
│         CircuitUndoData* d = und();
│         if (!d) return;
│         d->lastMergeKey.clear();
│         d->lastUndoTime = QDateTime();
│     }
│     
│     void Circuit::undo() {
│         CircuitUndoData* d = und();
│         if (!d) return;
│         if (m_viewOnly) return;
│         if (d->redoRunning) return;
│         if (d->undoStack.empty()) return;
│         d->redoRunning = true;
│         UndoEntry toUndo = d->undoStack.back();
│         d->undoStack.pop_back();
│         UndoEntry cur = captureSameScopeAs(toUndo);
│         d->redoStack.push_back(cur);
│         while (d->redoStack.size() > static_cast<size_t>(d->maxUndo))
│             d->redoStack.erase(d->redoStack.begin());
│         breakUndoMerge();
│         d->restoring = true;
│         applyUndoEntry(toUndo);
│         d->restoring = false;
│         d->redoRunning = false;
│         emit undoRedoChanged();
│     }
│     
│     void Circuit::redo() {
│         CircuitUndoData* d = und();
│         if (!d) return;
│         if (m_viewOnly) return;
│         if (d->redoRunning) return;
│         if (d->redoStack.empty()) return;
│         d->redoRunning = true;
│         UndoEntry toRedo = d->redoStack.back();
│         d->redoStack.pop_back();
│         UndoEntry cur = captureSameScopeAs(toRedo);
│         d->undoStack.push_back(cur);
│         while (d->undoStack.size() > static_cast<size_t>(d->maxUndo))
│             d->undoStack.erase(d->undoStack.begin());
│         breakUndoMerge();
│         d->restoring = true;
│         applyUndoEntry(toRedo);
│         d->restoring = false;
│         d->redoRunning = false;
│         emit undoRedoChanged();
│     }
│     
│     void Circuit::loadRecentFiles() { m_recentFiles = RecentStore::load(); }
│     void Circuit::saveRecentFiles() { RecentStore::save(m_recentFiles); }
│     
│     void Circuit::addRecentFile(const QString& path) {
│         if (path.isEmpty()) return;
│         for (int i = m_recentFiles.size() - 1; i >= 0; --i) {
│             if (m_recentFiles[i].toMap().value("path").toString() == path)
│                 m_recentFiles.removeAt(i);
│         }
│         QVariantMap m;
│         m["path"] = path;
│         QFileInfo fi(path);
│         m["name"] = fi.fileName();
│         m["time"] = QDateTime::currentDateTime().toString("yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm");
│         m_recentFiles.prepend(m);
│         while (m_recentFiles.size() > 10) m_recentFiles.removeLast();
│         saveRecentFiles();
│         QMetaObject::invokeMethod(this, [this]() {
│             emit recentFilesChanged();
│         }, Qt::QueuedConnection);
│     }
│     
│     void Circuit::removeRecentFile(const QString& path) {
│         for (int i = m_recentFiles.size() - 1; i >= 0; --i) {
│             if (m_recentFiles[i].toMap().value("path").toString() == path)
│                 m_recentFiles.removeAt(i);
│         }
│         saveRecentFiles();
│         emit recentFilesChanged();
│     }
│     
│     void Circuit::clearRecentFiles() {
│         m_recentFiles.clear();
│         saveRecentFiles();
│         emit recentFilesChanged();
│     }

│     QVariantMap Circuit::allPortsInfo() const {
│         QVariantMap out;
│         const Context& ctx = currentCtx();
│         for (const auto& cv : ctx.components) {
│             QVariantMap c = cv.toMap();
│             QString id = c.value("id").toString();
│             QVariantMap info;
│             info["out"] = ComponentLogic::outputPorts(c);
│             info["in"] = ComponentLogic::inputPorts(c);
│             out[id] = info;
│         }
│         return out;
│     }

│     QString Circuit::getInputBase(const QString& id) const {
│         int idx = indexOfComponent(id);
│         if (idx < 0) return "bin";
│         QVariantMap c = currentCtx().components[idx].toMap();
│         QString b = c.value("displayBase").toString();
│         return b.isEmpty() ? "bin" : b;
│     }

│     void Circuit::setInputBase(const QString& id, const QString& base) {
│         if (m_viewOnly) return;
│         int idx = indexOfComponent(id);
│         if (idx < 0) return;
│         Context& ctx = currentCtx();
│         QVariantMap c = ctx.components[idx].toMap();
│         if (c.value("displayBase").toString() == base) return;
│         pushUndo("base:" + id);
│         c["displayBase"] = base;
│         ctx.components[idx] = c;
│         emit changed();
│     }

│         return ValueCodec::format(binStr, base, bw);
│     }
│     
│     QString Circuit::getInputAsString(const QString& id) const {
│         int idx = indexOfComponent(id);
│         if (idx < 0) return "";
│         QVariantMap c = currentCtx().components[idx].toMap();
│         QString base = c.value("displayBase").toString();
│         if (base.isEmpty()) base = "bin";
│         int bw = c.value("bitWidth").toInt();
│         if (bw < 1) bw = 1;
│         if (bw > 64) bw = 64;
│         QVariantList bits = c.value("inputBits").toList();
│         QString binStr = ValueCodec::bitsToString(bits, bw);
│         return ValueCodec::format(binStr, base, bw);
│     }
│     
│     QString Circuit::setInputFromString(const QString& id, const QString& text) {
│         if (m_viewOnly) return "浏览模式不可修改";
│         int idx = indexOfComponent(id);
│         if (idx < 0) return "元件不存在";
│         Context& ctx = currentCtx();
│         QVariantMap c = ctx.components[idx].toMap();
│         QString t = c.value("type").toString();
│         if (t != "input" && t != "const") return "不是可编辑的元件";
│         int bw = c.value("bitWidth").toInt();
│         if (bw < 1) bw = 1;
│         if (bw > 64) bw = 64;
│         QString base = c.value("displayBase").toString();
│         if (base.isEmpty()) base = "bin";
│         QString err;
│         QVariantList bits = ValueCodec::parse(text, base, bw, &err);
│         if (!err.isEmpty()) return err;
│         if (c.value("inputBits").toList() == bits) return "";
│         c["inputBits"] = bits;
│         ctx.components[idx] = c;
│     
│         if (m_engineType == "event" && m_eventEngine && m_simulationRunning) {
│             if (m_engineDirty) {
│                 rebuildEventEngine();
│                 quint64 wd0 = m_eventEngine->wireDelay();
│                 quint64 gd0 = m_eventEngine->globalGateDelay();
│                 runEventEngineFully((wd0 + gd0) * 64 + 200, 500000);
│                 applyEngineOutputsToContexts();
│             }
│             QString binStr = ValueCodec::bitsToString(bits, bw);
│             QStringList qids = engineQidsFor(m_currentCtxId, id);
│             for (const auto& qid : qids)
│                 m_eventEngine->triggerInputString(qid, binStr);
│             quint64 wd = m_eventEngine->wireDelay();
│             quint64 gd = m_eventEngine->globalGateDelay();
│             runEventEngineFully((wd + gd) * 64 + 200, 500000);
│             applyEngineOutputsToContexts();
│             emit simulationTick();
│         } else if (m_engineType == "event") {
│             m_engineDirty = true;
│         } else {
│             evaluateAll();
│         }
│         emit changed();
│         return "";
│     }
│     
│     QVariantMap Circuit::statistics() const {
│         QVariantMap result;
│         QHash<QString, int> typeCount;
│         int totalGates = 0, totalInputs = 0, totalOutputs = 0, totalSplits = 0;
│         int totalHubs = 0, totalClocks = 0, totalSubInst = 0, totalTexts = 0;
│         int totalConsts = 0;
│     
│         std::function<void(const Context&, QSet<QString>&)> countCtx =
│             [&](const Context& ctx, QSet<QString>& visited) {
│                 for (const auto& cv : ctx.components) {
│                     QVariantMap c = cv.toMap();
│                     QString t = c.value("type").toString();
│                     if (t == "text") { totalTexts++; continue; }
│                     if (t == "input") { totalInputs++; continue; }
│                     if (t == "const") { totalConsts++; continue; }
│                     if (t == "output" || t == "led") { totalOutputs++; continue; }
│                     if (t == "clock") { totalClocks++; continue; }
│                     if (t == "splitter") { totalSplits++; continue; }
│                     if (t == "hub") { totalHubs++; continue; }
│                     if (t == "sub") {
│                         totalSubInst++;
│                         QString subId = c.value("subId").toString();
│                         if (!visited.contains(subId)) {
│                             visited.insert(subId);
│                             auto it = m_subContexts.find(subId);
│                             if (it != m_subContexts.end()) countCtx(it.value(), visited);
│                         }
│                         continue;
│                     }
│                     totalGates++;
│                     typeCount[t]++;
│                 }
│             };
│     
│         QSet<QString> visited;
│         countCtx(m_root, visited);
│     
│         result["totalGates"] = totalGates;
│         result["totalInputs"] = totalInputs;
│         result["totalConsts"] = totalConsts;
│         result["totalOutputs"] = totalOutputs;
│         result["totalSplits"] = totalSplits;
│         result["totalHubs"] = totalHubs;
│         result["totalClocks"] = totalClocks;
│         result["totalSubInst"] = totalSubInst;
│         result["totalTexts"] = totalTexts;
│         result["subCount"] = m_subContexts.size();
│     
│         QVariantMap byType;
│         for (auto it = typeCount.begin(); it != typeCount.end(); ++it)
│             byType[it.key()] = it.value();
│         result["byType"] = byType;
│         return result;
│     }

├── Circuit_selection.cpp

│     #include "../Circuit.h"
│     #include "../io/ClipboardCodec.h"
