│     #include "EventEngine.h"
│     #include "../ComponentLogic.h"
│     #include <QDebug>
│     #include <algorithm>
│     
│     EventEngine::EventEngine() {}
│     EventEngine::~EventEngine() {}
│     
│     void EventEngine::setGlobalGateDelay(quint64 d) {
│         if (d < 1) d = 1;
│         m_opts.globalGateDelay = d;
│     }
│     void EventEngine::setWireDelay(quint64 d) {
│         if (d < 1) d = 1;
│         m_opts.wireDelay = d;
│     }
│     void EventEngine::setGateTypeDelay(const QString& type, quint64 d) {
│         if (d < 1) d = 1;
│         m_gateDelays[type] = d;
│     }
│     void EventEngine::removeGateTypeDelay(const QString& type) {
│         m_gateDelays.remove(type);
│     }
│     void EventEngine::clearGateTypeDelays() {
│         m_gateDelays.clear();
│     }
│     
│     quint64 EventEngine::gateDelayFor(const QString& type) const {
│         auto it = m_gateDelays.constFind(type);
│         if (it != m_gateDelays.constEnd()) return it.value();
│         return m_opts.globalGateDelay;
│     }
│     
│     void EventEngine::clear() {
│         m_nodes.clear();
│         m_idToIdx.clear();
│         m_inDrivers.clear();
│         m_outDests.clear();
│         m_portDriverValues.clear();
│         while (!m_queue.empty()) m_queue.pop();
│         m_time = 0;
│         m_seq = 0;
│         m_totalEventsProcessed = 0;
│         m_dirtyQids.clear();
│         m_dirtySet.clear();
│     }
│     
│     void EventEngine::reset() {
│         QVector<QPair<int, QString>> savedInputs;
│         savedInputs.reserve(m_nodes.size());
│         for (int i = 0; i < m_nodes.size(); ++i) {
│             const Node& n = m_nodes[i];
│             if (n.isInput && !n.outVals.isEmpty())
│                 savedInputs.append(qMakePair(i, n.outVals[0]));
│         }
│     
│         while (!m_queue.empty()) m_queue.pop();
│         m_time = 0;
│         m_seq = 0;
│         m_totalEventsProcessed = 0;
│         m_dirtyQids.clear();
│         m_dirtySet.clear();
│     
│         for (auto& n : m_nodes) {
│             for (auto& s : n.inVals)  s = QString(s.length(), '0');
│             for (auto& s : n.outVals) s = QString(s.length(), '0');
│         }
│         for (auto it = m_portDriverValues.begin(); it != m_portDriverValues.end(); ++it)
│             it.value().clear();
│     
│         initValues();
│     
│         for (const auto& p : savedInputs) {
│             int i = p.first;
│             const QString& v = p.second;
│             if (i < 0 || i >= m_nodes.size()) continue;
│             if (!m_nodes[i].outVals.isEmpty()) m_nodes[i].outVals[0] = v;
│             if (!m_nodes[i].inVals.isEmpty())  m_nodes[i].inVals[0]  = v;
│         }
│     
│         initDriverValues();
│     }

│         // takeDirtyQids() 取走并清空
│         QVector<QString> takeDirtyQids() {
│             QVector<QString> r;
│             r.swap(m_dirtyQids);
│             m_dirtySet.clear();
│             return r;
│         }
│         void clearDirtyQids() { m_dirtyQids.clear(); m_dirtySet.clear(); }
│         int dirtyCount() const { return m_dirtyQids.size(); }
│     
│         // 去重：同一节点一次 run() 里只追加一次
│         void markDirty(const QString& qid) {
│             if (m_dirtySet.contains(qid)) return;
│             m_dirtySet.insert(qid);
│             m_dirtyQids.append(qid);
│         }

│             QString localId;
│             QString type;

│                 if (time != o.time) return time > o.time;

│         static quint64 pkey(int idx, int port) {
│             return (quint64(quint32(idx)) << 16) | quint64(port & 0xFFFF);
│         }
│         static qint64 dkey(int idx, int port) {
│             return (qint64(qint32(idx)) << 16) | qint64(port & 0xFFFF);
│         }

│     #include "EventEngine.h"
│     #include "../ComponentLogic.h"
│     #include <algorithm>
│     
│     QString EventEngine::resolveMultiDriver(const QVector<QString>& vals, int bw) {
│         if (bw < 1) bw = 1;
│         if (vals.isEmpty()) return QString(bw, '0');
│     
│         if (vals.size() == 1) {
│             QString s = vals[0];
│             if (s.length() < bw) s = QString(bw - s.length(), '0') + s;
│             else if (s.length() > bw) s = s.right(bw);
│             return s;
│         }
│     
│         QString result(bw, '0');
│         for (int pos = 0; pos < bw; ++pos) {
│             bool has0 = false, has1 = false, hasE = false;
│             for (const auto& v : vals) {
│                 QString s = v;
│                 if (s.length() < bw) s = QString(bw - s.length(), '0') + s;
│                 else if (s.length() > bw) s = s.right(bw);
│                 QChar c = (pos < s.length()) ? s[pos] : QChar('0');
│                 if (c == '0') has0 = true;
│                 else if (c == '1') has1 = true;
│                 else if (c == 'E') hasE = true;
│             }
│             if (hasE)              result[pos] = 'E';
│             else if (has0 && has1) result[pos] = 'E';
│             else if (has1)         result[pos] = '1';
│             else if (has0)         result[pos] = '0';
│             else                   result[pos] = 'Z';
│         }
│         return result;
│     }
│     
│     QString EventEngine::mapUnary(const QString& src, int bw) {
│         QString out; out.reserve(bw);
│         int n = src.length();
│         for (int b = 0; b < bw; ++b) {
│             QChar c = (b < n) ? src[b] : QChar('0');
│             if (c == 'Z' || c == 'E') out += 'E';
│             else                       out += (c == '1') ? '0' : '1';
│         }
│         return out;
│     }
│     
│     void EventEngine::scheduleRecalc(int idx, quint64 at) {
│         Event ev;
│         ev.kind = Event::Recalc;
│         ev.time = at;
│         ev.seq = ++m_seq;
│         ev.nodeIdx = idx;
│         ev.port = 0;
│         ev.toIdx = -1;
│         ev.toPort = -1;
│         m_queue.push(ev);
│     }
│     
│     void EventEngine::scheduleWirePropagation(int fromIdx, int fromPort,
│                                               int toIdx, int toPort,
│                                               const QString& value, quint64 at) {
│         Event ev;
│         ev.kind = Event::Wire;
│         ev.time = at;
│         ev.seq = ++m_seq;
│         ev.nodeIdx = fromIdx;
│         ev.port = fromPort;
│         ev.toIdx = toIdx;
│         ev.toPort = toPort;
│         ev.value = value;
│         m_queue.push(ev);
│     }
│     
│     bool EventEngine::step() {
│         if (m_queue.empty()) return false;
│         Event ev = m_queue.top();
│         m_queue.pop();
│         if (ev.time > m_time) m_time = ev.time;
│         processEvent(ev);
│         ++m_totalEventsProcessed;
│         return true;
│     }
│     
│     int EventEngine::drainNow(quint64 budget) {
│         if (budget == 0) budget = m_opts.runBudget;
│         int processed = 0;
│         quint64 startTime = m_time;
│         while (!m_queue.empty()) {
│             if (m_queue.top().time > startTime) break;
│             if (quint64(processed) >= budget) break;
│             Event ev = m_queue.top();
│             m_queue.pop();
│             processEvent(ev);
│             ++processed;
│             ++m_totalEventsProcessed;
│         }
│         return processed;
│     }
│     
│     bool EventEngine::run(quint64 units, quint64 budget) {
│         if (budget == 0) budget = m_opts.runBudget;
│         quint64 endTime = m_time + units;
│         quint64 processed = 0;
│     
│         while (!m_queue.empty()) {
│             if (m_queue.top().time > endTime) {
│                 if (m_time < endTime) m_time = endTime;
│                 return true;
│             }
│             if (processed >= budget) {
│                 return false;
│             }
│             Event ev = m_queue.top();
│             m_queue.pop();
│             if (ev.time > m_time) m_time = ev.time;
│             processEvent(ev);
│             ++processed;
│             ++m_totalEventsProcessed;
│         }
│     
│         if (m_time < endTime) m_time = endTime;
│         return true;
│     }
│     
│     void EventEngine::processEvent(const Event& ev) {
│         if (ev.kind == Event::Recalc) {
│             recalcNode(ev.nodeIdx);
│             return;
│         }
│     
│         int ti = ev.toIdx;
│         int tp = ev.toPort;
│         if (ti < 0 || ti >= m_nodes.size()) return;
│         Node& tn = m_nodes[ti];
│         if (tp < 0 || tp >= tn.inVals.size()) return;
│     
│         quint64 tkey = pkey(ti, tp);
│         qint64  dk   = dkey(ev.nodeIdx, ev.port);
│         QHash<qint64, QString>& dv = m_portDriverValues[tkey];
│         dv[dk] = ev.value;
│     
│         // 复用成员缓冲区，避免每事件堆分配
│         QVector<QString>& driverVals = m_driverValBuf;
│         driverVals.clear();
│         auto it = m_inDrivers.constFind(tkey);
│         if (it != m_inDrivers.constEnd()) {
│             driverVals.reserve(it.value().size());
│             for (const auto& d : it.value()) {
│                 qint64 k = dkey(d.fromIdx, d.fromPort);
│                 QString v = dv.value(k, QString());
│                 if (v.isEmpty()) {
│                     const Node& fn = m_nodes[d.fromIdx];
│                     if (d.fromPort >= 0 && d.fromPort < fn.outVals.size())
│                         v = fn.outVals[d.fromPort];
│                 }
│                 driverVals.append(v);
│             }
│         } else {
│             driverVals.append(ev.value);
│         }
│     
│         int bw = tn.inVals[tp].length();
│         if (bw < 1) bw = tn.bitWidth;
│         QString merged = resolveMultiDriver(driverVals, bw);
│     
│         if (tn.inVals[tp] == merged) return;
│         tn.inVals[tp] = merged;
│         recalcNode(ti);
│     }
│     
│     void EventEngine::recalcNode(int idx) {
│         Node& n = m_nodes[idx];
│         QVector<QString> newOuts = computeOutputs(n, n.inVals);
│         bool changed = false;
│         if (newOuts.size() != n.outVals.size()) {
│             n.outVals = newOuts;
│             changed = true;
│         } else {
│             for (int i = 0; i < newOuts.size(); ++i) {
│                 if (n.outVals[i] != newOuts[i]) { n.outVals[i] = newOuts[i]; changed = true; }
│             }
│         }
│         if (!changed) return;
│     
│         // 记录输出变化的节点，供 Circuit 侧增量更新（去重）
│         markDirty(n.qid);
│     
│         quint64 at = m_time + gateDelayFor(n.type);
│         for (int p = 0; p < n.outVals.size(); ++p) {
│             auto it = m_outDests.constFind(pkey(idx, p));
│             if (it == m_outDests.constEnd()) continue;
│             const QString v = n.outVals[p];
│             for (const auto& d : it.value()) {
│                 scheduleWirePropagation(idx, p, d.first, d.second, v, at);
│             }
│         }
│     }
│     
│     QVector<QString> EventEngine::computeOutputs(const Node& n,
│                                                  const QVector<QString>& inputs) const
│     {
│         const int bw = n.bitWidth;
│         const QString& t = n.type;
│     
│         // ---- 数据源相关类型：各自处理 ----
│         if (t == "input" || t == "const" || t == "output" || t == "led") {
│             QString s = inputs.isEmpty() ? QString(bw, '0') : inputs[0];
│             if (s.length() != bw) {
│                 if (s.length() < bw) s = QString(bw - s.length(), '0') + s;
│                 else                 s = s.right(bw);
│             }
│             return { s };
│         }
│         if (t == "clock") {
│             if (!n.outVals.isEmpty()) return { n.outVals[0] };
│             return { QString(bw, '0') };
│         }
│     
│         // ---- 纯组合逻辑类型：委托 ComponentLogic 统一实现 ----
│         // 覆盖 not/and/or/nand/nor/xor/xnor/tgate/ntran/ptran/splitter/hub
│         // 未识别类型（如 sub）返回空，保持与旧行为一致（sub 节点输出由外部驱动）
│         return ComponentLogic::computeCombinationalOutputs(
│             t, inputs, bw, n.raw.value("outputSplits").toList());
│     }

└── EventEngine_trigger.cpp
    内容:
      #include "EventEngine.h"
      
      void EventEngine::triggerInputBit(const QString& compId, int bit, bool value) {
          int idx = idxOf(compId);
          if (idx < 0) return;
          Node& n = m_nodes[idx];
          if (!n.isInput) return;
          QString s = n.outVals.isEmpty() ? QString(n.bitWidth, '0') : n.outVals[0];
          if (s.length() != n.bitWidth) s = QString(n.bitWidth, '0');
          int pos = bit;
          if (pos < 0 || pos >= s.length()) return;
          s[pos] = value ? '1' : '0';
          if (n.outVals.isEmpty()) n.outVals.append(s); else n.outVals[0] = s;
          if (n.inVals.isEmpty())  n.inVals.append(s);  else n.inVals[0]  = s;
      
          {
              QVariantList bits;
              bits.reserve(s.length());
              for (int i = 0; i < s.length(); ++i) bits.append(s[i] == '1');
              n.raw["inputBits"] = bits;
          }
          markDirty(n.qid);
      
          quint64 at = m_time + m_opts.wireDelay;
          auto it = m_outDests.constFind(pkey(idx, 0));
          if (it != m_outDests.constEnd()) {
              for (const auto& d : it.value())
                  scheduleWirePropagation(idx, 0, d.first, d.second, s, at);
          }
      }
      
      void EventEngine::triggerInputString(const QString& compId, const QString& binStr) {
          int idx = idxOf(compId);
          if (idx < 0) return;
          Node& n = m_nodes[idx];
          if (!n.isInput) return;
          QString s = binStr;
          while (s.length() < n.bitWidth) s = "0" + s;
          if (s.length() > n.bitWidth) s = s.right(n.bitWidth);
          if (n.outVals.isEmpty()) n.outVals.append(s); else n.outVals[0] = s;
          if (n.inVals.isEmpty())  n.inVals.append(s);  else n.inVals[0]  = s;
      
          {
              QVariantList bits;
              bits.reserve(s.length());
              for (int i = 0; i < s.length(); ++i) bits.append(s[i] == '1');
              n.raw["inputBits"] = bits;
          }
          markDirty(n.qid);
      
          quint64 at = m_time + m_opts.wireDelay;
          auto it = m_outDests.constFind(pkey(idx, 0));
          if (it != m_outDests.constEnd()) {
              for (const auto& d : it.value())
                  scheduleWirePropagation(idx, 0, d.first, d.second, s, at);
          }
      }
      
      void EventEngine::triggerClockToggle(const QString& compId) {
          int idx = idxOf(compId);
          if (idx < 0) return;
          Node& n = m_nodes[idx];
          if (!n.isClock) return;
      
          QString s;
          if (!n.outVals.isEmpty() && n.outVals[0].length() == n.bitWidth)
              s = n.outVals[0];
          else
              s = QString(n.bitWidth, '0');
      
          for (int i = 0; i < s.length(); ++i)
              s[i] = (s[i] == '1') ? '0' : '1';
      
          if (n.outVals.isEmpty()) n.outVals.append(s); else n.outVals[0] = s;
          if (n.inVals.isEmpty())  n.inVals.append(s);  else n.inVals[0]  = s;
      
          {
              QVariantList ops;
              ops.append(s);
              n.raw["outputPorts"] = ops;
          }
          markDirty(n.qid);
      
          quint64 at = m_time + m_opts.wireDelay;
          auto it = m_outDests.constFind(pkey(idx, 0));
          if (it != m_outDests.constEnd()) {
              for (const auto& d : it.value())
                  scheduleWirePropagation(idx, 0, d.first, d.second, s, at);
          }
      }
      
      void EventEngine::triggerForceOutput(const QString& compId, int port, const QString& value) {
          int idx = idxOf(compId);
          if (idx < 0) return;
          Node& n = m_nodes[idx];
          if (port < 0 || port >= n.outVals.size()) return;
      
          if (n.isClock && port == 0) {
              QVariantList ops;
              ops.append(value);
              n.raw["outputPorts"] = ops;
          }
      
          if (n.outVals[port] == value) return;
          n.outVals[port] = value;
          markDirty(n.qid);
      
          quint64 at = m_time + m_opts.wireDelay;
          auto it = m_outDests.constFind(pkey(idx, port));
          if (it != m_outDests.constEnd()) {
              for (const auto& d : it.value())
                  scheduleWirePropagation(idx, port, d.first, d.second, value, at);
          }
      }
      
      QString EventEngine::getOutput(const QString& compId, int port) const {
          int idx = idxOf(compId);
          if (idx < 0) return QString();
          const Node& n = m_nodes[idx];
          if (port < 0 || port >= n.outVals.size()) return QString();
          return n.outVals[port];
      }
      
      QStringList EventEngine::getOutputs(const QString& compId) const {
          int idx = idxOf(compId);
          if (idx < 0) return QStringList();
          const Node& n = m_nodes[idx];
          QStringList out;
          out.reserve(n.outVals.size());
          for (const auto& s : n.outVals) out << s;
          return out;
      }
      
      QStringList EventEngine::getInputs(const QString& compId) const {
          int idx = idxOf(compId);
          if (idx < 0) return QStringList();
          const Node& n = m_nodes[idx];
          QStringList out;
          out.reserve(n.inVals.size());
          for (const auto& s : n.inVals) out << s;
          return out;
      }

├── IterEngine.cpp
│   内容:
│     #include "IterEngine.h"
│     #include "../ComponentLogic.h"
│     #include "../WireNetworkSolver.h"
│     #include "../../core/ValueCodec.h"
│     #include <QVector>
│     #include <QDebug>
│     
│     static bool evalRecursive(CircuitContext& ctx,
│                               const QHash<QString, CircuitContext>& subContexts,
│                               int depth);
│     
│     static QString resolveMultiDriverE(const QVector<QString>& vals, int bw) {
│         if (bw < 1) bw = 1;
│         if (vals.isEmpty()) return QString(bw, '0');
│         if (vals.size() == 1) {
│             QString s = vals[0];
│             if (s.length() < bw) s = QString(bw - s.length(), '0') + s;
│             else if (s.length() > bw) s = s.right(bw);
│             return s;
│         }
│         QString result(bw, '0');
│         for (int pos = 0; pos < bw; ++pos) {
│             bool has0 = false, has1 = false, hasE = false;
│             for (const auto& v : vals) {
│                 QString s = v;
│                 if (s.length() < bw) s = QString(bw - s.length(), '0') + s;
│                 else if (s.length() > bw) s = s.right(bw);
│                 QChar c = (pos < s.length()) ? s[pos] : QChar('0');
│                 if (c == '0') has0 = true;
│                 else if (c == '1') has1 = true;
│                 else if (c == 'E') hasE = true;
│             }
│             if (hasE) result[pos] = 'E';
│             else if (has0 && has1) result[pos] = 'E';
│             else if (has1) result[pos] = '1';
│             else if (has0) result[pos] = '0';
│             else result[pos] = 'Z';
│         }
│         return result;
│     }
│     
│     bool IterEngine::evalOne(QVariantMap& c,
│                              const QHash<QString, QVector<QPair<int,int>>>& inputMap,
│                              const QVector<QVariantMap>& comps) {
│         const QString id = c.value("id").toString();
│         const QString t = c.value("type").toString();
│         if (t == "text") return false;
│     
│         int bw = c.value("bitWidth").toInt();
│         if (bw < 1) bw = 1;
│         if (bw > 64) bw = 64;
│     
│         const int ic = ComponentLogic::inputCount(c);
│         const QVariantList oldInList = c.value("inputPortValues").toList();
│         const QString idPrefix = id + ':';
│     
│         QVariantList newInList;
│         newInList.reserve(ic);
│         bool inputChanged = false;
│     
│         for (int p = 0; p < ic; ++p) {
│             int bwp = ComponentLogic::inputBitWidth(c, p);
│             if (bwp < 1) bwp = 1;
│             if (bwp > 64) bwp = 64;
│     
│             QString bits;
│             auto it = inputMap.constFind(idPrefix + QString::number(p));
│             if (it != inputMap.constEnd()) {
│                 QVector<QString> driverVals;
│                 for (const auto& pair : it.value()) {
│                     int fi = pair.first, fp = pair.second;
│                     if (fi >= 0 && fi < comps.size()) {
│                         const QVariantList fo = comps[fi].value("outputPorts").toList();
│                         if (fp >= 0 && fp < fo.size()) {
│                             QString src = fo[fp].toString();
│                             if (src.length() != bwp) src = QString(bwp, 'E');
│                             driverVals.append(src);
│                         }
│                     }
│                 }
│                 if (!driverVals.isEmpty()) bits = resolveMultiDriverE(driverVals, bwp);
│             }
│             if (bits.isEmpty()) {
│                 if (p < oldInList.size()) bits = oldInList[p].toString();
│                 if (bits.isEmpty()) bits = QString(bwp, '0');
│             }
│             if (p >= oldInList.size() || oldInList[p].toString() != bits) inputChanged = true;
│             newInList.append(bits);
│         }
│     
│         bool changed = false;
│         if (inputChanged) {
│             c["inputPortValues"] = newInList;
│             changed = true;
│         }
│     
│         QVariantList newOutList;
│         if (t == "input" || t == "const") {
│             QVariant ov = c.value("outputOverride");
│             QString ovStr = ov.isValid() ? ov.toString() : QString();
│             if (!ovStr.isEmpty()) newOutList.append(ovStr);
│             else newOutList.append(ValueCodec::bitsToString(c.value("inputBits").toList(), bw));
│         } else if (t == "clock") {
│             newOutList = c.value("outputPorts").toList();
│             if (newOutList.isEmpty()) newOutList.append(QString(bw, '0'));
│         } else if (t == "led" || t == "output") {
│             if (!newInList.isEmpty()) newOutList.append(newInList[0]);
│             else newOutList.append(QString(bw, '0'));
│         } else if (t == "sub") {
│             newOutList = c.value("outputPorts").toList();
│         } else {
│             QVector<QString> ins;
│             for (const auto& v : newInList) ins.append(v.toString());
│             QVector<QString> outs = ComponentLogic::computeCombinationalOutputs(
│                 t, ins, bw, c.value("outputSplits").toList());
│             for (const auto& s : outs) newOutList.append(s);
│         }
│         const QVariantList oldOutList = c.value("outputPorts").toList();
│         if (oldOutList != newOutList) {
│             c["outputPorts"] = newOutList;
│             changed = true;
│         }
│         return changed;
│     }
│     
│     bool IterEngine::evaluateContext(CircuitContext& ctx) {
│         const int n = ctx.components.size();
│         if (n == 0) return false;
│     
│         QHash<QString, int> idToIdx;
│         idToIdx.reserve(n * 2);
│         for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i)
│             idToIdx.insert(ctx.components[i].toMap().value("id").toString(), i);
│     
│         // 关键：先生成虚拟 wire，注入到临时 wire 列表
│         QVariantList virtualWires = WireNetworkSolver::generateVirtualWires(
│             ctx.components, ctx.wires);
│         QVariantList allWires = ctx.wires;
│         for (const auto& vw : virtualWires) allWires.append(vw);
│     
│         QVector<QVector<int>> succ(n);
│         QVector<int> inDeg(n, 0);
│         QHash<QString, QVector<QPair<int,int>>> inputMap;
│         inputMap.reserve(allWires.size() * 2);
│     
│         for (const auto& wv : allWires) {
│             QVariantMap w = wv.toMap();
│             int si = idToIdx.value(w.value("fromComp").toString(), -1);
│             int di = idToIdx.value(w.value("toComp").toString(), -1);
│             if (si < 0 || di < 0) continue;
│             succ[si].append(di);
│             inDeg[di]++;
│             QString key = w.value("toComp").toString() + ':' +
│                           QString::number(w.value("toPort").toInt());
│             inputMap[key].append({si, w.value("fromPort").toInt()});
│         }
│     
│         QVector<QVariantMap> comps(n);
│         for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) comps[i] = ctx.components[i].toMap();
│     
│         QVector<int> order; order.reserve(n);
│         QVector<int> queue; queue.reserve(n);
│         for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) if (inDeg[i] == 0) queue.append(i);
│         int qi = 0;
│         while (qi < queue.size()) {
│             int u = queue[qi++];
│             order.append(u);
│             for (int v : succ[u]) if (--inDeg[v] == 0) queue.append(v);
│         }
│     
│         const bool hasCycle = (order.size() < n);
│         bool anyChanged = false;
│         QVector<bool> changed(n, false);
│     
│         for (int idx : order) {
│             if (evalOne(comps[idx], inputMap, comps)) {
│                 anyChanged = true;
│                 changed[idx] = true;
│             }
│         }
│     
│         if (hasCycle) {
│             QVector<int> cycNodes;
│             for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) if (inDeg[i] > 0) cycNodes.append(i);
│             for (int iter = 0; iter < 200; ++iter) {
│                 bool iterChanged = false;
│                 for (int idx : cycNodes) {
│                     if (evalOne(comps[idx], inputMap, comps)) {
│                         iterChanged = true;
│                         changed[idx] = true;
│                     }
│                 }
│                 if (iterChanged) anyChanged = true;
│                 else break;
│             }
│         }
│     
│         if (anyChanged) {
│             for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i)
│                 if (changed[i]) ctx.components[i] = comps[i];
│         }
│         return anyChanged;
│     }
│     
│     void IterEngine::syncSubPins(CircuitContext& ctx,
│                                  const QHash<QString, CircuitContext>& subContexts) {
│         for (int i = 0; i < ctx.components.size(); ++i) {
│             QVariantMap c = ctx.components[i].toMap();
│             if (c.value("type").toString() != "sub") continue;
│             QString subId = c.value("subId").toString();
│             auto sit = subContexts.find(subId);
│             if (sit == subContexts.end()) continue;
│     
│             QVariantList subInputNames, subOutputNames, subInputWidths, subOutputWidths;
│             for (const auto& scv : sit.value().components) {
│                 auto sc = scv.toMap();
│                 QString st = sc.value("type").toString();
│                 QString sn = sc.value("name").toString();
│                 int sw = sc.value("bitWidth").toInt();
│                 if (sw < 1) sw = 1;
│                 if (st == "input") {
│                     subInputNames.append(sn.isEmpty() ? sc.value("id").toString() : sn);
│                     subInputWidths.append(sw);
│                 } else if (st == "output") {
│                     subOutputNames.append(sn.isEmpty() ? sc.value("id").toString() : sn);
│                     subOutputWidths.append(sw);
│                 }
│             }
│             bool changed = false;
│             if (c.value("subInputNames").toList() != subInputNames) { c["subInputNames"] = subInputNames; changed = true; }
│             if (c.value("subOutputNames").toList() != subOutputNames) { c["subOutputNames"] = subOutputNames; changed = true; }
│             if (c.value("subInputWidths").toList() != subInputWidths) { c["subInputWidths"] = subInputWidths; changed = true; }
│             if (c.value("subOutputWidths").toList() != subOutputWidths) { c["subOutputWidths"] = subOutputWidths; changed = true; }
│     
│             QVariantList inVals = c.value("inputPortValues").toList();
│             if (inVals.size() != subInputNames.size()) {
│                 QVariantList newIn;
│                 for (int k = 0; k < subInputNames.size(); ++k) {
│                     if (k < inVals.size()) newIn.append(inVals[k]);
│                     else {
│                         int w = subInputWidths.value(k).toInt(); if (w < 1) w = 1;
│                         newIn.append(QString(w, '0'));
│                     }
│                 }
│                 c["inputPortValues"] = newIn;
│                 changed = true;
│             }
│             QVariantList outVals = c.value("outputPorts").toList();
│             if (outVals.size() != subOutputNames.size()) {
│                 QVariantList newOut;
│                 for (int k = 0; k < subOutputNames.size(); ++k) {
│                     if (k < outVals.size()) newOut.append(outVals[k]);
│                     else {
│                         int w = subOutputWidths.value(k).toInt(); if (w < 1) w = 1;
│                         newOut.append(QString(w, '0'));
│                     }
│                 }
│                 c["outputPorts"] = newOut;
│                 changed = true;
│             }
│             if (changed) ctx.components[i] = c;
│         }
│     }
│     
│     static bool evalInstance(QVariantMap& subInst,
│                              const QHash<QString, CircuitContext>& subContexts,
│                              int depth) {
│         if (depth > 32) return false;
│         const QString subId = subInst.value("subId").toString();
│         auto sit = subContexts.constFind(subId);
│         if (sit == subContexts.constEnd()) return false;
│         CircuitContext instCtx = sit.value();
│         const QVariantList inVals = subInst.value("inputPortValues").toList();
│         int idx = 0;
│         for (int j = 0; j < instCtx.components.size(); ++j) {
│             QVariantMap sc = instCtx.components[j].toMap();
│             if (sc.value("type").toString() != "input") continue;
│             if (idx >= inVals.size()) break;
│             QString s = inVals[idx].toString();
│             int sw = sc.value("bitWidth").toInt(); if (sw < 1) sw = 1;
│             if (s.length() != sw) s = QString(sw, '0');
│             QVariantList bits;
│             for (int k = 0; k < s.length(); ++k) bits.append(s[k] == '1');
│             sc["inputBits"] = bits;
│             if (s.contains('Z') || s.contains('E')) sc["outputOverride"] = s;
│             else sc["outputOverride"] = QVariant();
│             instCtx.components[j] = sc;
│             ++idx;
│         }
│         evalRecursive(instCtx, subContexts, depth + 1);
│         QVariantList newOut;
│         for (int j = 0; j < instCtx.components.size(); ++j) {
│             QVariantMap sc = instCtx.components[j].toMap();
│             if (sc.value("type").toString() != "output") continue;
│             QVariantList inv = sc.value("inputPortValues").toList();
│             QString v = inv.isEmpty() ? QString() : inv[0].toString();
│             int sw = sc.value("bitWidth").toInt(); if (sw < 1) sw = 1;
│             if (v.length() != sw) v = QString(sw, '0');
│             newOut.append(v);
│         }
│         if (subInst.value("outputPorts").toList() != newOut) {
│             subInst["outputPorts"] = newOut;
│             return true;
│         }
│         return false;
│     }
│     
│     static bool evalRecursive(CircuitContext& ctx,
│                               const QHash<QString, CircuitContext>& subContexts,
│                               int depth) {
│         if (depth > 32) return false;
│         bool anyChanged = false;
│         for (int iter = 0; iter < 8; ++iter) {
│             bool iterChanged = false;
│             if (IterEngine::evaluateContext(ctx)) iterChanged = true;
│             for (int i = 0; i < ctx.components.size(); ++i) {
│                 QVariantMap c = ctx.components[i].toMap();
│                 if (c.value("type").toString() != "sub") continue;
│                 if (evalInstance(c, subContexts, depth)) {
│                     ctx.components[i] = c;
│                     iterChanged = true;
│                 }
│             }
│             if (iterChanged) {
│                 if (IterEngine::evaluateContext(ctx)) iterChanged = true;
│             }
│             if (iterChanged) anyChanged = true;
│             else break;
│         }
│         return anyChanged;
│     }
│     
│     void IterEngine::evaluateAll(CircuitContext& root,
│                                  QHash<QString, CircuitContext>& subContexts) {
│         syncSubPins(root, subContexts);
│         for (auto it = subContexts.begin(); it != subContexts.end(); ++it)
│             syncSubPins(it.value(), subContexts);
│         if (root.components.isEmpty()) return;
│         for (int round = 0; round < 50; ++round) {
│             if (!evalRecursive(root, subContexts, 0)) break;
│         }
│     }
│     
│     bool IterEngine::evaluateStandalone(
│         CircuitContext& ctx,
│         const QHash<QString, CircuitContext>& subContexts)
│     {
│         return evalRecursive(ctx, subContexts, 0);
│     }

└── IterEngine.h
    内容:
      #ifndef ITERENGINE_H
      #define ITERENGINE_H
      
      #include <QVariantMap>
      #include <QVariantList>
      #include <QHash>
      #include <QString>
      #include <QVector>
      #include <QPair>
      #include "../../core/CircuitContext.h"
      
      /**
       * @brief 电路求值引擎（旧引擎，迭代到收敛）。
       *
       * 支持多驱：一个输入端口可以接多个输出，
       * 合并时如果存在 0/1 冲突则输出 E。
       */
      class IterEngine {
      public:
          static bool evalOne(QVariantMap& c,
                              const QHash<QString, QVector<QPair<int,int>>>& inputMap,
                              const QVector<QVariantMap>& comps);
      
          static bool evaluateContext(CircuitContext& ctx);
      
          static void syncSubPins(CircuitContext& ctx,
                                  const QHash<QString, CircuitContext>& subContexts);
      
          static void evaluateAll(CircuitContext& root,
                                  QHash<QString, CircuitContext>& subContexts);
      
          static bool evaluateStandalone(CircuitContext& ctx,
                                         const QHash<QString, CircuitContext>& subContexts);
      };
      
      #endif // ITERENGINE_H

├── AvoidanceMap.cpp
│   内容:
│     #include "AvoidanceMap.h"
│     #include "Connector.h"
│     #include <cmath>
│     
│     const QString AvoidanceMap::ALLOW_NEITHER    = "neither";
│     const QString AvoidanceMap::ALLOW_VERTICAL   = "vert";
│     const QString AvoidanceMap::ALLOW_HORIZONTAL = "horz";
│     
│     AvoidanceMap* AvoidanceMap::create(const QList<QVariantMap>& elements, int dx, int dy) {
│         AvoidanceMap* m = new AvoidanceMap();
│         m->markAll(elements, dx, dy);
│         return m;
│     }
│     
│     AvoidanceMap* AvoidanceMap::cloneMap() const {
│         AvoidanceMap* m = new AvoidanceMap();
│         m->m_map = m_map;
│         return m;
│     }
│     
│     QString AvoidanceMap::get(double x, double y) const {
│         return m_map.value(keyOf(x, y), QString());
│     }
│     
│     void AvoidanceMap::markAll(const QList<QVariantMap>& elements, int dx, int dy) {
│         for (const auto& e : elements) {
│             QString wt = e.value("wireType").toString();
│             if (wt == "ortho" || wt == "bezier" || wt == "virtual") {
│                 markWire(e, dx, dy);
│             } else {
│                 markComponent(e, dx, dy);
│             }
│         }
│     }
│     
│     void AvoidanceMap::markComponent(const QVariantMap& comp, int dx, int dy) {
│         QString type = comp.value("type").toString();
│         if (type == "text") return;
│         double cx = comp.value("x").toDouble() + dx;
│         double cy = comp.value("y").toDouble() + dy;
│     
│         double w = 80, h = 60;
│         if (type == "input" || type == "output" || type == "const" || type == "led") {
│             int bw = comp.value("bitWidth").toInt();
│             if (bw < 1) bw = 1;
│             w = qMax(type == "led" ? 50.0 : (type == "output" ? 60.0 : 60.0), bw * 22.0 + 8.0);
│             h = 40;
│         } else if (type == "sub") {
│             w = 110;
│             int ic = comp.value("subInputNames").toList().size();
│             int oc = comp.value("subOutputNames").toList().size();
│             h = qMax(60.0, (double)qMax(ic, oc) * 24.0);
│         } else if (type == "splitter" || type == "hub") {
│             int n = comp.value("outputSplits").toList().size();
│             if (n < 1) n = 1;
│             w = 80; h = qMax(60.0, (double)n * 24.0);
│         } else if (type == "clock") {
│             w = 60; h = 40;
│         }
│     
│         double x0 = cx - w / 2.0;
│         double y0 = cy - h / 2.0;
│         double x1 = cx + w / 2.0;
│         double y1 = cy + h / 2.0;
│         x0 += 9 - std::fmod(x0 + 9, 10);
│         y0 += 9 - std::fmod(y0 + 9, 10);
│         for (double x = x0; x <= x1; x += 10) {
│             for (double y = y0; y <= y1; y += 10) {
│                 QString k = keyOf(x, y);
│                 QString prev = m_map.value(k);
│                 m_map[k] = ALLOW_NEITHER;
│                 if (prev != ALLOW_NEITHER) {
│                     // 若该点不在组件包围盒内，恢复
│                     double baseX = x - dx;
│                     double baseY = y - dy;
│                     if (baseX < cx - w / 2.0 || baseX > cx + w / 2.0 + dx * 0 ||
│                         baseY < cy - h / 2.0 || baseY > cy + h / 2.0 + dy * 0) {
│                         if (prev.isEmpty()) m_map.remove(k);
│                         else m_map[k] = prev;
│                     }
│                 }
│             }
│         }
│     }
│     
│     void AvoidanceMap::markWire(const QVariantMap& wire, int dx, int dy) {
│         QString wt = wire.value("wireType").toString();
│         if (wt == "ortho") {
│             QVariantList path = wire.value("path").toList();
│             for (int i = 0; i + 1 < path.size(); ++i) {
│                 QVariantMap p0 = path[i].toMap();
│                 QVariantMap p1 = path[i + 1].toMap();
│                 double x0 = p0.value("x").toDouble() + dx;
│                 double y0 = p0.value("y").toDouble() + dy;
│                 double x1 = p1.value("x").toDouble() + dx;
│                 double y1 = p1.value("y").toDouble() + dy;
│                 m_map[keyOf(x0, y0)] = ALLOW_NEITHER;
│                 m_map[keyOf(x1, y1)] = ALLOW_NEITHER;
│                 double len = std::hypot(x1 - x0, y1 - y0);
│                 int n = int(len / 10);
│                 if (n < 1) n = 1;    // ★ 除零保护
│                 if (x0 == x1) {
│                     for (int s = 0; s <= n; ++s) {
│                         double yy = y0 + (y1 - y0) * s / n;
│                         QString k = keyOf(x0, yy);
│                         QString prev = m_map.value(k);
│                         m_map[k] = ALLOW_HORIZONTAL;
│                         if (prev == ALLOW_NEITHER || prev == ALLOW_VERTICAL)
│                             m_map[k] = ALLOW_NEITHER;
│                     }
│                 } else if (y0 == y1) {
│                     for (int s = 0; s <= n; ++s) {
│                         double xx = x0 + (x1 - x0) * s / n;
│                         QString k = keyOf(xx, y0);
│                         QString prev = m_map.value(k);
│                         m_map[k] = ALLOW_VERTICAL;
│                         if (prev == ALLOW_NEITHER || prev == ALLOW_HORIZONTAL)
│                             m_map[k] = ALLOW_NEITHER;
│                     }
│                 }
│             }
│         } else if (wt == "bezier" || wt.isEmpty()) {
│             // 贝塞尔：端点不占网格
│         }
│     }
│     
│     void AvoidanceMap::unmarkLocation(double x, double y) {
│         m_map.remove(keyOf(x, y));
│     }
│     
│     void AvoidanceMap::unmarkWire(const QVariantMap& wire, const QVariantMap& deletedEnd) {
│         QString wt = wire.value("wireType").toString();
│         if (wt != "ortho") return;
│         QVariantList path = wire.value("path").toList();
│         for (int i = 0; i + 1 < path.size(); ++i) {
│             QVariantMap p0 = path[i].toMap();
│             QVariantMap p1 = path[i + 1].toMap();
│             double x0 = p0.value("x").toDouble();
│             double y0 = p0.value("y").toDouble();
│             double x1 = p1.value("x").toDouble();
│             double y1 = p1.value("y").toDouble();
│             double len = std::hypot(x1 - x0, y1 - y0);
│             int n = int(len / 10);
│             if (n < 1) n = 1;    // ★ 除零保护
│             if (x0 == x1) {
│                 for (int s = 0; s <= n; ++s) {
│                     double yy = y0 + (y1 - y0) * s / n;
│                     QString k = keyOf(x0, yy);
│                     QString prev = m_map.value(k);
│                     m_map.remove(k);
│                     if (prev == ALLOW_HORIZONTAL) m_map[k] = ALLOW_VERTICAL;
│                 }
│             } else if (y0 == y1) {
│                 for (int s = 0; s <= n; ++s) {
│                     double xx = x0 + (x1 - x0) * s / n;
│                     QString k = keyOf(xx, y0);
│                     QString prev = m_map.value(k);
│                     m_map.remove(k);
│                     if (prev != ALLOW_VERTICAL) m_map[k] = ALLOW_HORIZONTAL;
│                 }
│             }
│         }
│     }

├── AvoidanceMap.h

│     #include <QList>
│     #include <QHash>
│     #include <QString>

│     class AvoidanceMap {
│     public:
│         static const QString ALLOW_NEITHER;
│         static const QString ALLOW_VERTICAL;
│         static const QString ALLOW_HORIZONTAL;
│     
│         static AvoidanceMap* create(const QList<QVariantMap>& elements, int dx, int dy);
│         AvoidanceMap* cloneMap() const;
│     
│         QString get(double x, double y) const;
│         void markAll(const QList<QVariantMap>& elements, int dx, int dy);
│         void markComponent(const QVariantMap& comp, int dx, int dy);
│         void markWire(const QVariantMap& wire, int dx, int dy);
│         void unmarkLocation(double x, double y);
│         void unmarkWire(const QVariantMap& wire, const QVariantMap& deletedEnd);
│     
│     private:
│         AvoidanceMap() {}
│         QHash<QString, QString> m_map;
│     
│         static QString keyOf(double x, double y) {
│             return QString("%1,%2").arg(int(std::round(x))).arg(int(std::round(y)));
│         }
│     };

│             + qHash(m_loc.value("y").toDouble()) * 31

│         }

├── Connector.cpp
│   内容:
│     #include "Connector.h"
│     #include "MoveGesture.h"
│     #include "AvoidanceMap.h"
│     #include "SearchNode.h"
│     #include "ConnectionData.h"
│     #include "ConnectorThread.h"
│     #include "../../core/CircuitContext.h"

│     static QString dirReverse(const QString& d) {
│         if (d == "east") return "west";
│         if (d == "west") return "east";
│         if (d == "north") return "south";
│         if (d == "south") return "north";
│         return QString();
│     }
│     
│     static QString dirLeft(const QString& d) {
│         if (d == "east") return "north";
│         if (d == "west") return "south";
│         if (d == "north") return "west";
│         if (d == "south") return "east";
│         return QString();
│     }
│     
│     static QString dirRight(const QString& d) {
│         if (d == "east") return "south";
│         if (d == "west") return "north";
│         if (d == "north") return "east";
│         if (d == "south") return "west";
│         return QString();
│     }
│     
│     // 沿 wire 生成所有网格点（用于 processConnection 的起点）
│     static QList<QVariantMap> wireLocations(const QVariantMap& wire) {
│         QList<QVariantMap> out;
│         QVariantList path = wire.value("path").toList();
│         for (int i = 0; i + 1 < path.size(); ++i) {
│             QVariantMap p0 = path[i].toMap();
│             QVariantMap p1 = path[i + 1].toMap();
│             double x0 = p0.value("x").toDouble(), y0 = p0.value("y").toDouble();
│             double x1 = p1.value("x").toDouble(), y1 = p1.value("y").toDouble();
│             double len = std::hypot(x1 - x0, y1 - y0);
│             int n = int(len / 10);
│             if (n < 1) n = 1;
│             for (int s = 0; s <= n; ++s) {
│                 double t = double(s) / n;
│                 QVariantMap loc;
│                 loc["x"] = x0 + (x1 - x0) * t;
│                 loc["y"] = y0 + (y1 - y0) * t;
│                 out.append(loc);
│             }
│         }
│         return out;
│     }
│     
│     static void processConnection(const ConnectionData& conn, int dx, int dy,
│                                   QList<QVariantMap>& connLocs,
│                                   QList<SearchNode*>& connNodes,
│                                   AvoidanceMap* selAvoid,
│                                   QList<SearchNode*>& allNodes)
│     {
│         QVariantMap cur = conn.location();
│         QVariantMap dest;
│         dest["x"] = cur.value("x").toDouble() + dx;
│         dest["y"] = cur.value("y").toDouble() + dy;
│     
│         if (selAvoid->get(cur.value("x").toDouble(), cur.value("y").toDouble()).isEmpty()) {
│             QString preferred = conn.direction();
│             if (preferred.isEmpty()) {
│                 if (std::abs(dx) > std::abs(dy))
│                     preferred = dx > 0 ? "east" : "west";
│                 else
│                     preferred = dy > 0 ? "south" : "north";
│             }
│             connLocs.append(cur);
│             SearchNode* n = new SearchNode(nullptr, cur, preferred, dest);
│             connNodes.append(n);
│             allNodes.append(n);
│         }
│     
│         for (const auto& wire : conn.wirePath()) {
│             QList<QVariantMap> locs = wireLocations(wire);
│             for (const auto& loc : locs) {
│                 QString allow = selAvoid->get(loc.value("x").toDouble(), loc.value("y").toDouble());
│                 bool isDest = std::abs(loc.value("x").toDouble() - dest.value("x").toDouble()) < 0.01
│                               && std::abs(loc.value("y").toDouble() - dest.value("y").toDouble()) < 0.01;
│                 if (allow.isEmpty() || isDest) {
│                     bool already = false;
│                     for (const auto& l : connLocs) {
│                         if (l == loc) { already = true; break; }
│                     }
│                     if (!already) {
│                         connLocs.append(loc);
│                         QString dir;
│                         QVariantList path = wire.value("path").toList();
│                         if (!path.isEmpty()) {
│                             QVariantMap p0 = path.first().toMap();
│                             QVariantMap p1 = path.last().toMap();
│                             bool isEnd = (std::abs(loc.value("x").toDouble() - p0.value("x").toDouble()) < 0.01 &&
│                                           std::abs(loc.value("y").toDouble() - p0.value("y").toDouble()) < 0.01)
│                                          || (std::abs(loc.value("x").toDouble() - p1.value("x").toDouble()) < 0.01 &&
│                                              std::abs(loc.value("y").toDouble() - p1.value("y").toDouble()) < 0.01);
│                             if (isEnd) {
│                                 double wx = p1.value("x").toDouble() - p0.value("x").toDouble();
│                                 double wy = p1.value("y").toDouble() - p0.value("y").toDouble();
│                                 if (std::abs(wx) > std::abs(wy))
│                                     dir = wx > 0 ? "west" : "east";
│                                 else
│                                     dir = wy > 0 ? "north" : "south";
│                             }
│                         }
│                         SearchNode* n = new SearchNode(nullptr, loc, dir, dest);
│                         connNodes.append(n);
│                         allNodes.append(n);
│                     }
│                 }
│             }
│         }
│     }
│     
│     static SearchNode* findShortestPath(QList<SearchNode*>& nodes,
│                                         const QList<QVariantMap>& pathLocs,
│                                         AvoidanceMap* avoid)
│     {
│         const int MAX_ITERS = 20000;
│         QList<SearchNode*> q;
│         for (auto* n : nodes) q.append(n);
│         std::sort(q.begin(), q.end(),
│                   [](SearchNode* a, SearchNode* b) {
│                       return a->heuristicValue() < b->heuristicValue();
│                   });
│         QSet<int> visited;
│         int iters = 0;
│         while (!q.isEmpty() && iters < MAX_ITERS) {
│             iters++;
│             if (iters % 64 == 0 && ConnectorThread::isOverrideRequested()) return nullptr;
│             SearchNode* node = q.takeFirst();
│             if (!node) return nullptr;
│             if (node->isDestination()) return node;
│             int h = node->hash();
│             if (visited.contains(h)) continue;
│             visited.insert(h);
│     
│             QString dir = node->direction();
│             int neighbors = 3;
│             QVariantMap loc = node->location();
│             QString allowed = avoid->get(loc.value("x").toDouble(), loc.value("y").toDouble());
│     
│             bool isStart = node->isStart();
│             bool inPathLocs = false;
│             if (isStart) {
│                 for (const auto& pl : pathLocs) {
│                     if (pl == loc) { inPathLocs = true; break; }
│                 }
│             }
│             if (!allowed.isEmpty() && isStart && inPathLocs) allowed.clear();
│     
│             if (allowed == "neither") neighbors = 0;
│             else if (allowed == "vert") {
│                 if (dir.isEmpty()) { dir = "north"; neighbors = 2; }
│                 else if (dir == "north" || dir == "south") neighbors = 1;
│                 else neighbors = 0;
│             } else if (allowed == "horz") {
│                 if (dir.isEmpty()) { dir = "east"; neighbors = 2; }
│                 else if (dir == "east" || dir == "west") neighbors = 1;
│                 else neighbors = 0;
│             } else {
│                 if (dir.isEmpty()) { dir = "north"; neighbors = 4; }
│                 else neighbors = 3;
│             }
│     
│             for (int i = 0; i < neighbors; ++i) {
│                 QString oDir;
│                 if (i == 0) oDir = dir;
│                 else if (i == 1) oDir = (neighbors == 2) ? dirReverse(dir) : dirLeft(dir);
│                 else if (i == 2) oDir = dirRight(dir);
│                 else oDir = dirReverse(dir);
│     
│                 SearchNode* nextNode = node->next(oDir, !allowed.isEmpty());
│                 if (nextNode && !visited.contains(nextNode->hash())) {
│                     q.append(nextNode);
│                     std::sort(q.begin(), q.end(),
│                               [](SearchNode* a, SearchNode* b) {
│                                   return a->heuristicValue() < b->heuristicValue();
│                               });
│                 }
│             }
│         }
│         return nullptr;
│     }
│     
│     static QList<QVariantMap> convertToPath(SearchNode* last) {
│         QList<QVariantMap> ret;
│         SearchNode* next = last;
│         SearchNode* prev = last->previous();
│         ret.append(next->location());
│         while (prev != nullptr) {
│             if (prev->direction() != next->direction()) {
│                 ret.append(prev->location());
│             }
│             next = prev;
│             prev = prev->previous();
│         }
│         if (ret.last() != next->location()) {
│             ret.append(next->location());
│         }
│         std::reverse(ret.begin(), ret.end());
│         return ret;
│     }
│     
│     static void processPath(const QList<QVariantMap>& path, const ConnectionData& conn,
│                             AvoidanceMap* avoid, MoveResult* result)
│     {
│         int idx = 0;
│         QVariantMap loc0 = path[idx++];
│         QVariantMap connLoc = conn.location();
│     
│         if (loc0 != connLoc) {
│             QVariantMap pathLoc = conn.wirePathStart();
│             bool found = (loc0 == pathLoc);
│     
│             for (const auto& wire : conn.wirePath()) {
│                 QVariantList wpath = wire.value("path").toList();
│                 QVariantMap wireEnd = wpath.isEmpty() ? QVariantMap() : wpath.last().toMap();
│                 QVariantMap nextLoc = wireEnd;
│                 if (wpath.size() > 0) {
│                     QVariantMap f = wpath.first().toMap();
│                     QVariantMap l = wpath.last().toMap();
│                     if (pathLoc == f) nextLoc = l;
│                     else nextLoc = f;
│                 }
│     
│                 if (found) {
│                     result->removals.append(wire);
│                     avoid->unmarkWire(wire, nextLoc);
│                 } else {
│                     bool contains = false;
│                     for (const auto& pv : wpath) {
│                         if (pv.toMap() == loc0) { contains = true; break; }
│                     }
│                     if (contains) {
│                         found = true;
│                         if (loc0 != nextLoc) {
│                             avoid->unmarkWire(wire, nextLoc);
│                             QVariantMap shortened;
│                             shortened["wireType"] = "ortho";
│                             QVariantList newPath;
│                             newPath.append(pathLoc);
│                             newPath.append(loc0);
│                             shortened["path"] = newPath;
│                             QVariantMap oldW = wire;
│                             result->replacements.append({oldW, shortened});
│                             avoid->markWire(shortened, 0, 0);
│                         }
│                     }
│                 }
│                 pathLoc = nextLoc;
│             }
│         }
│     
│         while (idx < path.size()) {
│             QVariantMap loc1 = path[idx++];
│             QVariantMap newWire;
│             newWire["wireType"] = "ortho";
│             QVariantList newPath;
│             newPath.append(loc0);
│             newPath.append(loc1);
│             newWire["path"] = newPath;
│             result->additions.append(newWire);
│             avoid->markWire(newWire, 0, 0);
│             loc0 = loc1;
│         }
│     }
│     
│     static void sortConnects(QList<ConnectionData>& connects, int dx, int dy) {
│         std::sort(connects.begin(), connects.end(),
│                   [dx, dy](const ConnectionData& a, const ConnectionData& b) {
│                       QVariantMap l1 = a.location();
│                       QVariantMap l2 = b.location();
│                       double ax = l1.value("x").toDouble() - l2.value("x").toDouble();
│                       double ay = l1.value("y").toDouble() - l2.value("y").toDouble();
│                       return (ax * dx + ay * dy) < 0;
│                   });
│     }
│     
│     static MoveResult* tryList(const MoveRequest& req, MoveGesture* gesture,
│                                QList<ConnectionData>& connects, int dx, int dy,
│                                QHash<int, QList<QVariantMap>>& pathLocs,
│                                QHash<int, QList<SearchNode*>>& initNodes,
│                                qint64 stopTime)
│     {
│         AvoidanceMap* avoid = gesture->getFixedAvoidanceMap()->cloneMap();
│         avoid->markAll(gesture->getSelected(), dx, dy);
│     
│         MoveResult* result = new MoveResult();
│     
│         for (const auto& conn : connects) {
│             if (ConnectorThread::isOverrideRequested()) { delete avoid; delete result; return nullptr; }
│             if (QDateTime::currentMSecsSinceEpoch() > stopTime) {
│                 result->unsatisfied.append(conn);
│                 continue;
│             }
│             int h = conn.hash();
│             QList<SearchNode*>& connNodes = initNodes[h];
│             QList<QVariantMap>& connPathLocs = pathLocs[h];
│             SearchNode* node = findShortestPath(connNodes, connPathLocs, avoid);
│             if (node) {
│                 result->totalDistance += node->distance();
│                 QList<QVariantMap> path = convertToPath(node);
│                 processPath(path, conn, avoid, result);
│             } else if (ConnectorThread::isOverrideRequested()) {
│                 delete avoid; delete result; return nullptr;
│             } else {
│                 result->unsatisfied.append(conn);
│             }
│         }
│         delete avoid;
│         return result;
│     }
│     
│     MoveResult* Connector::computeWires(const MoveRequest& req) {
│         MoveGesture* gesture = req.gesture();
│         if (!gesture) return new MoveResult();
│     
│         int dx = req.dx(), dy = req.dy();
│         QList<ConnectionData> baseConnects = gesture->getConnections();
│         AvoidanceMap* fixedAvoid = gesture->getFixedAvoidanceMap();
│     
│         // 剔除不可能的连接
│         QList<ConnectionData> connects;
│         for (const auto& conn : baseConnects) {
│             QVariantMap dest;
│             dest["x"] = conn.location().value("x").toDouble() + dx;
│             dest["y"] = conn.location().value("y").toDouble() + dy;
│             QString avoidVal = fixedAvoid->get(dest.value("x").toDouble(), dest.value("y").toDouble());
│             if (!avoidVal.isEmpty()) {
│                 bool inPath = false;
│                 for (const auto& wire : conn.wirePath()) {
│                     QVariantList path = wire.value("path").toList();
│                     for (const auto& pv : path) {
│                         QVariantMap p = pv.toMap();
│                         if (p == dest) { inPath = true; break; }
│                     }
│                     if (inPath) break;
│                 }
│                 if (!inPath) continue;
│             }
│             connects.append(conn);
│         }
│     
│         AvoidanceMap* selAvoid = AvoidanceMap::create(gesture->getSelected(), dx, dy);
│         QHash<int, QList<QVariantMap>> pathLocs;
│         QHash<int, QList<SearchNode*>> initNodes;
│         QList<SearchNode*> allNodes;
│     
│         for (const auto& conn : connects) {
│             QList<QVariantMap> connLocs;
│             QList<SearchNode*> connNodes;
│             processConnection(conn, dx, dy, connLocs, connNodes, selAvoid, allNodes);
│             pathLocs[conn.hash()] = connLocs;
│             initNodes[conn.hash()] = connNodes;
│         }
│         delete selAvoid;
│     
│         int tries;
│         if (connects.size() == 0) tries = 0;
│         else if (connects.size() == 1) tries = 1;
│         else if (connects.size() == 2) tries = 2;
│         else if (connects.size() == 3) tries = 8;
│         else tries = 10;
│     
│         qint64 stopTime = QDateTime::currentMSecsSinceEpoch() + 10000;
│     
│         MoveResult* bestResult = nullptr;
│         for (int tryNum = 0; tryNum < tries; ++tryNum) {
│             if (ConnectorThread::isOverrideRequested()) {
│                 if (bestResult) delete bestResult;
│                 for (auto* n : allNodes) delete n;
│                 return nullptr;
│             }
│             if (QDateTime::currentMSecsSinceEpoch() > stopTime) break;
│     
│             QList<ConnectionData> copy = connects;
│             if (tryNum < 2) {
│                 sortConnects(copy, dx, dy);
│                 if (tryNum == 1) std::reverse(copy.begin(), copy.end());
│             } else {
│                 std::random_device rd;
│                 std::mt19937 g(rd());
│                 std::shuffle(copy.begin(), copy.end(), g);
│             }
│     
│             MoveResult* candidate = tryList(req, gesture, copy, dx, dy, pathLocs, initNodes, stopTime);
│             if (!candidate) {
│                 if (bestResult) delete bestResult;
│                 for (auto* n : allNodes) delete n;
│                 return nullptr;
│             }
│             if (!bestResult) bestResult = candidate;
│             else {
│                 if (candidate->unsatisfied.size() < bestResult->unsatisfied.size())
│                 { delete bestResult; bestResult = candidate; }
│                 else if (candidate->unsatisfied.size() == bestResult->unsatisfied.size() &&
│                          candidate->totalDistance < bestResult->totalDistance)
│                 { delete bestResult; bestResult = candidate; }
│                 else delete candidate;
│             }
│         }
│     
│         if (!bestResult) bestResult = new MoveResult();
│     
│         for (auto* n : allNodes) delete n;
│         return bestResult;
│     }

├── Connector.h

├── ConnectorThread.cpp
│   内容:
│     #include "ConnectorThread.h"
│     #include "Connector.h"
│     #include "MoveGesture.h"
│     #include <QMutexLocker>
│     
│     ConnectorThread* ConnectorThread::s_instance = nullptr;
│     
│     ConnectorThread* ConnectorThread::instance() {
│         if (!s_instance) {
│             s_instance = new ConnectorThread();
│             s_instance->start();
│         }
│         return s_instance;
│     }
│     
│     ConnectorThread::ConnectorThread() {
│         setObjectName("ConnectorThread");
│     }
│     
│     void ConnectorThread::enqueueRequest(const MoveRequest& req, bool priority) {
│         ConnectorThread* inst = instance();
│         QMutexLocker lk(&inst->m_lock);
│         if (priority) {
│             inst->m_queue.prepend(req);
│         } else {
│             inst->m_queue.append(req);
│         }
│         inst->m_hasPending = true;
│         inst->m_cond.wakeAll();
│     }
│     
│     bool ConnectorThread::isOverrideRequested() {
│         if (!s_instance) return false;
│         QMutexLocker lk(&s_instance->m_lock);
│         return s_instance->m_hasPending;
│     }
│     
│     void ConnectorThread::run() {
│         while (true) {
│             MoveRequest req(nullptr, 0, 0);
│             {
│                 QMutexLocker lk(&m_lock);
│                 while (m_queue.isEmpty()) {
│                     m_cond.wait(&m_lock);
│                 }
│                 req = m_queue.dequeue();
│                 m_hasPending = !m_queue.isEmpty();
│             }
│     
│             MoveResult* result = Connector::computeWires(req);
│             // 即使 result 为 nullptr（请求被覆盖/中止），也必须通知，
│             // 否则 MoveGesture::forceRequest 会永久等待 → UI 死锁
│             if (!result) result = new MoveResult();
│     
│             req.gesture()->notifyResult(req, result);
│         }
│     }

├── MoveGesture.cpp
│   内容:
│     #include "MoveGesture.h"
│     #include "AvoidanceMap.h"
│     #include "ConnectorThread.h"
│     #include "../ComponentLogic.h"
│     #include "../../core/CircuitContext.h"
│     #include <QSet>
│     #include <QPointF>
│     #include <cmath>
│     #include <QMutex>
│     #include <QMutexLocker>
│     
│     static QMutex g_gestureMutex;
│     
│     MoveGesture::MoveGesture(const CircuitContext* circuit,
│                              const QList<QVariantMap>& selectedComponents)
│     : m_circuit(circuit), m_selected(selectedComponents) {}
│     
│     MoveGesture::~MoveGesture() {
│         for (auto* r : m_cachedResults) delete r;
│         delete m_initAvoid;
│     }
│     
│     static bool isSelected(const QList<QVariantMap>& selected, const QString& id) {
│         for (const auto& s : selected)
│             if (s.value("id").toString() == id) return true;
│             return false;
│     }
│     
│     const QList<ConnectionData>& MoveGesture::getConnections() const {
│         if (!m_connectionsValid) {
│             const_cast<MoveGesture*>(this)->computeConnections();
│             m_connectionsValid = true;
│         }
│         return m_connections;
│     }
│     
│     void MoveGesture::computeConnections() {
│         m_connections.clear();
│         if (m_selected.isEmpty()) return;
│     
│         // 收集所有选中元件的端口位置
│         QList<QPointF> locs;
│         for (const auto& c : m_selected) {
│             double cx = c.value("x").toDouble();
│             double cy = c.value("y").toDouble();
│             QVariantList outs = ComponentLogic::outputPorts(c);
│             for (const auto& pv : outs) {
│                 QVariantMap p = pv.toMap();
│                 locs.append(QPointF(cx + p.value("x").toDouble(),
│                                     cy + p.value("y").toDouble()));
│             }
│             QVariantList ins = ComponentLogic::inputPorts(c);
│             for (const auto& pv : ins) {
│                 QVariantMap p = pv.toMap();
│                 locs.append(QPointF(cx + p.value("x").toDouble(),
│                                     cy + p.value("y").toDouble()));
│             }
│         }
│     
│         // 对每个位置，判断是否连接到非选中组件
│         for (const auto& loc : locs) {
│             bool found = false;
│             // 遍历所有"非选中组件"，检查其端口是否在 loc 上
│             for (const auto& cv : m_circuit->components) {
│                 QVariantMap c = cv.toMap();
│                 if (isSelected(m_selected, c.value("id").toString())) continue;
│                 double cx = c.value("x").toDouble();
│                 double cy = c.value("y").toDouble();
│                 QVariantList outs = ComponentLogic::outputPorts(c);
│                 for (const auto& pv : outs) {
│                     QVariantMap p = pv.toMap();
│                     if (qAbs(cx + p.value("x").toDouble() - loc.x()) < 0.5 &&
│                         qAbs(cy + p.value("y").toDouble() - loc.y()) < 0.5) {
│                         found = true; break;
│                         }
│                 }
│                 if (found) break;
│                 QVariantList ins = ComponentLogic::inputPorts(c);
│                 for (const auto& pv : ins) {
│                     QVariantMap p = pv.toMap();
│                     if (qAbs(cx + p.value("x").toDouble() - loc.x()) < 0.5 &&
│                         qAbs(cy + p.value("y").toDouble() - loc.y()) < 0.5) {
│                         found = true; break;
│                         }
│                 }
│                 if (found) break;
│             }
│             if (!found) continue;
│     
│             // 找 wirePath：从这个位置出发，沿非选中 wire 走
│             QList<QVariantMap> wirePath;
│             QPointF pathStart = loc;
│             QVariantMap lastOnPath;
│             QSet<QString> visited;
│             QPointF cur = loc;
│     
│             // 找第一条经过 cur 的、非选中的正交 wire
│             auto findWire = [&](const QPointF& pt, const QString& ignoreId) -> QVariantMap {
│                 for (const auto& wv : m_circuit->wires) {
│                     QVariantMap w = wv.toMap();
│                     QString id = w.value("id").toString();
│                     if (id == ignoreId) continue;
│                     if (visited.contains(id)) continue;
│                     QString wt = w.value("wireType").toString();
│                     if (wt != "ortho") continue;
│                     // 检查 wire 是否有 path 顶点在 pt 上
│                     QVariantList path = w.value("path").toList();
│                     for (const auto& pv : path) {
│                         QVariantMap p = pv.toMap();
│                         if (qAbs(p.value("x").toDouble() - pt.x()) < 0.5 &&
│                             qAbs(p.value("y").toDouble() - pt.y()) < 0.5) {
│                             return w;
│                             }
│                     }
│                 }
│                 return QVariantMap();
│             };
│     
│             QVariantMap first = findWire(cur, QString());
│             if (!first.isEmpty()) {
│                 visited.insert(first.value("id").toString());
│                 wirePath.append(first);
│                 lastOnPath = first;
│     
│                 // 沿 path 向外走
│                 QVariantList path = first.value("path").toList();
│                 // 找 cur 在 path 中的位置
│                 int curIdx = -1;
│                 for (int i = 0; i < path.size(); ++i) {
│                     QVariantMap p = path[i].toMap();
│                     if (qAbs(p.value("x").toDouble() - cur.x()) < 0.5 &&
│                         qAbs(p.value("y").toDouble() - cur.y()) < 0.5) {
│                         curIdx = i; break;
│                         }
│                 }
│                 if (curIdx >= 0) {
│                     if (curIdx == 0) {
│                         // 从 path.first 开始，向 path.last 走
│                         QVariantMap lastP = path.last().toMap();
│                         cur = QPointF(lastP.value("x").toDouble(), lastP.value("y").toDouble());
│                     } else if (curIdx == path.size() - 1) {
│                         QVariantMap firstP = path.first().toMap();
│                         cur = QPointF(firstP.value("x").toDouble(), firstP.value("y").toDouble());
│                     }
│                 }
│     
│                 // 继续向外找
│                 for (int iter = 0; iter < 100; ++iter) {
│                     QVariantMap next = findWire(cur, QString());
│                     if (next.isEmpty()) break;
│                     visited.insert(next.value("id").toString());
│                     wirePath.append(next);
│                     QVariantList p2 = next.value("path").toList();
│                     int idx2 = -1;
│                     for (int i = 0; i < p2.size(); ++i) {
│                         QVariantMap p = p2[i].toMap();
│                         if (qAbs(p.value("x").toDouble() - cur.x()) < 0.5 &&
│                             qAbs(p.value("y").toDouble() - cur.y()) < 0.5) {
│                             idx2 = i; break;
│                             }
│                     }
│                     if (idx2 < 0) break;
│                     if (idx2 == 0) {
│                         QVariantMap lp = p2.last().toMap();
│                         cur = QPointF(lp.value("x").toDouble(), lp.value("y").toDouble());
│                     } else if (idx2 == p2.size() - 1) {
│                         QVariantMap fp = p2.first().toMap();
│                         cur = QPointF(fp.value("x").toDouble(), fp.value("y").toDouble());
│                     } else break;
│                 }
│                 pathStart = cur;
│             }
│     
│             // 计算方向
│             QString dir;
│             if (!lastOnPath.isEmpty()) {
│                 QVariantList lp = lastOnPath.value("path").toList();
│                 if (lp.size() >= 2) {
│                     // 取 loc 附近的顶点与相邻点
│                     double dx = 0, dy = 0;
│                     QVariantMap a = lp.first().toMap();
│                     QVariantMap b = lp.last().toMap();
│                     dx = a.value("x").toDouble() - b.value("x").toDouble();
│                     dy = a.value("y").toDouble() - b.value("y").toDouble();
│                     if (std::abs(dx) > std::abs(dy))
│                         dir = dx > 0 ? "east" : "west";
│                     else
│                         dir = dy > 0 ? "south" : "north";
│                 }
│             }
│     
│             QVariantMap locMap;
│             locMap["x"] = loc.x();
│             locMap["y"] = loc.y();
│             QVariantMap startMap;
│             startMap["x"] = pathStart.x();
│             startMap["y"] = pathStart.y();
│     
│             m_connections.append(ConnectionData(locMap, dir, wirePath, startMap));
│         }
│     }
│     
│     AvoidanceMap* MoveGesture::getFixedAvoidanceMap() const {
│         if (m_initAvoid) return m_initAvoid;
│     
│         QList<QVariantMap> elements;
│         QSet<QString> selIds;
│         for (const auto& s : m_selected) selIds.insert(s.value("id").toString());
│     
│         // 所有非选中组件
│         for (const auto& cv : m_circuit->components) {
│             QVariantMap c = cv.toMap();
│             if (c.value("type").toString() == "text") continue;
│             if (selIds.contains(c.value("id").toString())) continue;
│             elements.append(c);
│         }
│         // 所有非选中 wire
│         for (const auto& wv : m_circuit->wires) {
│             QVariantMap w = wv.toMap();
│             QString from = w.value("fromComp").toString();
│             QString to = w.value("toComp").toString();
│             if (selIds.contains(from) || selIds.contains(to)) continue;
│             if (w.value("wireType").toString() != "ortho") continue;
│             elements.append(w);
│         }
│     
│         m_initAvoid = AvoidanceMap::create(elements, 0, 0);
│         return m_initAvoid;
│     }
│     
│     void MoveGesture::notifyResult(const MoveRequest& req, MoveResult* result) {
│         QMutexLocker lk(&g_gestureMutex);
│         m_cachedResults[req.hash()] = result;
│     }
│     
│     MoveResult* MoveGesture::findResult(int dx, int dy) {
│         QMutexLocker lk(&g_gestureMutex);
│         MoveRequest req(const_cast<MoveGesture*>(this), dx, dy);
│         return m_cachedResults.value(req.hash(), nullptr);
│     }
│     
│     bool MoveGesture::enqueueRequest(int dx, int dy) {
│         MoveRequest req(this, dx, dy);
│         {
│             QMutexLocker lk(&g_gestureMutex);
│             if (m_cachedResults.contains(req.hash())) return false;
│         }
│         ConnectorThread::instance()->enqueueRequest(req, false);
│         return true;
│     }
│     
│     MoveResult* MoveGesture::forceRequest(int dx, int dy) {
│         MoveRequest req(this, dx, dy);
│         ConnectorThread::instance()->enqueueRequest(req, true);
│         QMutexLocker lk(&g_gestureMutex);
│         while (!m_cachedResults.contains(req.hash())) {
│             lk.unlock();
│             QThread::msleep(5);
│             lk.relock();
│         }
│         return m_cachedResults.value(req.hash(), nullptr);
│     }
