├── SearchNode.cpp
│   内容:
│     #include "SearchNode.h"
│     #include "ConnectionData.h"
│     #include <cmath>
│     #include <QHash>
│     
│     SearchNode::SearchNode(ConnectionData* conn, const QVariantMap& src,
│                            const QString& srcDir, const QVariantMap& dst)
│     : m_loc(src), m_dir(srcDir), m_conn(conn), m_dest(dst),
│     m_dist(0), m_extendsWire(!srcDir.isEmpty()), m_prev(nullptr)
│     {
│         m_heur = m_dist + getHeuristic();
│     }
│     
│     SearchNode::SearchNode(const QVariantMap& loc, const QString& dir,
│                            ConnectionData* conn, const QVariantMap& dest,
│                            int dist, bool extendsWire, SearchNode* prev)
│     : m_loc(loc), m_dir(dir), m_conn(conn), m_dest(dest),
│     m_dist(dist), m_extendsWire(extendsWire), m_prev(prev)
│     {
│         m_heur = m_dist + getHeuristic();
│     }
│     
│     bool SearchNode::isDestination() const {
│         return std::abs(m_loc.value("x").toDouble() - m_dest.value("x").toDouble()) < 0.01
│         && std::abs(m_loc.value("y").toDouble() - m_dest.value("y").toDouble()) < 0.01;
│     }
│     
│     bool SearchNode::equals(const SearchNode& o) const {
│         return m_loc == o.m_loc && m_dir == o.m_dir && m_dest == o.m_dest;
│     }
│     
│     int SearchNode::hash() const {
│         int dh = qHash(m_dir);
│         return ((qHash(m_loc.value("x").toDouble()) * 31 + qHash(m_loc.value("y").toDouble())) * 31 + dh) * 31
│         + (qHash(m_dest.value("x").toDouble()) * 31 + qHash(m_dest.value("y").toDouble()));
│     }
│     
│     int SearchNode::getHeuristic() {
│         double curX = m_loc.value("x").toDouble();
│         double curY = m_loc.value("y").toDouble();
│         double dstX = m_dest.value("x").toDouble();
│         double dstY = m_dest.value("y").toDouble();
│         double dx = dstX - curX;
│         double dy = dstY - curY;
│         int ret = -1;
│     
│         if (m_extendsWire) {
│             if (m_dir == "east") {
│                 if (dx > 0) ret = int(dx / 10.0) * 9 + int(std::abs(dy));
│             } else if (m_dir == "west") {
│                 if (dx < 0) ret = int(-dx / 10.0) * 9 + int(std::abs(dy));
│             } else if (m_dir == "south") {
│                 if (dy > 0) ret = int(std::abs(dx)) + int(dy / 10.0) * 9;
│             } else if (m_dir == "north") {
│                 if (dy < 0) ret = int(std::abs(dx)) + int(-dy / 10.0) * 9;
│             }
│         }
│     
│         if (ret < 0) {
│             ret = int(std::abs(dx) + std::abs(dy));
│         }
│     
│         bool penalizeDoubleTurn = false;
│         if (m_dir == "east") penalizeDoubleTurn = dx < 0;
│         else if (m_dir == "west") penalizeDoubleTurn = dx > 0;
│         else if (m_dir == "north") penalizeDoubleTurn = dy > 0;
│         else if (m_dir == "south") penalizeDoubleTurn = dy < 0;
│         else if (m_dir.isEmpty()) {
│             if (dx != 0 || dy != 0) ret += TURN_PENALTY;
│         }
│     
│         if (penalizeDoubleTurn) ret += 2 * TURN_PENALTY;
│         else if (dx != 0 && dy != 0) ret += TURN_PENALTY;
│     
│         return ret;
│     }
│     
│     SearchNode* SearchNode::next(const QString& moveDir, bool crossing) {
│         int newDist = m_dist;
│         QString connDir = m_conn ? m_conn->direction() : QString();
│         double curX = m_loc.value("x").toDouble();
│         double curY = m_loc.value("y").toDouble();
│         double nextX = curX, nextY = curY;
│         if (moveDir == "east") nextX += 10;
│         else if (moveDir == "west") nextX -= 10;
│         else if (moveDir == "south") nextY += 10;
│         else if (moveDir == "north") nextY -= 10;
│     
│         bool exWire = m_extendsWire && moveDir == connDir;
│         newDist += exWire ? 9 : 10;
│         if (crossing) newDist += CROSSING_PENALTY;
│         if (moveDir != m_dir) newDist += TURN_PENALTY;
│     
│         if (nextX < 0 || nextY < 0) return nullptr;
│     
│         QVariantMap newLoc;
│         newLoc["x"] = nextX;
│         newLoc["y"] = nextY;
│         return new SearchNode(newLoc, moveDir, m_conn, m_dest, newDist, exWire, this);
│     }

└── SearchNode.h
    内容:
      #ifndef SEARCHNODE_H
      #define SEARCHNODE_H
      
      #include <QVariantMap>
      #include <QString>
      
      class ConnectionData;
      
      class SearchNode {
      public:
          static const int CROSSING_PENALTY = 20;
          static const int TURN_PENALTY = 50;
      
          SearchNode(ConnectionData* conn, const QVariantMap& src,
                     const QString& srcDir, const QVariantMap& dst);
      
          SearchNode(const QVariantMap& loc, const QString& dir, ConnectionData* conn,
                     const QVariantMap& dest, int dist, bool extendsWire, SearchNode* prev);
      
          int heuristicValue() const { return m_heur; }
          QVariantMap location() const { return m_loc; }
          QVariantMap destination() const { return m_dest; }
          QString direction() const { return m_dir; }
          int distance() const { return m_dist; }
          SearchNode* previous() const { return m_prev; }
          bool isDestination() const;
          bool isStart() const { return m_prev == nullptr; }
          bool isExtendingWire() const { return m_extendsWire; }
          ConnectionData* connection() const { return m_conn; }
      
          bool equals(const SearchNode& o) const;
          int hash() const;
      
          SearchNode* next(const QString& moveDir, bool crossing);
      
      private:
          int getHeuristic();
      
          QVariantMap m_loc;
          QString m_dir;
          ConnectionData* m_conn;
          QVariantMap m_dest;
          int m_dist;
          int m_heur;
          bool m_extendsWire;
          SearchNode* m_prev;
      };
      
      #endif

├── ComponentLogic.cpp
│   内容:
│     #include "ComponentLogic.h"
│     #include <QtGlobal>
│     
│     namespace ComponentLogic {
│     
│     // ============================================================
│     // 基础属性
│     // ============================================================
│     
│     int inputCount(const QVariantMap& comp) {
│         const QString t = comp.value("type").toString();
│         if (t == "input" || t == "clock" || t == "const" || t == "text") return 0;
│         if (t == "not") return 1;
│         if (t == "and" || t == "or" || t == "nand" || t == "nor" || t == "xor" || t == "xnor") {
│             int n = comp.value("inputCount").toInt();
│             return n < 2 ? 2 : qMin(n, 64);
│         }
│         if (t == "led" || t == "output") return 1;
│         if (t == "splitter") return 1;
│         if (t == "hub") return qMax(1, comp.value("outputSplits").toList().size());
│         if (t == "sub") return comp.value("subInputNames").toList().size();
│         if (t == "tgate" || t == "ntran" || t == "ptran") return 2;
│         return 0;
│     }
│     
│     int outputCount(const QVariantMap& comp) {
│         const QString t = comp.value("type").toString();
│         if (t == "input" || t == "clock" || t == "const") return 1;
│         if (t == "text") return 0;
│         if (t == "not" || t == "and" || t == "or" || t == "nand" ||
│             t == "nor" || t == "xor" || t == "xnor") return 1;
│         if (t == "splitter") return qMax(1, comp.value("outputSplits").toList().size());
│         if (t == "hub") return 1;
│         if (t == "led" || t == "output") return 0;
│         if (t == "sub") return comp.value("subOutputNames").toList().size();
│         if (t == "tgate" || t == "ntran" || t == "ptran") return 1;
│         return 0;
│     }
│     
│     int outputBitWidth(const QVariantMap& comp, int portIdx) {
│         const QString t = comp.value("type").toString();
│         int bw = comp.value("bitWidth").toInt();
│         if (bw < 1) bw = 1;
│         if (t == "input" || t == "const" || t == "clock" || t == "not" ||
│             t == "and" || t == "or" || t == "nand" || t == "nor" ||
│             t == "xor" || t == "xnor") return bw;
│         if (t == "tgate" || t == "ntran" || t == "ptran") return bw;
│         if (t == "splitter") {
│             QVariantList splits = comp.value("outputSplits").toList();
│             if (portIdx >= 0 && portIdx < splits.size()) {
│                 int v = splits[portIdx].toInt();
│                 return v < 1 ? 1 : v;
│             }
│             return 1;
│         }
│         if (t == "hub") return bw;
│         if (t == "sub") {
│             QVariantList ws = comp.value("subOutputWidths").toList();
│             if (portIdx >= 0 && portIdx < ws.size()) {
│                 int v = ws[portIdx].toInt();
│                 return v < 1 ? 1 : v;
│             }
│         }
│         return bw;
│     }
│     
│     int inputBitWidth(const QVariantMap& comp, int portIdx) {
│         const QString t = comp.value("type").toString();
│         int bw = comp.value("bitWidth").toInt();
│         if (bw < 1) bw = 1;
│         if (t == "splitter") return bw;
│         if (t == "hub") {
│             QVariantList splits = comp.value("outputSplits").toList();
│             if (portIdx >= 0 && portIdx < splits.size()) {
│                 int v = splits[portIdx].toInt();
│                 return v < 1 ? 1 : v;
│             }
│             return 1;
│         }
│         if (t == "sub") {
│             QVariantList ws = comp.value("subInputWidths").toList();
│             if (portIdx >= 0 && portIdx < ws.size()) {
│                 int v = ws[portIdx].toInt();
│                 return v < 1 ? 1 : v;
│             }
│         }
│         return bw;
│     }
│     
│     // ============================================================
│     // 端口几何
│     // ============================================================
│     
│     static void rotatePoint(double& x, double& y, int rot) {
│         rot = ((rot % 4) + 4) % 4;
│         double nx = x, ny = y;
│         switch (rot) {
│         case 1: nx = -y; ny =  x; break;
│         case 2: nx = -x; ny = -y; break;
│         case 3: nx =  y; ny = -x; break;
│         default: break;
│         }
│         x = nx; y = ny;
│     }
│     
│     QVariantList outputPorts(const QVariantMap& comp) {
│         QVariantList result;
│         const QString t = comp.value("type").toString();
│         int n = outputCount(comp);
│         int rot = comp.value("rotation").toInt();
│     
│         double W = 80.0, H = 60.0;
│     
│         if (t == "input" || t == "clock" || t == "const") {
│             W = qMax(60.0, comp.value("bitWidth").toInt() * 22.0 + 8.0);
│             H = 40.0;
│             QVariantMap p;
│             p["index"] = 0; p["x"] = W/2; p["y"] = 0;
│             p["bitWidth"] = outputBitWidth(comp, 0);
│             result.append(p);
│         } else if (t == "splitter") {
│             W = 80.0;
│             QVariantList splits = comp.value("outputSplits").toList();
│             int segN = qMax(1, splits.size());
│             H = qMax(60.0, (double)segN * 24.0);
│             for (int i = 0; i < segN; ++i) {
│                 QVariantMap p;
│                 p["index"] = i;
│                 p["x"] = W/2;
│                 p["y"] = -H/2 + (i + 0.5) * (H / segN);
│                 p["bitWidth"] = outputBitWidth(comp, i);
│                 result.append(p);
│             }
│         } else if (t == "hub") {
│             W = 80.0; H = 60.0;
│             QVariantMap p;
│             p["index"] = 0; p["x"] = W/2; p["y"] = 0;
│             p["bitWidth"] = outputBitWidth(comp, 0);
│             result.append(p);
│         } else if (t == "ntran") {
│             W = 80.0; H = 60.0;
│             QVariantMap p;
│             p["index"] = 0;
│             p["x"] = W/2;
│             p["y"] = -H/4;
│             p["bitWidth"] = outputBitWidth(comp, 0);
│             result.append(p);
│         } else if (t == "ptran") {
│             W = 80.0; H = 60.0;
│             QVariantMap p;
│             p["index"] = 0;
│             p["x"] = W/2;
│             p["y"] = H/4;
│             p["bitWidth"] = outputBitWidth(comp, 0);
│             result.append(p);
│         } else if (t == "not" || t == "and" || t == "or" || t == "nand" ||
│                    t == "nor" || t == "xor" || t == "xnor" || t == "sub" ||
│                    t == "tgate") {
│             W = (t == "sub") ? 110.0 : 80.0;
│             int ic = inputCount(comp);
│             int oc = n; if (oc < 1) oc = 1;
│             H = qMax(60.0, (double)qMax(ic, oc) * 24.0);
│             for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
│                 QVariantMap p;
│                 p["index"] = i;
│                 p["x"] = W/2;
│                 p["y"] = n == 1 ? 0.0 : (-H/2 + (i + 0.5) * (H / n));
│                 p["bitWidth"] = outputBitWidth(comp, i);
│                 result.append(p);
│             }
│         }
│     
│         for (int i = 0; i < result.size(); ++i) {
│             QVariantMap p = result[i].toMap();
│             double px = p.value("x").toDouble();
│             double py = p.value("y").toDouble();
│             rotatePoint(px, py, rot);
│             p["x"] = px; p["y"] = py;
│             result[i] = p;
│         }
│         return result;
│     }
│     
│     QVariantList inputPorts(const QVariantMap& comp) {
│         QVariantList result;
│         const QString t = comp.value("type").toString();
│         int n = inputCount(comp);
│         int rot = comp.value("rotation").toInt();
│     
│         double W = 80.0, H = 60.0;
│     
│         if (t == "led" || t == "output") {
│             W = qMax(t == "output" ? 60.0 : 50.0, comp.value("bitWidth").toInt() * 22.0 + 8.0);
│             H = 40.0;
│             QVariantMap p;
│             p["index"] = 0; p["x"] = -W/2; p["y"] = 0;
│             p["bitWidth"] = inputBitWidth(comp, 0);
│             result.append(p);
│         } else if (t == "splitter") {
│             W = 80.0; H = 60.0;
│             QVariantMap p;
│             p["index"] = 0; p["x"] = -W/2; p["y"] = 0;
│             p["bitWidth"] = inputBitWidth(comp, 0);
│             result.append(p);
│         } else if (t == "hub") {
│             W = 80.0;
│             QVariantList splits = comp.value("outputSplits").toList();
│             int segN = qMax(1, splits.size());
│             H = qMax(60.0, (double)segN * 24.0);
│             for (int i = 0; i < segN; ++i) {
│                 QVariantMap p;
│                 p["index"] = i;
│                 p["x"] = -W/2;
│                 p["y"] = -H/2 + (i + 0.5) * (H / segN);
│                 p["bitWidth"] = inputBitWidth(comp, i);
│                 result.append(p);
│             }
│         } else if (t == "not") {
│             W = 80.0; H = 60.0;
│             QVariantMap p;
│             p["index"] = 0; p["x"] = -W/2; p["y"] = 0;
│             p["bitWidth"] = inputBitWidth(comp, 0);
│             result.append(p);
│         } else if (t == "ntran") {
│             W = 80.0; H = 60.0;
│             QVariantMap pg;
│             pg["index"] = 0;
│             pg["x"] = -W/2; pg["y"] = 0;
│             pg["bitWidth"] = inputBitWidth(comp, 0);
│             result.append(pg);
│     
│             QVariantMap pd;
│             pd["index"] = 1;
│             pd["x"] = W/2;
│             pd["y"] = H/4;
│             pd["bitWidth"] = inputBitWidth(comp, 1);
│             result.append(pd);
│         } else if (t == "ptran") {
│             W = 80.0; H = 60.0;
│             QVariantMap pg;
│             pg["index"] = 0;
│             pg["x"] = -W/2; pg["y"] = 0;
│             pg["bitWidth"] = inputBitWidth(comp, 0);
│             result.append(pg);
│     
│             QVariantMap pd;
│             pd["index"] = 1;
│             pd["x"] = W/2;
│             pd["y"] = -H/4;
│             pd["bitWidth"] = inputBitWidth(comp, 1);
│             result.append(pd);
│         } else if (t == "and" || t == "or" || t == "nand" ||
│                    t == "nor" || t == "xor" || t == "xnor" || t == "sub" ||
│                    t == "tgate") {
│             W = (t == "sub") ? 110.0 : 80.0;
│             H = qMax(60.0, (double)n * 24.0);
│             for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
│                 QVariantMap p;
│                 p["index"] = i;
│                 p["x"] = -W/2;
│                 p["y"] = n == 1 ? 0.0 : (-H/2 + (i + 0.5) * (H / n));
│                 p["bitWidth"] = inputBitWidth(comp, i);
│                 result.append(p);
│             }
│         }
│     
│         for (int i = 0; i < result.size(); ++i) {
│             QVariantMap p = result[i].toMap();
│             double px = p.value("x").toDouble();
│             double py = p.value("y").toDouble();
│             rotatePoint(px, py, rot);
│             p["x"] = px; p["y"] = py;
│             result[i] = p;
│         }
│         return result;
│     }
│     
│     // ============================================================
│     // 组合逻辑输出计算（两个引擎共用）
│     // ============================================================
│     QVector<QString> computeCombinationalOutputs(const QString& type,
│                                                  const QVector<QString>& inputs,
│                                                  int bitWidth,
│                                                  const QVariantList& outputSplits)
│     {
│         QVector<QString> outs;
│         int bw = bitWidth < 1 ? 1 : bitWidth;
│     
│         auto bitAt = [](const QString& s, int b) -> QChar {
│             return (b < s.length()) ? s[b] : QChar('0');
│         };
│         auto inStr = [&](int i) -> QString {
│             if (i < inputs.size()) return inputs[i];
│             return QString(bw, '0');
│         };
│     
│         // ---- NOT ----
│         if (type == "not") {
│             const QString a = inStr(0);
│             QString out; out.reserve(bw);
│             for (int b = 0; b < bw; ++b) {
│                 QChar c = bitAt(a, b);
│                 if (c == 'Z' || c == 'E') out += 'E';
│                 else                       out += (c == '1') ? '0' : '1';
│             }
│             outs.append(out);
│             return outs;
│         }
│     
│         // ---- AND / OR / NAND / NOR / XOR / XNOR ----
│         if (type == "and" || type == "or" || type == "nand" ||
│             type == "nor" || type == "xor" || type == "xnor") {
│             const int  ic      = inputs.size();
│             const bool isAnd   = (type == "and"  || type == "nand");
│             const bool isOr    = (type == "or"   || type == "nor");
│             const bool needNot = (type == "nand" || type == "nor" || type == "xnor");
│     
│             QString out; out.reserve(bw);
│             for (int b = 0; b < bw; ++b) {
│                 bool hasErr = false;
│                 for (int p = 0; p < ic; ++p) {
│                     QChar c = bitAt(inputs[p], b);
│                     if (c == 'Z' || c == 'E') { hasErr = true; break; }
│                 }
│                 if (hasErr) { out += 'E'; continue; }
│     
│                 bool r = isAnd ? true : false;
│                 for (int p = 0; p < ic; ++p) {
│                     bool v = (b < inputs[p].length() && inputs[p][b] == '1');
│                     if (isAnd)      r = r && v;
│                     else if (isOr)  r = r || v;
│                     else            r = r != v;
│                 }
│                 if (needNot) r = !r;
│                 out += r ? '1' : '0';
│             }
│             outs.append(out);
│             return outs;
│         }
│     
│         // ---- TGATE / NTRAN / PTRAN ----
│         if (type == "tgate" || type == "ntran" || type == "ptran") {
│             const QString in0 = inStr(0);
│             const QString in1 = inStr(1);
│             const bool isT = (type == "tgate");
│             const bool isN = (type == "ntran");
│             // else: ptran
│     
│             QString out; out.reserve(bw);
│             for (int b = 0; b < bw; ++b) {
│                 QChar v0 = bitAt(in0, b);
│                 QChar v1 = bitAt(in1, b);
│                 if (isT) {
│                     // tgate：in0=data，in1=enable；enable=1 时输出 data，否则 Z
│                     if (v1 == 'Z' || v1 == 'E') out += 'E';
│                     else if (v1 == '1')         out += v0;
│                     else                        out += 'Z';
│                 } else if (isN) {
│                     // ntran：in0=gate，in1=data；gate=1 时导通
│                     if (v0 == 'Z' || v0 == 'E') out += 'E';
│                     else if (v0 == '1')         out += v1;
│                     else                        out += 'Z';
│                 } else {
│                     // ptran：in0=gate，in1=data；gate=0 时导通
│                     if (v0 == 'Z' || v0 == 'E') out += 'E';
│                     else if (v0 == '0')         out += v1;
│                     else                        out += 'Z';
│                 }
│             }
│             outs.append(out);
│             return outs;
│         }
│     
│         // ---- SPLITTER ----
│         if (type == "splitter") {
│             QString mainIn = inStr(0);
│             while (mainIn.length() < bw) mainIn += '0';
│             if (mainIn.length() > bw) mainIn = mainIn.left(bw);
│     
│             int offset = bw;
│             for (auto& sv : outputSplits) {
│                 int len = sv.toInt();
│                 if (len < 1) len = 1;
│                 offset -= len;
│                 if (offset < 0) offset = 0;
│                 QString out; out.reserve(len);
│                 for (int b = 0; b < len; ++b)
│                     out += (offset + b < mainIn.length()) ? mainIn[offset + b] : QChar('0');
│                 outs.append(out);
│             }
│             return outs;
│         }
│     
│         // ---- HUB ----
│         if (type == "hub") {
│             QString merged; merged.reserve(bw + 8);
│             for (int s = inputs.size() - 1; s >= 0; --s) {
│                 int len = (s < outputSplits.size()) ? outputSplits[s].toInt() : 1;
│                 if (len < 1) len = 1;
│                 QString sv = inputs[s];
│                 while (sv.length() < len) sv += '0';
│                 if (sv.length() > len) sv = sv.left(len);
│                 merged += sv;
│             }
│             while (merged.length() < bw) merged += '0';
│             if (merged.length() > bw) merged = merged.left(bw);
│             outs.append(merged);
│             return outs;
│         }
│     
│         // 未识别类型（如 sub）由调用者处理
│         return outs;
│     }
│     
│     } // namespace ComponentLogic

├── ComponentLogic.h
│   内容:
│     #ifndef COMPONENTLOGIC_H
│     #define COMPONENTLOGIC_H
│     
│     #include <QVariantMap>
│     #include <QVariantList>
│     #include <QString>
│     #include <QVector>
│     
│     /**
│      * @brief 元件类型的基础属性查询 + 端口几何计算 + 组合逻辑输出计算。
│      *
│      * 这是 logic 层唯一的"元件知识入口"。
│      * 引擎、QML 端口面板、错误检查都通过这里获取元件信息。
│      */
│     namespace ComponentLogic {
│     
│     int inputCount(const QVariantMap& comp);
│     int outputCount(const QVariantMap& comp);
│     int outputBitWidth(const QVariantMap& comp, int portIdx);
│     int inputBitWidth(const QVariantMap& comp, int portIdx);
│     
│     QVariantList outputPorts(const QVariantMap& comp);
│     QVariantList inputPorts(const QVariantMap& comp);
│     
│     // ============================================================
│     // 组合逻辑输出计算（EventEngine / IterEngine 共用）
│     // ============================================================
│     // 对"纯组合逻辑"类型统一计算输出：
│     //   not / and / or / nand / nor / xor / xnor
│     //   tgate / ntran / ptran
│     //   splitter / hub
│     // 其它类型（input/const/clock/output/led/sub）返回空 QVector，
│     // 由调用者按各自数据源处理。
│     //
│     // 参数：
│     //   type         — 元件类型
│     //   inputs       — 各输入端的二进制字符串（位宽由调用者保证与元件配置一致）
│     //   bitWidth     — 元件位宽
│     //   outputSplits — splitter / hub 的 outputSplits（其它类型传空即可）
│     //
│     // 返回：
│     //   与端口顺序匹配的输出字符串向量；未识别类型返回空。
│     QVector<QString> computeCombinationalOutputs(const QString& type,
│                                                  const QVector<QString>& inputs,
│                                                  int bitWidth,
│                                                  const QVariantList& outputSplits);
│     
│     } // namespace ComponentLogic
│     
│     #endif // COMPONENTLOGIC_H

│      * @brief 引擎种类枚举 + 字符串互转。

├── WireNetworkSolver.cpp
│   内容:
│     #include "WireNetworkSolver.h"
│     #include "ComponentLogic.h"
│     #include <QHash>
│     #include <QSet>
│     #include <QStringList>
│     #include <QVariantMap>
│     #include <QList>
│     #include <QVector>
│     #include <algorithm>
│     #include <cmath>
│     
│     namespace {
│     
│     struct DSU {
│         QHash<int, int> p;
│         int find(int x) {
│             if (!p.contains(x)) { p[x] = x; return x; }
│             int r = x;
│             while (p[r] != r) r = p[r];
│             while (p[x] != r) { int n = p[x]; p[x] = r; x = n; }
│             return r;
│         }
│         void unite(int a, int b) {
│             a = find(a); b = find(b);
│             if (a != b) p[a] = b;
│         }
│     };
│     
│     QString posKey(double x, double y) {
│         return QString("%1,%2").arg(int(std::round(x))).arg(int(std::round(y)));
│     }
│     
│     // 点 (px,py) 是否严格落在正交线段内部（不含端点）
│     bool pointOnSegmentInterior(double px, double py,
│                                 double x1, double y1, double x2, double y2) {
│         if (std::abs(y1 - y2) < 0.5) {
│             if (std::abs(py - y1) > 0.5) return false;
│             double lo = std::min(x1, x2);
│             double hi = std::max(x1, x2);
│             return px > lo + 0.5 && px < hi - 0.5;
│         }
│         if (std::abs(x1 - x2) < 0.5) {
│             if (std::abs(px - x1) > 0.5) return false;
│             double lo = std::min(y1, y2);
│             double hi = std::max(y1, y2);
│             return py > lo + 0.5 && py < hi - 0.5;
│         }
│         return false;
│     }
│     
│     // 点是否落在 wire 的任意段的"内部"（不含端点）
│     bool pointOnWireInterior(double px, double py, const QVariantList& path) {
│         for (int i = 0; i + 1 < path.size(); ++i) {
│             QVariantMap p0 = path[i].toMap();
│             QVariantMap p1 = path[i + 1].toMap();
│             if (pointOnSegmentInterior(px, py,
│                                        p0.value("x").toDouble(), p0.value("y").toDouble(),
│                                        p1.value("x").toDouble(), p1.value("y").toDouble())) {
│                 return true;
│             }
│         }
│         return false;
│     }
│     
│     } // namespace
│     
│     namespace WireNetworkSolver {
│     
│     // ============================================================
│     // 生成虚拟线（用于电气互联）
│     //   严格按照 Logisim CircuitWires.connectWires 的规则：
│     //     1. 元件端口 → 位置节点
│     //     2. wire 的两个端点 → unite（端点对端点）
│     //     3. T 型接入（端点落在另一根 wire 的中间段）→ unite
│     //        （等价于 Logisim 分割 wire 后变成端点对端点）
│     //     4. 中间对中间（十字交叉）→ 不 unite
│     //   问题："不是只要两根线有一个点坐标一样就算交叉"
│     //     → 本实现只 unite "端点重合" 和 "端点落在段中间"，
│     //       中间对中间交叉不 unite。
│     // ============================================================
│     QVariantList generateVirtualWires(const QVariantList& components,
│                                       const QVariantList& wires)
│     {
│         QVariantList result;
│         if (components.isEmpty() && wires.isEmpty()) return result;
│     
│         DSU dsu;
│         int nextId = 0;
│     
│         QHash<QString, int> portNode;   // "cid:port:isOut" → nodeId
│         QHash<QString, int> posNode;    // "x,y" → nodeId
│         QVector<int> wireFirst(wires.size(), -1);
│     
│         auto getPosNode = [&](double x, double y) -> int {
│             QString k = posKey(x, y);
│             if (!posNode.contains(k)) {
│                 int nid = nextId++;
│                 posNode[k] = nid;
│                 dsu.find(nid);
│             }
│             return posNode[k];
│         };
│         auto getPortNode = [&](const QString& cid, int port, bool isOut) -> int {
│             QString k = QString("%1:%2:%3").arg(cid).arg(port).arg(isOut ? 1 : 0);
│             if (!portNode.contains(k)) {
│                 int nid = nextId++;
│                 portNode[k] = nid;
│                 dsu.find(nid);
│             }
│             return portNode[k];
│         };
│     
│         // ============ 1. 元件端口 → 位置节点 ============
│         for (const auto& cv : components) {
│             QVariantMap c = cv.toMap();
│             QString cid = c.value("id").toString();
│             QString t = c.value("type").toString();
│             if (t == "text") continue;
│             double cx = c.value("x").toDouble();
│             double cy = c.value("y").toDouble();
│     
│             QVariantList outs = ComponentLogic::outputPorts(c);
│             for (int i = 0; i < outs.size(); ++i) {
│                 QVariantMap p = outs[i].toMap();
│                 int pn = getPortNode(cid, i, true);
│                 int loc = getPosNode(cx + p.value("x").toDouble(),
│                                      cy + p.value("y").toDouble());
│                 dsu.unite(pn, loc);
│             }
│             QVariantList ins = ComponentLogic::inputPorts(c);
│             for (int i = 0; i < ins.size(); ++i) {
│                 QVariantMap p = ins[i].toMap();
│                 int pn = getPortNode(cid, i, false);
│                 int loc = getPosNode(cx + p.value("x").toDouble(),
│                                      cy + p.value("y").toDouble());
│                 dsu.unite(pn, loc);
│             }
│         }
│     
│         // ============ 2. 每根 wire 内部 unite ============
│         //   ortho：path 所有顶点 unite 到 first（端点 + 拐点）
│         //          同时把 fromComp/toComp 的端口 unite 进来
│         //   bezier：fromComp/output 和 toComp/input unite
│         for (int wi = 0; wi < wires.size(); ++wi) {
│             QVariantMap w = wires[wi].toMap();
│             QString wt = w.value("wireType").toString();
│             if (wt == "virtual") continue;
│     
│             if (wt == "ortho") {
│                 QVariantList path = w.value("path").toList();
│                 if (path.size() < 2) continue;
│     
│                 int first = getPosNode(path[0].toMap().value("x").toDouble(),
│                                        path[0].toMap().value("y").toDouble());
│                 wireFirst[wi] = first;
│     
│                 for (int i = 1; i < path.size(); ++i) {
│                     int n = getPosNode(path[i].toMap().value("x").toDouble(),
│                                        path[i].toMap().value("y").toDouble());
│                     dsu.unite(first, n);
│                 }
│     
│                 if (w.contains("fromComp")) {
│                     QString fc = w.value("fromComp").toString();
│                     int fp = w.value("fromPort").toInt();
│                     int pn = getPortNode(fc, fp, true);
│                     dsu.unite(pn, first);
│                 }
│                 if (w.contains("toComp")) {
│                     QString tc = w.value("toComp").toString();
│                     int tp = w.value("toPort").toInt();
│                     int pn = getPortNode(tc, tp, false);
│                     dsu.unite(pn, first);
│                 }
│             } else {
│                 // bezier
│                 QString fc = w.value("fromComp").toString();
│                 int fp = w.value("fromPort").toInt();
│                 QString tc = w.value("toComp").toString();
│                 int tp = w.value("toPort").toInt();
│                 if (fc.isEmpty() || tc.isEmpty()) continue;
│                 int pn1 = getPortNode(fc, fp, true);
│                 int pn2 = getPortNode(tc, tp, false);
│                 dsu.unite(pn1, pn2);
│             }
│         }
│     
│         // ============ 3. T 型连接：位置 P 落在 wire 的中间段 ============
│         //   严格按 Logisim 分割语义：
│         //     位置 P 是某根 wire 的中间点（不是任何 wire 的端点）
│         //     则该 wire 被 P 分割成两段，P 成为新端点 → unite
│         //   ★ 遍历所有 posNode 中的位置（wire 顶点 + 元件端口），
│         //     检查它们是否落在其他 wire 的"中间段"上。
│         //   ★ 十字交叉：交叉点不是任何 wire 顶点或元件端口，
│         //     不在 posNode 里，不会被处理 → 不 unite。
│         for (auto it = posNode.constBegin(); it != posNode.constEnd(); ++it) {
│             QStringList parts = it.key().split(',');
│             if (parts.size() != 2) continue;
│             double px = parts[0].toDouble();
│             double py = parts[1].toDouble();
│             int pn = it.value();
│     
│             for (int wi = 0; wi < wires.size(); ++wi) {
│                 if (wireFirst[wi] < 0) continue;
│                 QVariantMap w = wires[wi].toMap();
│                 QVariantList path = w.value("path").toList();
│                 if (pointOnWireInterior(px, py, path)) {
│                     dsu.unite(pn, wireFirst[wi]);
│                 }
│             }
│         }
│     
│         // ============ 4. 分类 output / input ============
│         QHash<int, QList<QString>> netOutputs;
│         QHash<int, QList<QString>> netInputs;
│     
│         for (auto it = portNode.constBegin(); it != portNode.constEnd(); ++it) {
│             QString key = it.key();
│             QStringList parts = key.split(':');
│             if (parts.size() != 3) continue;
│             QString cid = parts[0];
│             int port = parts[1].toInt();
│             bool isOut = parts[2].toInt() == 1;
│             int root = dsu.find(it.value());
│             if (isOut) netOutputs[root].append(cid + ":" + QString::number(port));
│             else       netInputs[root].append(cid + ":" + QString::number(port));
│         }
│     
│         // ============ 5. 生成虚拟线：每个网络 output × input ============
│         for (auto it = netOutputs.constBegin(); it != netOutputs.constEnd(); ++it) {
│             int root = it.key();
│             if (!netInputs.contains(root)) continue;
│             for (const auto& out : it.value()) {
│                 for (const auto& in : netInputs.value(root)) {
│                     QStringList op = out.split(':');
│                     QStringList ip = in.split(':');
│                     QVariantMap vw;
│                     vw["id"] = QString("VW_%1_%2_%3_%4")
│                                    .arg(op[0]).arg(op[1]).arg(ip[0]).arg(ip[1]);
│                     vw["wireType"] = "virtual";
│                     vw["fromComp"] = op[0];
│                     vw["fromPort"] = op[1].toInt();
│                     vw["toComp"] = ip[0];
│                     vw["toPort"] = ip[1].toInt();
│                     result.append(vw);
│                 }
│             }
│         }
│     
│         return result;
│     }
│     
│     // ============================================================
│     // 连接圆点（junction dots）
│     //   Logisim 规则：连接数 >= 3 画圆点
│     //   本实现模拟 Logisim 分割语义：
│     //     - wire 端点 / 元件端口：+1
│     //     - T 型接入点（wire 顶点落在另一根 wire 中间）：+2
│     //       （模拟：被接入的 wire 被分割成两根 → 该 wire 贡献 2 个端点）
│     //   这样：
│     //     三根 wire 端点共点（3 端点）→ 3 → 画
│     //     T 型（1 端点 + 1 wire 被分割=2）→ 3 → 画
│     //     两根 wire 端点相连 → 2 → 不画（视觉上就是一条线，不需要圆点）
│     //     十字交叉（中间对中间）→ 0 → 不画
│     // ============================================================
│     QVariantList connectionDots(const QVariantList& components,
│                                 const QVariantList& wires)
│     {
│         QHash<QString, int> posCount;
│         QHash<QString, QVariantMap> posMap;
│     
│         // ============ 元件端口 ============
│         for (const auto& cv : components) {
│             QVariantMap c = cv.toMap();
│             QString t = c.value("type").toString();
│             if (t == "text") continue;
│             double cx = c.value("x").toDouble();
│             double cy = c.value("y").toDouble();
│     
│             QVariantList outs = ComponentLogic::outputPorts(c);
│             for (const auto& pv : outs) {
│                 QVariantMap p = pv.toMap();
│                 double px = cx + p.value("x").toDouble();
│                 double py = cy + p.value("y").toDouble();
│                 QString k = posKey(px, py);
│                 posCount[k]++;
│                 QVariantMap m; m["x"] = px; m["y"] = py;
│                 posMap[k] = m;
│             }
│             QVariantList ins = ComponentLogic::inputPorts(c);
│             for (const auto& pv : ins) {
│                 QVariantMap p = pv.toMap();
│                 double px = cx + p.value("x").toDouble();
│                 double py = cy + p.value("y").toDouble();
│                 QString k = posKey(px, py);
│                 posCount[k]++;
│                 QVariantMap m; m["x"] = px; m["y"] = py;
│                 posMap[k] = m;
│             }
│         }
│     
│         // 收集 ortho wire paths
│         QVector<QVariantList> orthoPaths;
│         for (const auto& wv : wires) {
│             QVariantMap w = wv.toMap();
│             if (w.value("wireType").toString() != "ortho") continue;
│             orthoPaths.append(w.value("path").toList());
│         }
│     
│         // ============ wire 顶点 ============
│         for (int wi = 0; wi < orthoPaths.size(); ++wi) {
│             const QVariantList& path = orthoPaths[wi];
│             QSet<QString> seen;
│             for (const auto& pv : path) {
│                 QVariantMap p = pv.toMap();
│                 double px = p.value("x").toDouble();
│                 double py = p.value("y").toDouble();
│                 QString k = posKey(px, py);
│                 if (!seen.contains(k)) {
│                     seen.insert(k);
│                     posCount[k]++;
│                     QVariantMap m; m["x"] = px; m["y"] = py;
│                     posMap[k] = m;
│                 }
│             }
│     
│             // T 型接入：wire wi 的顶点落在另一根 wire 的中间段
│             // +2（模拟 wire 被分割成两根）
│             for (const auto& pv : path) {
│                 QVariantMap p = pv.toMap();
│                 double px = p.value("x").toDouble();
│                 double py = p.value("y").toDouble();
│                 bool isT = false;
│                 for (int wj = 0; wj < orthoPaths.size(); ++wj) {
│                     if (wi == wj) continue;
│                     if (pointOnWireInterior(px, py, orthoPaths[wj])) {
│                         isT = true;
│                         break;
│                     }
│                 }
│                 if (isT) {
│                     QString k = posKey(px, py);
│                     posCount[k] += 2;
│                 }
│             }
│         }
│     
│         // ============ 输出 ============
│         QVariantList out;
│         for (auto it = posCount.constBegin(); it != posCount.constEnd(); ++it) {
│             if (it.value() >= 3) {
│                 out.append(posMap.value(it.key()));
│             }
│         }
│         return out;
│     }
│     
│     } // namespace WireNetworkSolver

│     Circuit::Circuit(QObject* parent) : QObject(parent) {
│         CircuitUndoData* d = new CircuitUndoData();
│         setProperty(kUndoPropName, QVariant::fromValue(static_cast<void*>(d)));
│     
│         loadRecentFiles();
│     
│         m_clockTimer = new QTimer(this);
│         connect(m_clockTimer, &QTimer::timeout, this, &Circuit::tick);
│     
│         m_eventEngine = new EventEngine();
│     
│         m_simTimer = new QTimer(this);
│         m_simTimer->setInterval(16);
│         connect(m_simTimer, &QTimer::timeout, this, &Circuit::onSimTimerTick);
│     
│         loadSettings();
│     }

│     Circuit::~Circuit() {
│         CircuitUndoData* d = und();
│         if (d) {
│             delete d;
│             setProperty(kUndoPropName, QVariant());
│         }
│         if (m_eventEngine) { delete m_eventEngine; m_eventEngine = nullptr; }
│     }
│     
│     void Circuit::loadSettings() {
│         QSettings s;
│         QString et = s.value("sim/engineType", "event").toString();
│         m_engineType = (et == "iter") ? "iter" : "event";
│     
│         if (m_eventEngine) {
│             qulonglong gd = s.value("sim/globalGateDelay", 1).toULongLong();
│             if (gd < 1) gd = 1;
│             m_eventEngine->setGlobalGateDelay(gd);
│     
│             qulonglong wd = s.value("sim/wireDelay", 1).toULongLong();
│             if (wd < 1) wd = 1;
│             m_eventEngine->setWireDelay(wd);
│         }
│     
│         m_simulationSpeedExp = qBound(0, s.value("sim/simulationSpeedExp", 3).toInt(), 12);
│         qulonglong sp = 1;
│         for (int i = 0; i < m_simulationSpeedExp; ++i) sp *= 10;
│         m_simulationSpeed = sp;
│     
│         m_autoSpeed      = s.value("sim/autoSpeed", true).toBool();
│         m_showSimTime    = s.value("sim/showSimTime", false).toBool();
│         m_clockFrequency = qBound(1, s.value("sim/clockFrequency", 2).toInt(), 1000);
│     
│         m_wireMode = s.value("sim/wireMode", "bezier").toString();
│         if (m_wireMode != "bezier" && m_wireMode != "ortho" && m_wireMode != "switchable")
│             m_wireMode = "bezier";
│         m_currentWireType = s.value("sim/currentWireType", "bezier").toString();
│         if (m_currentWireType != "bezier" && m_currentWireType != "ortho")
│             m_currentWireType = "bezier";
│     }
│     
│     void Circuit::saveSettings() {
│         QSettings s;
│         s.setValue("sim/engineType", m_engineType);
│         s.setValue("sim/globalGateDelay",
│                    m_eventEngine ? m_eventEngine->globalGateDelay() : qulonglong(1));
│         s.setValue("sim/wireDelay",
│                    m_eventEngine ? m_eventEngine->wireDelay() : qulonglong(1));
│         s.setValue("sim/simulationSpeedExp", m_simulationSpeedExp);
│         s.setValue("sim/autoSpeed", m_autoSpeed);
│         s.setValue("sim/showSimTime", m_showSimTime);
│         s.setValue("sim/clockFrequency", m_clockFrequency);
│         s.setValue("sim/wireMode", m_wireMode);
│         s.setValue("sim/currentWireType", m_currentWireType);
│     }
│     
│     void Circuit::setShowSimTime(bool b) {
│         if (m_showSimTime == b) return;
│         m_showSimTime = b;
│         saveSettings();
│         emit showSimTimeChanged();
│     }
│     
│     void Circuit::setWireMode(const QString& m) {
│         if (m_wireMode == m) return;
│         if (m != "bezier" && m != "ortho" && m != "switchable") return;
│         m_wireMode = m;
│         saveSettings();
│         emit wireModeChanged();
│     }

│         if (t != "bezier" && t != "ortho") return;

│         saveSettings();
│         emit wireModeChanged();
│     }

│         if (m_currentCtxId.isEmpty()) return m_root;
│         auto it = m_subContexts.find(m_currentCtxId);
│         if (it == m_subContexts.end()) return m_root;
│         return it.value();

│         if (it == m_subContexts.end()) return m_root;
│         return it.value();
│     }

│         return &it.value();

│         return &it.value();
│     }

│     void Circuit::setSimulationSpeedExp(int e) {
│         if (e < 0) e = 0;
│         if (e > 12) e = 12;
│         if (m_simulationSpeedExp == e) return;
│         m_simulationSpeedExp = e;
│         qulonglong v = 1;
│         for (int i = 0; i < e; ++i) v *= 10;
│         m_simulationSpeed = v;
│         saveSettings();
│         emit engineSettingsChanged();
│     }

│         if (m_autoSpeed == a) return;
│         m_autoSpeed = a;
│         saveSettings();
│         emit engineSettingsChanged();
│     }

      #ifndef CIRCUIT_H
      #define CIRCUIT_H
      
      #include <QObject>
      #include <QVariantList>
      #include <QVariantMap>
      #include <QString>
      #include <QStringList>
      #include <QHash>

      class Circuit : public QObject
      {
          Q_OBJECT
          Q_DISABLE_COPY_MOVE(Circuit)
      
          Q_PROPERTY(QVariantList components READ components NOTIFY changed)
          Q_PROPERTY(QVariantList wires READ wires NOTIFY changed)
          Q_PROPERTY(QString contextId READ contextId NOTIFY contextChanged)
          Q_PROPERTY(QString contextName READ contextName NOTIFY contextChanged)
          Q_PROPERTY(bool isRootContext READ isRootContext NOTIFY contextChanged)
          Q_PROPERTY(bool isViewOnly READ isViewOnly NOTIFY contextChanged)
          Q_PROPERTY(QVariantList subcircuits READ subcircuits NOTIFY subcircuitsChanged)
          Q_PROPERTY(QVariantList editContexts READ editContexts NOTIFY editContextsChanged)
          Q_PROPERTY(QVariantList errors READ errors NOTIFY errorsChanged)
          Q_PROPERTY(QVariantList recentFiles READ recentFiles NOTIFY recentFilesChanged)
          Q_PROPERTY(bool clockRunning READ clockRunning NOTIFY clockChanged)
          Q_PROPERTY(int clockFrequency READ clockFrequency NOTIFY clockChanged)
          Q_PROPERTY(int clockTickCount READ clockTickCount NOTIFY clockChanged)
          Q_PROPERTY(bool canUndo READ canUndo NOTIFY undoRedoChanged)
          Q_PROPERTY(bool canRedo READ canRedo NOTIFY undoRedoChanged)
          Q_PROPERTY(QString engineType READ engineType WRITE setEngineType NOTIFY engineChanged)
          Q_PROPERTY(bool simulationRunning READ simulationRunning NOTIFY simulationChanged)
          Q_PROPERTY(qulonglong simTime READ simTime NOTIFY simulationTick)
          Q_PROPERTY(qulonglong globalGateDelay READ globalGateDelay WRITE setGlobalGateDelay NOTIFY engineSettingsChanged)
          Q_PROPERTY(qulonglong wireDelay READ wireDelay WRITE setWireDelay NOTIFY engineSettingsChanged)
          Q_PROPERTY(qulonglong simulationSpeed READ simulationSpeed WRITE setSimulationSpeed NOTIFY engineSettingsChanged)
          Q_PROPERTY(int simulationSpeedExp READ simulationSpeedExp WRITE setSimulationSpeedExp NOTIFY engineSettingsChanged)
          Q_PROPERTY(bool autoSpeed READ autoSpeed WRITE setAutoSpeed NOTIFY engineSettingsChanged)
          Q_PROPERTY(bool showSimTime READ showSimTime WRITE setShowSimTime NOTIFY showSimTimeChanged)
          Q_PROPERTY(QString wireMode READ wireMode WRITE setWireMode NOTIFY wireModeChanged)
          Q_PROPERTY(QString currentWireType READ currentWireType WRITE setCurrentWireType NOTIFY wireModeChanged)
          Q_PROPERTY(QString effectiveWireType READ effectiveWireType NOTIFY wireModeChanged)
      
      public:
          explicit Circuit(QObject* parent = nullptr);
          ~Circuit() override;
      
          QVariantList components() const;
          QVariantList wires() const;
          QString contextId() const { return m_currentCtxId; }
          QString contextName() const;
          bool isRootContext() const { return m_currentCtxId.isEmpty(); }
          bool isViewOnly() const { return m_viewOnly; }
          QVariantList subcircuits() const;
          QVariantList editContexts() const;
          QVariantList errors() const { return m_errors; }
          QVariantList recentFiles() const { return m_recentFiles; }
          bool clockRunning() const { return m_clockRunning; }
          int clockFrequency() const { return m_clockFrequency; }
          int clockTickCount() const { return m_clockTickCount; }
          bool canUndo() const;
          bool canRedo() const;
      
          QString engineType() const { return m_engineType; }
          void setEngineType(const QString& t);
          bool simulationRunning() const { return m_simulationRunning; }
          qulonglong simTime() const;
          qulonglong globalGateDelay() const;
          void setGlobalGateDelay(qulonglong d);
          qulonglong wireDelay() const;
          void setWireDelay(qulonglong d);
          qulonglong simulationSpeed() const { return m_simulationSpeed; }
          void setSimulationSpeed(qulonglong s);
          int simulationSpeedExp() const { return m_simulationSpeedExp; }
          void setSimulationSpeedExp(int e);
          bool autoSpeed() const { return m_autoSpeed; }
          void setAutoSpeed(bool a);
          bool showSimTime() const { return m_showSimTime; }
          void setShowSimTime(bool b);
      
          QString wireMode() const { return m_wireMode; }
          void setWireMode(const QString& m);
          QString currentWireType() const { return m_currentWireType; }
          void setCurrentWireType(const QString& t);
          Q_INVOKABLE QString effectiveWireType() const;
      
          Q_INVOKABLE void setGateTypeDelay(const QString& type, qulonglong d);
          Q_INVOKABLE qulonglong getGateTypeDelay(const QString& type) const;
          Q_INVOKABLE void clearGateTypeDelays();
          Q_INVOKABLE void startSimulation();
          Q_INVOKABLE void pauseSimulation();
          Q_INVOKABLE void resetSimulation();
          Q_INVOKABLE void simulateStep();
      
          Q_INVOKABLE QString addComponent(const QString& type, double x, double y,
                                           int bitWidth = 1, int inputCount = 2,
                                           const QString& subId = QString());
          Q_INVOKABLE void removeComponent(const QString& id);
          Q_INVOKABLE void removeComponents(const QStringList& ids);
          Q_INVOKABLE void moveComponent(const QString& id, double x, double y);
          Q_INVOKABLE void moveSelection(const QStringList& ids, double dx, double dy);
          Q_INVOKABLE void moveComponentWithWires(const QString& id, double x, double y);
          Q_INVOKABLE void moveSelectionWithWires(const QStringList& ids, double dx, double dy);
          Q_INVOKABLE void toggleInput(const QString& id);
          Q_INVOKABLE void setBitValue(const QString& id, int bit, bool value);

          Q_INVOKABLE void renameComponent(const QString& id, const QString& name);

          Q_INVOKABLE QString addText(double x, double y, const QString& text = QString("文本"));

                      delegate: Rectangle {

                          anchors.verticalCenter: parent.verticalCenter

                          border.color: main.mode === modelData.id ? Theme.borderBtnActive : Theme.borderWeak

                          }

                              anchors.fill: parent
                              enabled: !main.circuitViewOnly
                              onClicked: {
                                  main.mode = modelData.id
                                  main.wiring = false
                                  main.placingType = ""
                                  if (modelData.id !== "select") main.selectedIds = []
                              }
                          }
                      }

                          onClicked: {
                              var t = main.circuitRef.currentWireType
                              main.circuitRef.currentWireType = (t === "ortho") ? "bezier" : "ortho"
                          }
                      }
                  }
      
                  Rectangle {
                      width: 1; height: 22; color: Theme.borderStrong
                      anchors.verticalCenter: parent.verticalCenter
                  }
      
                  Rectangle {
                      width: 28; height: 28; radius: Theme.buttonRadius
                      anchors.verticalCenter: parent.verticalCenter
                      color: undoBtn.pressed && main.circuitRef.canUndo ? Theme.bgButtonHover : Theme.bgButton
                      border.color: Theme.borderStrong; border.width: 1
                      opacity: main.circuitRef.canUndo ? 1 : 0.4
                      Text { anchors.centerIn: parent; text: "↶"; color: Theme.text; font.pixelSize: 15 }
                      MouseArea {
                          id: undoBtn; anchors.fill: parent
                          enabled: main.circuitRef.canUndo && !main.circuitViewOnly

                      anchors.verticalCenter: parent.verticalCenter

                      opacity: main.circuitRef.canRedo ? 1 : 0.4
                      Text { anchors.centerIn: parent; text: "↷"; color: Theme.text; font.pixelSize: 15 }
                      MouseArea {
                          id: redoBtn; anchors.fill: parent
                          enabled: main.circuitRef.canRedo && !main.circuitViewOnly
                          onClicked: main.circuitRef.redo()
                      }
                  }
      
                  Rectangle {
                      width: 1; height: 22; color: Theme.borderStrong
                      anchors.verticalCenter: parent.verticalCenter
                  }
      
                  Rectangle {
                      width: 28; height: 28; radius: Theme.buttonRadius
                      anchors.verticalCenter: parent.verticalCenter

                      Text { anchors.centerIn: parent; text: "−"; color: Theme.text; font.pixelSize: 15 }
                      MouseArea { id: zo; anchors.fill: parent; onClicked: main.zoomOut() }

                      width: 46; height: 28; radius: Theme.buttonRadius

                      color: rst.pressed ? Theme.bgButtonHover : Theme.bgButton

                      width: 1; height: 22; color: Theme.borderStrong

                      anchors.verticalCenter: parent.verticalCenter

                      border.color: Theme.borderDialog; border.width: 1

                          anchors.fill: parent

                      anchors.verticalCenter: parent.verticalCenter

                          onClicked: main.topCollapsed = true
                      }
                  }
      
                  Item { width: 8; height: 1 }
              }
          }
      }

      .pragma library
      
      function findPortAt(main, cv, sx, sy) {
          var comps = main.cachedComps
          var best = null
          var bestDist = 900
          for (var i = 0; i < comps.length; i++) {
              var c = comps[i]
              var outs = main.outPortsById[c.id] || []
              for (var k = 0; k < outs.length; k++) {
                  var p = outs[k]
                  var pp = cv.worldToScreen(c.x + p.x, c.y + p.y)
                  var dx = sx - pp.x, dy = sy - pp.y
                  var d = dx*dx + dy*dy
                  if (d < bestDist) {
                      bestDist = d
                      best = { compId: c.id, portIdx: k, isOutput: true }
                  }
              }
              var ins = main.inPortsById[c.id] || []
              for (var k2 = 0; k2 < ins.length; k2++) {
                  var p2 = ins[k2]
                  var pp2 = cv.worldToScreen(c.x + p2.x, c.y + p2.y)
                  var dx2 = sx - pp2.x, dy2 = sy - pp2.y
                  var d2 = dx2*dx2 + dy2*dy2
                  if (d2 < bestDist) {
                      bestDist = d2
                      best = { compId: c.id, portIdx: k2, isOutput: false }
                  }
              }
          }
          return best
      }
      
      function resolveEffectiveWireType(main) {
          var wm = main.circuitRef.wireMode
          if (wm === "ortho") return "ortho"
          if (wm === "bezier") return "bezier"
          var cwt = main.circuitRef.currentWireType
          return (cwt === "ortho") ? "ortho" : "bezier"
      }
      
      function snapWorldPoint(main, cv, mouse) {
          var port = findPortAt(main, cv, mouse.x, mouse.y)
          if (port) {
              var ep = cv.portPos(port.compId, port.portIdx, port.isOutput)
              return { x: ep.x, y: ep.y, port: port }
          }
          var wp = cv.screenToWorld(mouse.x, mouse.y)
          return {
              x: Math.round(wp.x / 10) * 10,
              y: Math.round(wp.y / 10) * 10,
              port: null
          }
      }
      
      function snapToWire(main, wireId, wx, wy) {
          var w = main.circuitRef.getWire(wireId)
          if (!w || !w.path || w.path.length < 2) return null
          var path = w.path
          var bestX = 0, bestY = 0, bestD = 1e18
          var bestIsHorizontal = false
          for (var i = 0; i + 1 < path.length; i++) {
              var p0 = path[i], p1 = path[i + 1]
              var x1 = p0.x, y1 = p0.y, x2 = p1.x, y2 = p1.y
              var dx = x2 - x1, dy = y2 - y1
              var len2 = dx*dx + dy*dy
              var t = 0
              if (len2 > 1e-9) {
                  t = ((wx - x1) * dx + (wy - y1) * dy) / len2
                  if (t < 0) t = 0; else if (t > 1) t = 1
              }
              var cx = x1 + t * dx, cy = y1 + t * dy
              var d = (wx - cx) * (wx - cx) + (wy - cy) * (wy - cy)
              if (d < bestD) {
                  bestD = d
                  bestX = cx
                  bestY = cy
                  bestIsHorizontal = Math.abs(y1 - y2) < 0.5
              }
          }
          if (bestIsHorizontal) {
              var snappedX = Math.round(bestX / 10) * 10
              var lo = Math.min(path[0].x, path[path.length-1].x)
              var hi = Math.max(path[0].x, path[path.length-1].x)
              if (snappedX < lo) snappedX = lo
              if (snappedX > hi) snappedX = hi
              return { x: snappedX, y: bestY }
          } else {
              var snappedY = Math.round(bestY / 10) * 10
              var lo2 = Math.min(path[0].y, path[path.length-1].y)
              var hi2 = Math.max(path[0].y, path[path.length-1].y)
              if (snappedY < lo2) snappedY = lo2
              if (snappedY > hi2) snappedY = hi2
              return { x: bestX, y: snappedY }
          }
      }
      
      function updateWiringDirection(main, sx, sy, wx, wy) {
          var H = 1, V = 2
          var sameX = Math.abs(wx - sx) < 0.5
          var sameY = Math.abs(wy - sy) < 0.5
          var dir = main.wiringDirection
          if (dir === 0) {
              if (!sameX) dir = H
              else if (!sameY) dir = V
          } else if (dir === H && sameX) {
              if (sameY) dir = 0
              else dir = V
          } else if (dir === V && sameY) {
              if (sameX) dir = 0
              else dir = H
          }
          main.wiringDirection = dir
      }
      
      // ★ 容差从 0.5 放宽到 2.0：端点在段内部更容易命中（尤其缩放后）
      function pointOnSegInterior(px, py, x1, y1, x2, y2) {
          var tol = 2.0
          if (Math.abs(y1 - y2) < 0.5) {
              if (Math.abs(py - y1) > 0.5) return false
              var lo = Math.min(x1, x2)
              var hi = Math.max(x1, x2)
              return px > lo + tol && px < hi - tol
          }
          if (Math.abs(x1 - x2) < 0.5) {
              if (Math.abs(px - x1) > 0.5) return false
              var lo2 = Math.min(y1, y2)
              var hi2 = Math.max(y1, y2)
              return py > lo2 + tol && py < hi2 - tol
          }
          return false
      }
      
      // 起点是否在某 wire 的端点附近（起点在端点 → 缩短候选）
      function findShortenTargetAt(main, wx, wy) {
          var wids = []
          for (var i = 0; i < main.cachedWires.length; i++) {
              var w = main.cachedWires[i]
              if ((w.wireType || "bezier") !== "ortho") continue
              if (!w.path || w.path.length < 2) continue
              var p0 = w.path[0]
              var pn = w.path[w.path.length - 1]
              if ((Math.abs(p0.x - wx) < 3 && Math.abs(p0.y - wy) < 3) ||
                  (Math.abs(pn.x - wx) < 3 && Math.abs(pn.y - wy) < 3)) {
                  wids.push(w.id)
              }
          }
          return wids
      }
      
      function startWiringFromPort(main, cv, onPort) {
          var startPt = cv.portPos(onPort.compId, onPort.portIdx, onPort.isOutput)
          main.wiringFromComp = onPort.compId
          main.wiringFromPort = onPort.portIdx
          main.wiringStartIsOutput = onPort.isOutput
          main.wiring = true
          main.wiringStartX = startPt.x
          main.wiringStartY = startPt.y
          var ss = cv.worldToScreen(startPt.x, startPt.y)
          main.wireEndX = ss.x
          main.wireEndY = ss.y
          main.wiringDirection = 0
          main.selectedWireId = ""
      }
      
      function startWiringFromWire(main, cv, snapPt) {
          main.wiringFromComp = ""
          main.wiringFromPort = -1
          main.wiringStartIsOutput = true
          main.wiring = true
          main.wiringStartX = snapPt.x
          main.wiringStartY = snapPt.y
          var ss = cv.worldToScreen(snapPt.x, snapPt.y)
          main.wireEndX = ss.x
          main.wireEndY = ss.y
          main.wiringDirection = 0
          main.selectedWireId = ""
      }
      
      function onPressed(main, cv, mouse) {
          cv.lastMouseX = mouse.x; cv.lastMouseY = mouse.y
          var wp = cv.screenToWorld(mouse.x, mouse.y)
          var editable = !main.circuitViewOnly
      
          if (editable && main.mode === "edit" && main.placingType !== "") {
              var subId = main.placingType === "sub" ? main.placingSubId : ""
              var newId = main.circuitRef.addComponent(main.placingType, wp.x, wp.y,
                                                       main.placingBitWidth,
                                                       main.placingInputCount,
                                                       subId)
              if (newId === "") main.statusText = "⚠ 无法放置"
              else {
                  main.selectedCompId = newId
                  main.selectedWireId = ""
                  main.refreshSel()
              }
              main.placingType = ""
              main.placingSubId = ""
              return
          }
      
          var comps = main.cachedComps
      
          // select 模式
          if (main.mode === "select") {
              var wid = main.circuitRef.wireAtPoint(wp.x, wp.y, 15 / Math.max(0.1, main.viewScale))
              if (wid && wid !== "") {
                  main.selectedWireId = wid
                  main.selectedIds = []
                  main.selectedCompId = ""
                  main.statusText = "已选中连线（Delete 删除，Ctrl+C 复制）"
                  cv.requestPaint()
                  return
              }
              for (var i = comps.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
                  var c = comps[i]
                  var size = cv.compSize(c)
                  if (Math.abs(wp.x - c.x) < size.w/2 && Math.abs(wp.y - c.y) < size.h/2) {
                      if (main.selectedIds.indexOf(c.id) < 0) main.selectedIds = [c.id]
                      main.selectedWireId = ""
                      main.circuitRef.breakUndoMerge()
                      main.draggingSelection = true
                      main.lastDragWorldX = wp.x
                      main.lastDragWorldY = wp.y
                      cv.requestPaint()
                      return
                  }
              }
              main.selectedIds = []
              main.selectedWireId = ""
              main.boxSelecting = true
              main.boxStartX = mouse.x; main.boxStartY = mouse.y
              main.boxCurrentX = mouse.x; main.boxCurrentY = mouse.y
              cv.requestPaint()
              return
          }
      
          // ★ 编辑模式：无论起点是什么，先探测缩短候选
          var shortenCandidates = []
          if (editable && main.mode === "edit") {
              shortenCandidates = findShortenTargetAt(main, wp.x, wp.y)
          }
      
          // 端口优先
          if (editable && main.mode === "edit") {
              var onPort = findPortAt(main, cv, mouse.x, mouse.y)
              if (onPort) {
                  startWiringFromPort(main, cv, onPort)
                  main.shortenWireCandidates = shortenCandidates
                  main.statusText = "从端口引出（拖回原线内部可缩短）"
                  cv.requestPaint()
                  return
              }
      
              // 从线中间：T 型接入
              var wireId = main.circuitRef.wireAtPoint(wp.x, wp.y, 15 / Math.max(0.1, main.viewScale))
              if (wireId && wireId !== "") {
                  var effType = resolveEffectiveWireType(main)
                  if (effType === "ortho") {
                      var snapPt = snapToWire(main, wireId, wp.x, wp.y)
                      if (snapPt) {
                          startWiringFromWire(main, cv, snapPt)
                          // ★ 修复：保留缩短候选（用户可能想从这条线上缩短回原线内部）
                          main.shortenWireCandidates = shortenCandidates
                          main.statusText = "从线引出（T 型接入，拖回线上方可缩短）"
                          cv.requestPaint()
                          return
                      }
                  } else {
                      main.statusText = "⚠ 贝塞尔线只能从端口引出"
                  }
              }
          }
      
          // 元件
          for (var i4 = comps.length - 1; i4 >= 0; i4--) {
              var c4 = comps[i4]
              var size4 = cv.compSize(c4)
              if (Math.abs(wp.x - c4.x) < size4.w/2 && Math.abs(wp.y - c4.y) < size4.h/2) {
                  main.selectedCompId = c4.id
                  main.selectedWireId = ""
                  main.refreshSel()
      
                  if (editable && main.mode === "control") {
                      if (c4.type === "input" || c4.type === "const") {
                          var base = c4.displayBase || "bin"
                          if (base === "bin") {
                              var bitIdx = cv.bitIndexAt(c4, wp.x)
                              var bits = c4.inputBits || []
                              main.circuitRef.setBitValue(c4.id, bitIdx, !(bits[bitIdx] || false))
                          }
                      } else if (c4.type === "clock") {
                          main.circuitRef.toggleClock(c4.id)
                      }
                  } else if (editable && main.mode === "edit") {
                      main.circuitRef.breakUndoMerge()
                      cv.draggingComp = true
                      cv.dragCompId = c4.id
                  }
                  return
              }
          }
      
          // 空白
          main.selectedCompId = ""
          main.selectedWireId = ""
          main.refreshSel()
          main.shortenWireCandidates = []
          cv.panning = true
      }
      
      function onPositionChanged(main, cv, mouse) {
          if (main.boxSelecting) {
              main.boxCurrentX = mouse.x; main.boxCurrentY = mouse.y
              return
          }
      
          if (main.draggingSelection) {
              var wp = cv.screenToWorld(mouse.x, mouse.y)
              var dwx = wp.x - main.lastDragWorldX
              var dwy = wp.y - main.lastDragWorldY
              if (Math.abs(dwx) > 0.001 || Math.abs(dwy) > 0.001) {
                  main.circuitRef.moveSelection(main.selectedIds, dwx, dwy)
                  main.lastDragWorldX = wp.x
                  main.lastDragWorldY = wp.y
              }
              return
          }
      
          if (main.wiring) {
              var sp = snapWorldPoint(main, cv, mouse)
              var sx = main.wiringStartX, sy = main.wiringStartY
              updateWiringDirection(main, sx, sy, sp.x, sp.y)
              var es = cv.worldToScreen(sp.x, sp.y)
              main.wireEndX = es.x
              main.wireEndY = es.y
              cv.requestPaint()
              return
          }
      
          if (cv.draggingComp && cv.dragCompId !== "" && !main.circuitViewOnly) {
              var wp3 = cv.screenToWorld(mouse.x, mouse.y)
              main.circuitRef.moveComponent(cv.dragCompId, wp3.x, wp3.y)
              return
          }
      
          if (cv.panning) {
              main.viewX += mouse.x - cv.lastMouseX
              main.viewY += mouse.y - cv.lastMouseY
              cv.lastMouseX = mouse.x; cv.lastMouseY = mouse.y
              cv.requestPaint()
          }
      }
      
      function onReleased(main, cv, mouse) {
          if (main.boxSelecting) {
              var x1 = Math.min(main.boxStartX, mouse.x)
              var y1 = Math.min(main.boxStartY, mouse.y)
              var x2 = Math.max(main.boxStartX, mouse.x)
              var y2 = Math.max(main.boxStartY, mouse.y)
              if (Math.abs(x2 - x1) < 5 && Math.abs(y2 - y1) < 5) {
                  main.selectedIds = []
              } else {
                  var ids = []
                  for (var i = 0; i < main.cachedComps.length; i++) {
                      var c = main.cachedComps[i]
                      var sp = cv.worldToScreen(c.x, c.y)
                      if (sp.x >= x1 && sp.x <= x2 && sp.y >= y1 && sp.y <= y2)
                          ids.push(c.id)
                  }
                  main.selectedIds = ids
                  main.statusText = "已选中 " + ids.length + " 个元件"
              }
              main.boxSelecting = false
              cv.requestPaint()
          }
      
          if (main.wiring) {
              var sp2 = snapWorldPoint(main, cv, mouse)
              var endX = sp2.x
              var endY = sp2.y
              var endPort = sp2.port
      
              // ★ 缩短优先检查：终点落在候选线的内部 → 缩短
              var cands = main.shortenWireCandidates || []
              var didShorten = false
              for (var ci = 0; ci < cands.length; ci++) {
                  var candId = cands[ci]
                  var sw = main.circuitRef.getWire(candId)
                  if (!sw || !sw.path || sw.path.length < 2) continue
                  var inside = false
                  for (var k = 0; k + 1 < sw.path.length; k++) {
                      if (pointOnSegInterior(endX, endY,
                                             sw.path[k].x, sw.path[k].y,
                                             sw.path[k+1].x, sw.path[k+1].y)) {
                          inside = true
                          break
                      }
                  }
                  if (inside) {
                      main.circuitRef.shortenWireEnd(candId, endX, endY)
                      main.statusText = "已缩短连线"
                      didShorten = true
                      break
                  }
              }
      
              if (!didShorten) {
                  var effType = resolveEffectiveWireType(main)
                  var startX = main.wiringStartX
                  var startY = main.wiringStartY
      
                  if (effType === "bezier") {
                      if (main.wiringFromComp === "") {
                          main.statusText = "⚠ 贝塞尔线需从端口开始"
                      } else if (!endPort) {
                          main.statusText = "⚠ 贝塞尔线需拖到端口"
                      } else {
                          var same = (endPort.compId === main.wiringFromComp
                                      && endPort.portIdx === main.wiringFromPort
                                      && endPort.isOutput === main.wiringStartIsOutput)
                          if (!same) {
                              var startIsOut = main.wiringStartIsOutput
                              var endIsOut = endPort.isOutput
                              if (startIsOut && !endIsOut) {
                                  main.circuitRef.addWire(main.wiringFromComp, main.wiringFromPort,
                                                          endPort.compId, endPort.portIdx)
                                  main.statusText = "已连线（贝塞尔）"
                              } else if (!startIsOut && endIsOut) {
                                  main.circuitRef.addWire(endPort.compId, endPort.portIdx,
                                                          main.wiringFromComp, main.wiringFromPort)
                                  main.statusText = "已连线（贝塞尔）"
                              } else {
                                  main.statusText = "⚠ 需连接 输出→输入"
                              }
                          }
                      }
                  } else {
                      // 正交：终点若无端口，尝试吸线
                      if (!endPort) {
                          var endWireId = main.circuitRef.wireAtPoint(endX, endY, 15 / Math.max(0.1, main.viewScale))
                          if (endWireId && endWireId !== "") {
                              var snapEnd = snapToWire(main, endWireId, endX, endY)
                              if (snapEnd) { endX = snapEnd.x; endY = snapEnd.y }
                          }
                      }
                      var hasDragged = (Math.abs(endX - startX) > 0.5 ||
                                        Math.abs(endY - startY) > 0.5)
                      if (hasDragged) {
                          var dir = main.wiringDirection
                          if (dir === 0) dir = 1
                          main.circuitRef.addOrthoWire(startX, startY, endX, endY, dir)
                          main.statusText = "已连线（正交）"
                      }
                  }
              }
      
              main.wiring = false
              main.wiringFromComp = ""
              main.wiringFromPort = -1
              main.wiringDirection = 0
              main.shortenWireCandidates = []
              cv.requestPaint()
          }
      
          cv.panning = false
          cv.draggingComp = false
          cv.dragCompId = ""
          main.draggingSelection = false
          main.circuitRef.breakUndoMerge()
      }
      
      function onCanceled(main, cv) {
          cv.panning = false
          cv.draggingComp = false
          cv.dragCompId = ""
          main.draggingSelection = false
          main.boxSelecting = false
          main.wiring = false
          main.wiringFromComp = ""
          main.wiringFromPort = -1
          main.wiringDirection = 0
          main.shortenWireCandidates = []
          main.circuitRef.breakUndoMerge()
          cv.requestPaint()
      }
      
      function onWheel(main, cv, wheel) {
          var oldScale = main.viewScale
          var factor = wheel.angleDelta.y > 0 ? 1.15 : 1/1.15
          var newScale = Math.max(0.15, Math.min(4.0, main.viewScale * factor))
          if (newScale === oldScale) return
          var mx = wheel.x, my = wheel.y
          main.viewX = mx - (mx - main.viewX) * (newScale / oldScale)
          main.viewY = my - (my - main.viewY) * (newScale / oldScale)
          main.viewScale = newScale
          main.triggerRepaint()
      }
      
      function onDoubleClicked(main, cv, mouse) {
          var wp = cv.screenToWorld(mouse.x, mouse.y)
          for (var i = main.cachedComps.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
              var c = main.cachedComps[i]
              var size = cv.compSize(c)
              if (Math.abs(wp.x - c.x) < size.w/2 && Math.abs(wp.y - c.y) < size.h/2) {
                  if (c.type === "sub") main.circuitRef.enterSubcircuit(c.subId)
                  return
              }
          }
      }

│         function compSize(comp) {
│             var t = comp.type
│             var bw = comp.bitWidth || 1
│             var rot = comp.rotation || 0
│             var ic = comp.inputCount || 2
│             var splits = comp.outputSplits ? comp.outputSplits.length : 0
│             var subIn = comp.subInputNames ? comp.subInputNames.length : 0
│             var subOut = comp.subOutputNames ? comp.subOutputNames.length : 0
│             var sig = t + "|" + bw + "|" + rot + "|" + ic + "|" + splits + "|" + subIn + "|" + subOut
│     
│             var cached = _sizeCache[comp.id]
│             if (cached !== undefined && cached._sig === sig) return cached
│     
│             var w0, h0
│             if (t === "text") { w0 = 140; h0 = 44 }
│             else if (t === "clock") { w0 = 60; h0 = 40 }
│             else if (t === "led") { w0 = Math.max(50, bw * 22 + 8); h0 = 40 }
│             else if (t === "input" || t === "output" || t === "const")
│                 { w0 = Math.max(60, bw * 22 + 8); h0 = 40 }
│             else if (t === "splitter" || t === "hub") {
│                 var oc = splits || bw
│                 w0 = 80; h0 = Math.max(60, oc * 24)
│             }
│             else if (t === "not") { w0 = 80; h0 = 60 }
│             else if (t === "tgate" || t === "ntran" || t === "ptran") { w0 = 80; h0 = 60 }
│             else if (t === "sub") {
│                 w0 = 110; h0 = Math.max(60, Math.max(subIn, subOut) * 24)
│             }
│             else { w0 = 80; h0 = Math.max(60, ic * 24) }
│     
│             var result
│             if (rot === 1 || rot === 3) result = { w: h0, h: w0, _sig: sig }
│             else                        result = { w: w0, h: h0, _sig: sig }
│             _sizeCache[comp.id] = result
│             return result
│         }

│         function clearSizeCache() { _sizeCache = ({}) }

│             return Geometry.worldToScreen(main.viewScale, main.viewX, main.viewY, wx, wy)

│             return Geometry.screenToWorld(main.viewScale, main.viewX, main.viewY, sx, sy)

│         function portPos(compId, portIdx, isOutput) {
│             var c = main.compById[compId]
│             if (!c) return { x: 0, y: 0 }
│             var ports = isOutput ? main.outPortsById[compId] : main.inPortsById[compId]
│             if (!ports || portIdx < 0 || portIdx >= ports.length) return { x: c.x, y: c.y }
│             var p = ports[portIdx]
│             return { x: c.x + p.x, y: c.y + p.y }
│         }
│         function bitIndexAt(comp, worldX) {
│             var size = compSize(comp)
│             var left = comp.x - size.w / 2 + 4
│             var i = Math.floor((worldX - left) / 22)
│             var bw = comp.bitWidth || 1
│             if (i < 0) i = 0
│             if (i >= bw) i = bw - 1
│             return i
│         }
